villa\\\\SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP\\\\css\\\\support.css\""}},{"name":"write_file","args":{"Content":"\"/**\\n * URBANDROPS â€” Shared Support Pages Vanilla JS\\n * Handles:\\n *  - Dynamic Cart Badge synced with localStorage ('cartCount')\\n *  - Shared Navbar Search bar behavior\\n *  - Store Locator filtering & dark map pin selection (pages/store-locator.html)\\n *  - FAQ Accordion [+] / [-] toggles & category filtering (pages/faq.html)\\n *  - Contact Support real-time email validation & ticket confirmation (pages/contact-support.html)\\n */\\n\\n(function () {\\n  'use strict';\\n\\n  /* ========================================================================\\n     1. SHARED CART BADGE (localStorage 'cartCount')\\n     ======================================================================== */\\n  function getCartCount() {\\n    try {\\n      const raw = localStorage.getItem('cartCount');\\n      const parsed = parseInt(raw, 10);\\n      return Number.isFinite(parsed) && parsed >= 0 ? parsed : 0;\\n    } catch (err) {\\n      return 0;\\n    }\\n  }\\n\\n  function renderCartBadge() {\\n    const badges = document.querySelectorAll('[data-cart-badge]');\\n    const count = getCartCount();\\n    badges.forEach(function (badge) {\\n      badge.textContent = String(count);\\n      badge.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Cart items: ' + count);\\n    });\\n  }\\n\\n  function initCartBadge() {\\n    renderCartBadge();\\n    window.addEventListener('storage', function (event) {\\n      if (event.key === 'cartCount') {\\n        renderCartBadge();\\n      }\\n    });\\n  }\\n\\n  /* ========================================================================\\n     2. SHARED NAVBAR SEARCH BAR\\n     ======================================================================== */\\n  function showToast(message) {\\n    const existing = document.getElementById('udSharedToast');\\n    if (existing) existing.remove();\\n\\n    const toast = document.createElement('div');\\n    toast.id = 'udSharedToast';\\n    toast.className = 'ud-toast';\\n    toast.setAttribute('role', 'status');\\n    toast.textContent = message;\\n    document.body.appendChild(toast);\\n\\n    window.setTimeout(functio\n<truncated 19912 bytes>","Overwrite":"true","Path":"\"c:\\\\Users\\\\laptop villa\\\\SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP\\\\js\\\\support.js\""}}],"truncated_fields":["tool_calls"]}

Cities`), dynamically filtered store cards displaying address, opening hours, phone, and `OPEN NOW` badge, paired with an interactive dark-styled map container card with synchronized pins and store details.\n- [pages/faq.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/faq.html) â€” FAQ Accordion organized into **Shipping**, **Returns**, and **Sizing** sections, with smooth CSS/JS expand-collapse animations, `[+]` / `[-]` toggle indicators, category filter pills, a flat-lay sizing measurement table, and navbar search filtering.\n- [pages/contact-support.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/contact-support.html) â€” Support ticket form with **Name**, **Email**, **Subject** dropdown, and **Message** fields, real-time JS email format validation (`input` and `blur`), character counter, and a confirmation alert displaying a generated `#UD-XXXXX` ticket ID upon valid submission.\n- [css/support.css](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/css/support.css) â€” Shared dark streetwear styles (`#0D0D0D` background, `#1A1A1A` cards, `#FFFFFF` text, `#FF4D00` CTAs, `#CCFF00` accents) and responsive layouts.\n- [js/support.js](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/js/support.js) â€” Shared vanilla JS handling the `localStorage` `'cartCount'` badge sync, navbar search bar, store filtering & map pin selection, FAQ `[+]`/`[-]` toggles, and contact form validation/submission.\n\n### Shared Navbar & Footer Details\n- All three pages include the shared URBANDROPS top navbar with `<img src=\"../images/Logo.png\">` (plus an `onerror` fallback monogram if `Logo.png` is not yet present), navigation links, search bar, dynamic {"step_index":36,"source":"USER_EXPLICIT","type":"USER_INPUT","status":"DONE","created_at":"2026-11-27T05:28:39Z","content":"<USER_REQUEST>\ntext\nBuild pages/womens-catalog.html, pages/cart-checkout.html, and pages/order-tracking.html for 'URBANDROPS'.\n\nTechnical & Visual Requirements:\n1. Style: Modern dark streetwear palette (#0D0D0D background, #1A1A1A card bg, #FFFFFF text, #FF4D00 CTAs, #CCFF00 accents). Use Bootstrap 5 and vanilla JavaScript only.\n2. Shared Header & Footer: Include shared URBANDROPS top navbar with Logo.png (<img src=\"../images/Logo.png\">), navigation links, search bar, and dynamic cart badge reading from localStorage ('cartCount').\n3. pages/womens-catalog.html (Women's Catalog): Grid of women's streetwear items rendered from a JS data array with search bar filter, category tags, and price sorting options (Low to High / High to Low).\n4. pages/cart-checkout.html (Cart & Checkout): Two-column layout. Left column lists cart items dynamically loaded from localStorage with quantity controls and remove buttons. Right column includes a promo code field ('URBAN10'), order summary breakdown (Subtotal, Shipping, Discount, Total), shipping address form, and 'PLACE ORDER' button.\n5. pages/order-tracking.html (Order Tracking): Order ID lookup input (#UD-1042) that displays a 4-step progress bar (Order Placed -> Processing -> Shipped -> Delivered) with status timestamp cards.\n6. Ensure all pages are 100% responsive using Bootstrap 5 container grid.\n\n</USER_REQUEST>\n<ADDITIONAL_METADATA>\nThe current local time is: 2026-11-27T10:28:39+05:00.\n</ADDITIONAL_METADATA>"}

{"step_index":58,"source":"MODEL","type":"GENERIC","status":"DONE","created_at":"2026-11-27T05:30:49Z","content":"Created At: 2026-11-27T10:30:49+05:00\nCompleted At: 2026-11-27T10:30:50+05:00\nCreated At: 2026-11-27T10:30:49+05:00\nCompleted At: 2026-11-27T10:30:50+05:00\nShowing lines 55-120 of 574\n55:     toast.setAttribute('role', 'status');\n56:     toast.textContent = message;\n57:     document.body.appendChild(toast);\n58: \n59:     window.setTimeout(function () {\n60:       if (toast.parentNode) toast.remove();\n61:     }, 3500);\n62:   }\n63: \n64:   function initNavbarSearch() {\n65:     const searchForm = document.getElementById('udNavSearchForm');\n66:     const searchInput = document.getElementById('udNavSearchInput');\n67:     if (!searchForm || !searchInput) return;\n68: \n69:     searchForm.addEventListener('submit', function (event) {\n70:       event.preventDefault();\n71:       const query = searchInput.value.trim();\n72:       if (!query) {\n73:         searchInput.focus();\n74:         showToast('Enter a keyword to search URBANDROPS.');\n75:         return;\n76:       }\n77: \n78:       // If on FAQ page, filter FAQ items directly\n79:       const faqItems = document.querySelectorAll('.ud-faq-item');\n80:       if (faqItems.length > 0) {\n81:         const lower = query.toLowerCase();\n82:         let matchCount = 0;\n83:         faqItems.forEach(function (item) {\n84:           const text = item.textContent.toLowerCase();\n85:           const matched = text.includes(lower);\n86:           item.style.display = matched ? '' : 'none';\n87:           if (matched) matchCount += 1;\n88:         });\n89: \n90:         // Reveal/hide section wrappers based on visible items\n91:         document.querySelectorAll('[data-faq-section]').forEach(function (section) {\n92:           const visibleInSection = Array.from(section.querySelectorAll('.ud-faq-item')).some(function (el) {\n93:             return el.style.display !== 'none';\n94:           });\n95:           section.style.display = visibleInSection ? '' : 'none';\n96:         });\n97: \n98:         const faqEmpty = document.getElementById('faqEmptyState');\n99:         if (faqEmpty) {\n100:           faqEmpty.classList.toggle('d-none', matchCount > 0);\n101:         }\n102:         showToast(matchCount + ' FAQ result(s) for \"' + query + '\".');\n103:         return;\n104:       }\n105: \n106:       // If on Store Locator page, match city or store name\n107:       const cityFilter = document.getElementById('cityFilter');\n108:       if (cityFilter) {\n109:         const lower = query.toLowerCase();\n110:         const cities = ['islamabad', 'lahore', 'karachi'];\n111:         const matchedCity = cities.find(function (c) {\n112:           return c.includes(lower) || lower.includes(c);\n113:         });\n114:         if (matchedCity) {\n115:           const properCity = matchedCity.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + matchedCity.slice(1);\n116:           cityFilter.value = properCity;\n117:           cityFilter.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));\n118:           showToast('Filtered stores for ' + properCity + '.');\n119:           return;\n120:         }"}

{"step_index":77,"source":"MODEL","type":"PLANNER_RESPONSE","status":"DONE","created_at":"2026-11-27T05:31:35Z","model":"gemini-4-hydrogen-high","input_tokens":2412,"cache_read_tokens":92098,"output_tokens":1017,"content":"Built the three new URBANDROPS e-commerce pages using Bootstrap 5, the shared dark streetwear palette (`#0D0D0D`, `#1A1A1A`, `#FFFFFF`, `#FF4D00`, `#CCFF00`), and vanilla JavaScript.\n\n### New & Updated Files\n- [pages/womens-catalog.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/womens-catalog.html) â€” Women's streetwear catalog rendered from the `WOMENS_CATALOG` JS data array, with keyword search filtering (synced with the navbar search input), category filter tags (`All`, `Hoodies & Sweats`, `Outerwear`, `Tees & Tops`, `Cargos & Bottoms`, `Accessories`), price sorting (`Low to High` / `High to Low`), per-card size chips, and `Add to Cart` actions updating `localStorage` (`urbandropsCart` and `cartCount`).\n- [pages/cart-checkout.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/cart-checkout.html) â€” Responsive two-column Cart & Checkout layout. The left column dynamically loads items from `localStorage` with `-`/`+` quantity controls, `Remove` buttons, and a sample-bag loader when empty. The right column features the `URBAN10` promo code field (10% discount), live `Subtotal` / `Shipping` / `Discount` / `Total` breakdown, shipping address form validation, and a `PLACE ORDER` button that records the order in `localStorage` and links straight to tracking.\n- [pages/order-tracking.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/order-tracking.html) â€” Order ID lookup (`#UD-1042` default plus `#UD-1019`, `#UD-1088`, and newly placed checkout orders) displaying a 4-step progress bar (`Order Placed` â†’ `Processing` â†’ `Shipped` â†’ `Delivered`) and status timestamp cards with location and courier notes.\n- [css/commerce.css](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/css/commerce.css) â€” Styling for product cards, size chips, cart rows, quantity controls, and the 4-step order tracking progress bar/timestamp cards (paired with [css/support.css](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/css/support.css)).\n- [js/commerce.js](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/js/commerce.js) â€” Vanilla JS powering the catalog rendering/filtering/sorting, cart persistence (`cartCount` + `urbandropsCart`), `URBAN10` promo math, checkout submission, and order tracking lookup.\n- Updated shared navbar & cart links across [pages/store-locator.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/store-locator.html), [pages/faq.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/faq.html), [pages/contact-support.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/contact-support.html), and [js/support.js](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/js/support.js) so every page includes `<img src=\"../images/Logo.png\">`, unified navigation (`Women's`, `Stores`, `Track Order`, `FAQ`, `Support`), search bar, `cart-checkout.html` cart button with `[data-cart-badge]`, and shared footer.\n\n### Verification\n- Verified with `Select-String` across all new and updated HTML, CSS, and JS files that Bootstrap 5 CDN includes, `../images/Logo.png`, `data-cart-badge` / `cartCount`, catalog filter/sort IDs, `URBAN10` promo & `PLACE ORDER` checkout IDs, `#UD-1042` 4-step tracking IDs, and cross-page navigation links are all present and aligned. (No headless browser or Node CLI runtime was executed in this environment.)"}

==========================================================================\n   URBANDROPS â€” Shared Streetwear Dark Theme & Support Pages Stylesheet\n   Palette:\n     Background: #0D0D0D\n     Card BG:    #1A1A1A\n     Text:       #FFFFFF\n     CTA:        #FF4D00\n     Accent:     #CCFF00\n   ========================================================================== */\n\n:root {\n  --ud-bg: #0D0D0D;\n  --ud-surface: #1A1A1A;\n  --ud-surface-elevated: #222222;\n  --ud-border: #2C2C2C;\n  --ud-border-hover: #404040;\n  --ud-text: #FFFFFF;\n  --ud-text-muted: #A3A3A3;\n  --ud-cta: #FF4D00;\n  --ud-cta-hover: #FF6A2B;\n  --ud-accent: #CCFF00;\n  --ud-danger: #FF3B30;\n  --ud-font-display: 'Space Grotesk', system-ui, -apple-system, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, sans-serif;\n  --ud-font-body: 'Inter', system-ui, -apple-system, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, sans-serif;\n  --ud-font-mono: 'JetBrains Mono', ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, Monaco, Consolas, monospace;\n}\n\n*,\n*::before,\n*::after {\n  box-sizing: border-box;\n}\n\nhtml {\n  scroll-behavior: smooth;\n}\n\nbody {\n  background-color: var(--ud-bg);\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-body);\n  min-height: 100vh;\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  overflow-x: hidden;\n  -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;\n}\n\nmain {\n  flex: 1 0 auto;\n}\n\nh1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6,\n.ud-display {\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-display);\n  letter-spacing: -0.02em;\n}\n\n.ud-mono {\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n}\n\n/* ---------- Selection & Scrollbar ---------- */\n::selection {\n  background-color: var(--ud-accent);\n  color: var(--ud-bg);\n}\n\n/* ---------- Top Announcement Strip ---------- */\n.ud-ticker {\n  background-color: var(--ud-accent);\n  color: var(--ud-bg);\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.75rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  letter-spacing: 0.08em;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  padding: 0.4rem 0;\n}\n\n/* ---------- Shared Navbar ---------- */\n.ud-navbar {\n  background-color: rgba(13, 13, 13, 0.94);\n  backdrop-filter: blur(12px);\n  -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(12px);\n  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  padding-top: 0.85rem;\n  padding-bottom: 0.85rem;\n}\n\n.ud-brand {\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 0.65rem;\n  text-decoration: none;\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n}\n\n.ud-brand-logo {\n  height: 36px;\n  width: auto;\n  max-width: 140px;\n  object-fit: contain;\n  display: block;\n}\n\n.ud-brand-fallback {\n  display: none;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: center;\n  width: 34px;\n  height: 34px;\n  background-color: var(--ud-cta);\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-display);\n  font-weight: 800;\n  font-size: 0.95rem;\n  letter-spacing: 0.04em;\n  border-radius: 4px;\n}\n\n.ud-brand-logo.is-missing + .ud-brand-fallback {\n  display: inline-flex;\n}\n\n.ud-brand-text {\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-display);\n  font-weight: 800;\n  font-size: 1.2rem;\n  letter-spacing: 0.08em;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n}\n\n.ud-navbar .nav-link {\n  color: var(--ud-text-muted);\n  font-size: 0.875rem;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.06em;\n  padding: 0.5rem 0.85rem;\n  transition: color 0.2s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-navbar .nav-link:hover,\n.ud-navbar .nav-link:focus-visible {\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n}\n\n.ud-navbar .nav-link.active {\n  color: var(--ud-accent);\n}\n\n.ud-navbar .navbar-toggler {\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n  padding: 0.45rem 0.65rem;\n}\n\n.ud-navbar .navbar-toggler:focus {\n  box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px rgba(204, 255, 0, 0.35);\n}\n\n.ud-navbar-toggler-icon {\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: center;\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n}\n\n/* Search Bar */\n.ud-search-form {\n  position: relative;\n  min-width: 210px;\n}\n\n.ud-search-input {\n  background-color: var(--ud-surface);\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n  font-size: 0.875rem;\n  padding: 0.5rem 2.6rem 0.5rem 0.9rem;\n  border-radius: 6px;\n  transition: border-color 0.2s ease, box-shadow 0.2s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-search-input::placeholder {\n  color: var(--ud-text-muted);\n}\n\n.ud-search-input:focus {\n  background-color: var(--ud-surface);\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n  border-color: var(--ud-accent);\n  box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px rgba(204, 255, 0, 0.18);\n  outline: none;\n}\n\n.ud-search-btn {\n  position: absolute;\n  right: 4px;\n  top: 50%;\n  transform: translateY(-50%);\n  background: transparent;\n  border: none;\n  color: var(--ud-text-muted);\n  padding: 0.35rem 0.5rem;\n  border-radius: 4px;\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: center;\n  transition: color 0.2s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-search-btn:hover {\n  color: var(--ud-accent);\n}\n\n/* Cart var(--ud-border);\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.8rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  padding: 0.5rem 0.85rem;\n  border-radius: 6px;\n  text-decoration: none;\n  transition: border-color 0.2s ease, transform 0.15s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-cart-btn:hover {\n  border-color: var(--ud-cta);\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n}\n\n.ud-cart-badge {\n  background-color: var(--ud-accent);\n  color: var(--ud-bg);\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.72rem;\n  font-weight: 800;\n  min-width: 22px;\n  height: 22px;\n  padding: 0 6px;\n  border-radius: 999px;\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: center;\n}\n\n/* ---------- Page Hero Header ---------- */\n.ud-page-hero {\n  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  background:\n    radial-gradient(circle at 88% 18%, rgba(255, 77, 0, 0.14), transparent 45%),\n    radial-gradient(circle at 12% 85%, rgba(204, 255, 0, 0.08), transparent 40%),\n    var(--ud-bg);\n  padding: 3.25rem 0 2.75rem;\n}\n\n.ud-eyebrow {\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 0.5rem;\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.75rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.12em;\n  color: var(--ud-accent);\n  margin-bottom: 0.75rem;\n}\n\n.ud-eyebrow::before {\n  content: \"\";\n  display: inline-block;\n  width: 8px;\n  height: 8px;\n  background-color: var(--ud-accent);\n  border-radius: 2px;\n}\n\n.ud-hero-title {\n  font-size: clamp(2rem, 4vw, 3.25rem);\n  font-weight: 800;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  line-height: 1.05;\n  margin-bottom: 0.85rem;\n}\n\n.ud-hero-subtitle {\n  color: var(--ud-text-muted);\n  font-size: 1.02rem;\n  max-width: 640px;\n  margin-bottom: 0;\n}\n\n/* ---------- Cards & Controls ---------- */\n.ud-card {\n  background-color: var(--ud-surface);\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  border-radius: 10px;\n  transition: border-color border-color: var(--ud-accent);\n  box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px rgba(204, 255, 0, 0.35);\n}\n\n.ud-btn-cta {\n  background-color: var(--ud-cta);\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-cta);\n  color: border-radius: 6px;\n  transition: background-color 0.2s ease, border-color 0.2s ease, transform 0.15s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-btn-cta:hover,\n.ud-btn-cta:focus-visible {\n  background-color: letter-spacing: 0.05em;\n  padding: 0.55rem 1rem;\n  border-radius: 6px;\n  transition: border-color 0.2s ease, color 0.2s ease, background-color 0.2s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-btn-outline:hover,\n.ud-btn-outline.active {\n  border-color: var(--ud-accent);\n  color: var(--ud-accent);\n  background-color: rgba(204, 255, 0, 0.06);\n}\n\n.ud-form-label {\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.78rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.08em;\n  color: var(--ud-text-muted);\n  margin-bottom: var(--ud-accent);\n}\n\n.ud-form-control.is-invalid-ud,\n.ud-form-select.is-invalid-ud {\n  border-color: var(--ud-cta);\n}\n\n.ud-validation-msg {\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.76rem;\n  margin-top: 0.4rem;\n  min-height: 1.1rem;\n}\n\n.ud-validation-msg.is-valid {\n  color: var(--ud-accent);\n}\n\n.ud-validation-msg.is-invalid {\n  color: var(--ud-cta);\n}\n\n/* ---------- Store Locator Specific ---------- */\n.ud-status-pill {\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 0.4rem;\n  background-color: rgba(204, 255, 0, 0.12);\n  border: 1px solid rgba(204, 255, 0, 0.4);\n  color: var(--ud-accent);\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.72rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  letter-spacing: 0.08em;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  padding: 0.25rem 0.6rem;\n  border-radius: 999px;\n}\n\n.ud-status-dot {\n  width: 7px;\n  height: 7px;\n  border-radius: 50%;\n  background-color: var(--ud-accent);\n  box-shadow: 0 0 8px var(--ud-accent);\n}\n\n.ud-city-badge {\n  display: inline-block;\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.72rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.08em;\n  color: var(--ud-text-muted);\n  background-color: var(--ud-bg);\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  padding: 0.22rem 0.55rem;\n  border-radius: 4px;\n}\n\n.ud-map-card {\n  position: sticky;\n  top: 5.5rem;\n  overflow: hidden;\n}\n\n.ud-map-canvas {\n  position: relative;\n  height: 360px;\n  background-color: #101010;\n  background-image:\n    linear-gradient(to right, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04) 1px, transparent 1px),\n    linear-gradient(to bottom, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04) 1px, transparent 1px);\n  background-size: 28px 28px;\n  border-top: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  overflow: hidden;\n}\n\n.ud-map-radar {\n  position: absolute;\n  inset: 0;\n  background: radial-gradient(circle at 50% 50%, rgba(204, 255, 0, 0.08), var(--ud-text-muted);\n  letter-spacing: 0.08em;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  background: rgba(13, 13, 13, 0.85);\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  padding: 0.25rem 0.55rem;\n  border-radius: 4px;\n  z-index: 2;\n}\n\n.ud-map-pin {\n  position: absolute;\n  transform: translate(-50%, -50%);\n  background-color: var(--ud-surface);\n  border: 2px solid var(--ud-cta);\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.72rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  padding: 0.28rem 0.6rem;\n  border-radius: 999px;\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 0.35rem;\n  cursor: pointer;\n  z-index: 3;\n  transition: transform 0.2s ease, background-color 0.2s ease, border-color 0.2s ease, color 0.2s ease;\n  box-shadow: 0 6px 16px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.55);\n}\n\n.ud-map-pin:hover,\n.ud-map-pin.is-active {\n  background-color: var(--ud-accent);\n  border-color: var(--ud-accent);\n  color: var(--ud-bg);\n  transform: translate(-50%, -50%) scale(1.08);\n  z-index: 4;\n}\n\n.ud-map-pin-dot {\n  width: 7px;\n  height: 7px;\n  border-radius: 50%;\n  background-color: currentColor;\n}\n\n/* ---------- FAQ Accordion Specific ---------- */\n.ud-faq-section-header {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  padding-bottom: 0.75rem;\n  margin-bottom: 1rem;\n}\n\n.ud-faq-item {\n  background-color: var(--ud-surface);\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  border-radius: 8px;\n  margin-bottom: 0.75rem;\n  overflow: hidden;\n  transition: border-color 0.2s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-faq-item.is-open {\n  border-color: var(--ud-accent);\n}\n\n.ud-faq-trigger {\n  width: 100%;\n  background: transparent;\n  border: none;\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n  padding: 1.15rem 1.25rem;\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  gap: 1rem;\n  text-align: left;\n  cursor: pointer;\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-display);\n  font-size: 1.02rem;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  transition: color 0.2s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-faq-trigger:hover {\n  color: var(--ud-accent);\n}\n\n.ud-faq-toggle-icon {\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.95rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  color: var(--ud-accent);\n  flex-shrink: 0;\n  transition: transform 0.2s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-faq-panel {\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-rows: 0fr;\n  transition: grid-template-rows 0.28s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-faq-item.is-open .ud-faq-panel {\n  grid-template-rows: 1fr;\n}\n\n.ud-faq-panel-inner {\n  overflow: hidden;\n}\n\n.ud-faq-answer {\n  padding: 0 1.25rem 1.25rem;\n  color: var(--ud-text-muted);\n  font-size: 0.95rem;\n  line-height: 1.65;\n  border-top: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);\n  padding-top: 0.95rem;\n}\n\n/* ---------- Confirmation Alert ---------- */\n.ud-alert-success {\n  background-color: rgba(204, 255, 0, 0.1);\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-accent);\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n  border-radius: 8px;\n  padding: 1.15rem 1.25rem;\n}\n\n.ud-alert-success .ud-alert-title {\n  color: var(--ud-accent);\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-display);\n  font-weight: 700;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.04em;\n}\n\n/* ---------- Shared Footer ---------- */\n.ud-footer {\n  background-color: #080808;\n  border-top: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  padding: 3.25rem 0 2rem;\n  margin-top: 4rem;\n}\n\n.ud-footer-heading {\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.78rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.1em;\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n  margin-bottom: 1rem;\n}\n\n.ud-footer-link {\n  display: inline-block;\n  color: var(--ud-text-muted);\n  text-decoration: none;\n  font-size: 0.9rem;\n  margin-bottom: 0.5rem;\n  transition: color 0.2s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-footer-link:hover {\n  color: var(--ud-accent);\n}\n\n.ud-footer-bottom {\n  border-top: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  margin-top: 2.25rem;\n  padding-top: 1.25rem;\n  font-size: 0.82rem;\n  color: var(--ud-text-muted);\n}\n\n/* ---------- Toast Notification ---------- */\n.ud-toast {\n  position: fixed;\n  bottom: 1.25rem;\n  right: 1.25rem;\n  z-index: 1080;\n  background-color: var(--ud-surface);\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-accent);\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n  padding: 0.8rem 1.1rem;\n  border-radius: 8px;\n  font-size: 0.875rem;\n  box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.65);\n  max-width: 340px;\n}\n\n@media (max-width: 991.98px) {\n  .ud-map-card {\n    position: static;\n  }\n\n  .ud-search-form {\n    width: 100%;\n    margin-top: 0.75rem;\n    margin-bottom: 0.75rem;\n  }\n}\n","Overwrite":true,"Path":"c:\\Users\\laptop villa\\SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP\\css\\support.css"}},{"name":"write_file","args":{"Content":"/**\n * URBANDROPS â€” Shared Support Pages Vanilla JS\n * Handles:\n *  - Dynamic Cart Badge synced with localStorage ('cartCount')\n *  - Shared Navbar Search bar behavior\n *  - Store Locator filtering & dark map pin selection (pages/store-locator.html)\n *  - FAQ Accordion [+] / [-] toggles & category filtering (pages/faq.html)\n *  - Contact Support real-time email validation & ticket confirmation (pages/contact-support.html)\n */\n\n(function () {\n  'use strict';\n\n  /* ========================================================================\n     1. SHARED CART BADGE (localStorage 'cartCount')\n     ======================================================================== */\n  function getCartCount() {\n    try {\n      const raw = localStorage.getItem('cartCount');\n      const parsed = parseInt(raw, 10);\n      return Number.isFinite(parsed) && parsed >= 0 ? parsed : 0;\n    } catch (err) {\n      return 0;\n    }\n  }\n\n  function renderCartBadge() {\n    const badges = document.querySelectorAll('[data-cart-badge]');\n    const count = getCartCount();\n    badges.forEach(function (badge) {\n      badge.textContent = String(count);\n      badge.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Cart items: ' + count);\n    });\n  }\n\n  function initCartBadge() {\n    renderCartBadge();\n    window.addEventListener('storage', function (event) {\n      if (event.key === 'cartCount') {\n        renderCartBadge();\n      }\n    });\n  }\n\n  /* ========================================================================\n     2. SHARED NAVBAR SEARCH BAR\n     ======================================================================== */\n  function showToast(message) {\n    const existing = document.getElementById('udSharedToast');\n    if (existing) existing.remove();\n\n    const toast = document.createElement('div');\n    toast.id = 'udSharedToast';\n    toast.className = 'ud-toast';\n    toast.setAttribute('role', 'status');\n    toast.textContent = message;\n    document.body.appendChild(toast);\n\n    window.setTimeout(function () {\n      if (toast.parentNode) toast.remove();\n    }, 3500);\n  }\n\n  function initNavbarSearch() {\n    const searchForm = document.getElementById('udNavSearchForm');\n    const searchInput = document.getElementById('udNavSearchInput');\n    if (!searchForm || !searchInput) return;\n\n    searchForm.addEventListener('submit', function (event) {\n      event.preventDefault();\n      const query = searchInput.value.trim();\n      if (!query) {\n        searchInput.focus();\n        showToast('Enter a keyword to search URBANDROPS.');\n        return;\n      }\n\n      // If on FAQ page, filter FAQ items directly\n      const faqItems = document.querySelectorAll('.ud-faq-item');\n      if (faqItems.length > 0) {\n        const lower = query.toLowerCase();\n        let matchCount = 0;\n        faqItems.forEach(function (item) {\n          const text = item.textContent.toLowerCase();\n          const matched = text.includes(lower);\n          item.style.display = matched ? '' : 'none';\n          if (matched) matchCount += 1;\n        });\n\n        // Reveal/hide section wrappers based on visible items\n        document.querySelectorAll('[data-faq-section]').forEach(function (section) {\n          const visibleInSection = Array.from(section.querySelectorAll('.ud-faq-item')).some(function (el) {\n            return el.style.display !== 'none';\n          });\n          section.style.display = visibleInSection ? '' : 'none';\n        });\n\n        const faqEmpty = document.getElementById('faqEmptyState');\n        if (faqEmpty) {\n          faqEmpty.classList.toggle('d-none', matchCount > 0);\n        }\n        showToast(matchCount + ' FAQ result(s) for \"' + query + '\".');\n        return;\n      }\n\n      // If on Store Locator page, match city or store name\n      const cityFilter = document.getElementById('cityFilter');\n      if (cityFilter) {\n        const lower = query.toLowerCase();\n        const cities = ['islamabad', 'lahore', 'karachi'];\n        const matchedCity = cities.find(function (c) {\n          return c.includes(lower) || lower.includes(c);\n        });\n        if (matchedCity) {\n          const properCity = matchedCity.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + matchedCity.slice(1);\n          cityFilter.value = properCity;\n          cityFilter.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));\n          showToast('Filtered stores for ' + properCity + '.');\n          return;\n        }\n      }\n\n      showToast('Searching catalog for \"' + query + '\"...');\n    });\n  }\n\n  /* ========================================================================\n     3. STORE LOCATOR PAGE (pages/store-locator.html)\n     ======================================================================== */\n  const STORES = [\n    {\n      id: 'isb-f7',\n      city: 'Islamabad',\n      name: 'URBANDROPS // F-7 JINNAH SUPER',\n      badge: 'Flagship Store',\n      address: 'Shop 14, Block 13-L, Jinnah Super Market, F-7 Markaz, Islamabad',\n      hours: 'Mon â€“ Sun: 11:00 AM â€“ 10:30 PM',\n      phone: '+92 51 2654109',\n      status: 'OPEN NOW',\n      coords: '33.7215Â° N, 73.0561Â° E',\n      pinX: 58,\n      pinY: 22\n    },\n    {\n      id: 'isb-centaurus',\n      city: 'Islamabad',\n      name: 'URBANDROPS // CENTAURUS DROP LAB',\n      badge: 'Concept Studio',\n      address: 'Level 2, Unit 208, The Centaurus Mall, Jinnah Ave, F-8/4, Islamabad',\n      hours: 'Mon â€“ Sun: 11:00 AM â€“ 11:00 PM',\n      phone: '+92 51 2701822',\n      status: 'OPEN NOW',\n      coords: '33.7077Â° N, 73.0501Â° E',\n      pinX: 66,\n      pinY: 30\n    },\n    {\n      id: 'lhr-gulberg',\n      city: 'Lahore',\n      name: 'URBANDROPS // MM ALAM GULBERG',\n      badge: 'Flagship Store',\n      address: '42-B, MM Alam Road, Block C-2, Gulberg III, Lahore',\n      hours: 'Mon â€“ Sun: 12:00 PM â€“ 11:00 PM',\n      phone: '+92 42 35784910',\n      status: 'OPEN NOW',\n      coords: '31.5122Â° N, 74.3514Â° E',\n      pinX: 72,\n      pinY: 48\n    },\n    {\n      id: 'lhr-dha',\n      city: 'Lahore',\n      name: 'URBANDROPS // DHA PHASE 5',\n      badge: 'Archive & Drops',\n      address: 'Plaza 18, Sector CCA, DHA Phase 5, Lahore',\n      hours: 'Mon â€“ Sun: 12:00 PM â€“ 10:30 PM',\n      phone: '+92 42 37182204',\n      status: 'OPEN NOW',\n      coords: '31.4628Â° N, 74.4076Â° E',\n      pinX: 80,\n      pinY: 56\n    },\n    {\n      id: 'khi-clifton',\n      city: 'Karachi',\n      name: 'URBANDROPS // CLIFTON BLOCK 4',\n      badge: 'Flagship Store',\n      address: 'Plot BC-9, Marine Promenade, Block 4 Clifton, Karachi',\n      hours: 'Mon â€“ Sun: 12:00 PM â€“ 11:30 PM',\n      phone: '+92 21 35871140',\n      status: 'OPEN NOW',\n      coords: '24.8138Â° N, 67.0299Â° E',\n      pinX: 25,\n      pinY: 78\n    },\n    {\n      id: 'khi-dha',\n      city: 'Karachi',\n      name: 'URBANDROPS // DHA BUKHARI COMMERCIAL',\n      badge: 'Streetwear Vault',\n      address: '17-C, Lane 5, Bukhari Commercial, DHA Phase 6, Karachi',\n      hours: 'Tue â€“ Sun: 1:00 PM â€“ 11:00 PM',\n      phone: '+92 21 35249081',\n      status: 'OPEN NOW',\n      coords: '24.7937Â° N, 67.0645Â° E',\n      pinX: 34,\n      pinY: 84\n    }\n  ];\n\n  function initStoreLocator() {\n    const cityFilter = document.getElementById('cityFilter');\n    const storeCardsContainer = document.getElementById('storeCardsContainer');\n    const mapPinsContainer = document.getElementById('mapPinsContainer');\n    const storeCountLabel = document.getElementById('storeCountLabel');\n    const resetCityBtn = document.getElementById('resetCityFilter');\n\n    if (!cityFilter || !storeCardsContainer || !mapPinsContainer) return;\n\n    let selectedStoreId = STORES[0].id;\n\n    function updateMapOverlay(store) {\n      const titleEl = document.getElementById('mapActiveName');\n      const cityEl = document.getElementById('mapActiveCity');\n      const addressEl = document.getElementById('mapActiveAddress');\n      const hoursEl = document.getElementById('mapActiveHours');\n      const coordsEl = document.getElementById('mapActiveCoords');\n      const directionsEl = document.getElementById('mapDirectionsBtn');\n\n      if (!store) return;\n      if (titleEl) titleEl.textContent = store.name;\n      if (cityEl) cityEl.textContent = store.city + ' â€¢ ' + store.badge;\n      if (addressEl) addressEl.textContent = store.address;\n      if (hoursEl) hoursEl.textContent = store.hours;\n      if (coordsEl) coordsEl.textContent = store.coords;\n      if (directionsEl) {\n        const query = encodeURIComponent(store.address);\n        directionsEl.href = 'https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=' + query;\n      }\n    }\n\n    function selectStore(storeId) {\n      selectedStoreId = storeId;\n      const store = STORES.find(function (s) {\n        return s.id === storeId;\n      });\n      updateMapOverlay(store);\n\n      storeCardsContainer.querySelectorAll('[data-store-id]').forEach(function (card) {\n        card.classList.toggle('is-selected', card.getAttribute('data-store-id') === storeId);\n      });\n\n      mapPinsContainer.querySelectorAll('[data-pin-id]').forEach(function (pin) {\n        pin.classList.toggle('is-active', pin.getAttribute('data-pin-id') === storeId);\n      });\n    }\n\n    function renderStores() {\n      const selectedCity = cityFilter.value;\n      const filtered = selectedCity === 'all'\n        ? STORES\n        : STORES.filter(function (s) {\n            return s.city === selectedCity;\n          });\n\n      if (storeCountLabel) {\n        storeCountLabel.textContent = 'Showing ' + filtered.length + ' ' + (filtered.length === 1 ? 'Location' : 'Locations');\n      }\n\n      if (resetCityBtn) {\n        resetCityBtn.classList.toggle('d-none', selectedCity === 'all');\n      }\n\n      if (!filtered.some(function (s) { return s.id === selectedStoreId; })) {\n        selectedStoreId = filtered.length > 0 ? filtered[0].id : null;\n      }\n\n      // Render Store Cards\n      storeCardsContainer.innerHTML = '';\n      filtered.forEach(function (store) {\n        const col = document.createElement('div');\n        col.className = 'col-12 col-md-6 col-lg-12';\n\n        const isSelected = store.id === selectedStoreId;\n        col.innerHTML =\n          '<article class=\"ud-card ud-card-interactive p-4 h-100 ' + (isSelected ? 'is-selected' : '') + '\" data-store-id=\"' + store.id + '\">' +\n            '<div class=\"d-flex flex-wrap justify-content-between align-items-start gap-2 mb-3\">' +\n              '<div>' +\n                '<span class=\"ud-city-badge me-2\">' + store.city + '</span>' +\n                '<span class=\"ud-mono small text-secondary\">' + store.badge + '</span>' +\n              '</div>' +\n              '<span class=\"ud-status-pill\">' +\n                '<span class=\"ud-status-dot\" aria-hidden=\"true\"></span>' +\n                store.status +\n              '</span>' +\n            '</div>' +\n            '<h2 class=\"h5 fw-bold mb-2\">' + store.name + '</h2>' +\n            '<p class=\"text-secondary small mb-3\">' + store.address + '</p>' +\n            '<div class=\"d-flex flex-column gap-1 mb-3 small\">' +\n              '<div><span class=\"ud-mono text-secondary\">HOURS:</span> <span class=\"text-white\">' + store.hours + '</span></div>' +\n              '<div><span class=\"ud-mono text-secondary\">PHONE:</span> <span class=\"text-white\">' + store.phone + '</span></div>' +\n            '</div>' +\n            '<div class=\"d-flex flex-wrap gap-2\">' +\n              '<button type=\"button\" class=\"btn ud-btn-cta btn-sm\" data-focus-store=\"' + store.id + '\">Focus on Map</button>' +\n              '<a href=\"contact-support.html\" class=\"btn ud-btn-outline btn-sm\">Store Inquiry</a>' +\n            '</div>' +\n          '</article>';\n\n        storeCardsContainer.appendChild(col);\n      });\n\n      // Render Map Pins\n      mapPinsContainer.innerHTML = '';\n      filtered.forEach(function (store) {\n        const pin = document.createElement('button');\n        pin.type = 'button';\n        pin.className = 'ud-map-pin' + (store.id === selectedStoreId ? ' is-active' : '');\n        pin.style.left = store.pinX + '%';\n        pin.style.top = store.pinY + '%';\n        pin.setAttribute('data-pin-id', store.id);\n        pin.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Select ' + store.name + ' in ' + store.city);\n        pin.innerHTML = '<span class=\"ud-map-pin-dot\" aria-hidden=\"true\"></span><span>' + store.city.slice(0, 3).toUpperCase() + '</span>';\n\n        pin.addEventListener('click', function () {\n          selectStore(store.id);\n        });\n        mapPinsContainer.appendChild(pin);\n      });\n\n      // Bind focus buttons on cards\n      storeCardsContainer.querySelectorAll('[data-focus-store]').forEach(function (btn) {\n        btn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n          selectStore(btn.getAttribute('data-focus-store'));\n        });\n      });\n\n      const activeStore = filtered.find(function (s) { return s.id === selectedStoreId; });\n      updateMapOverlay(activeStore);\n    }\n\n    cityFilter.addEventListener('change', renderStores);\n\n    if (resetCityBtn) {\n      resetCityBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n        cityFilter.value = 'all';\n        renderStores();\n      });\n    }\n\n    renderStores();\n  }\n\n  /* ========================================================================\n     4. FAQ ACCORDION PAGE (pages/faq.html)\n     ======================================================================== */\n  function initFaqAccordion() {\n    const triggers = document.querySelectorAll('.ud-faq-trigger');\n    if (triggers.length === 0) return;\n\n    function setFaqState(item, open) {\n      const trigger = item.querySelector('.ud-faq-trigger');\n      const icon = item.querySelector('.ud-faq-toggle-icon');\n      item.classList.toggle('is-open', open);\n      if (trigger) {\n        trigger.setAttribute('aria-expanded', open ? 'true' : 'false');\n      }\n      if (icon) {\n        icon.textContent = open ? '[-]' : '[+]';\n      }\n    }\n\n    triggers.forEach(function (trigger) {\n      trigger.addEventListener('click', function () {\n        const item = trigger.closest('.ud-faq-item');\n        if (!item) return;\n        const currentlyOpen = item.classList.contains('is-open');\n        setFaqState(item, !currentlyOpen);\n      });\n    });\n\n    // Category filter buttons (All / Shipping / Returns / Sizing)\n    const categoryButtons = document.querySelectorAll('[data-faq-filter]');\n    const sections = document.querySelectorAll('[data-faq-section]');\n    const resetFaqSearchBtn = document.getElementById('resetFaqSearchBtn');\n\n    categoryButtons.forEach(function (btn) {\n      btn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n        const target = btn.getAttribute('data-faq-filter');\n\n        categoryButtons.forEach(function (b) {\n          b.classList.toggle('active', b === btn);\n        });\n\n        // Clear any search-filtered hidden items first\n        document.querySelectorAll('.ud-faq-item').forEach(function (item) {\n          item.style.display = '';\n        });\n\n        sections.forEach(function (section) {\n          const sectionKey = section.getAttribute('data-faq-section');\n          section.style.display = (target === 'all' || target === sectionKey) ? '' : 'none';\n        });\n\n        const faqEmpty = document.getElementById('faqEmptyState');\n        if (faqEmpty) faqEmpty.classList.add('d-none');\n      });\n    });\n\n    if (resetFaqSearchBtn) {\n      resetFaqSearchBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n        const searchInput = document.getElementById('udNavSearchInput');\n        if (searchInput) searchInput.value = '';\n        document.querySelectorAll('.ud-faq-item').forEach(function (item) {\n          item.style.display = '';\n        });\n        sections.forEach(function (section) {\n          section.style.display = '';\n        });\n        const faqEmpty = document.getElementById('faqEmptyState');\n        if (faqEmpty) faqEmpty.classList.add('d-none');\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n  /* ========================================================================\n     5. CONTACT SUPPORT PAGE (pages/contact-support.html)\n     ======================================================================== */\n  function initContactSupport() {\n    const form = document.getElementById('supportTicketForm');\n    if (!form) return;\n\n    const nameInput = document.getElementById('supportName');\n    const emailInput = document.getElementById('supportEmail');\n    const subjectSelect = document.getElementById('supportSubject');\n    const messageInput = document.getElementById('supportMessage');\n\n    const nameFeedback = document.getElementById('nameFeedback');\n    const emailFeedback = document.getElementById('emailFeedback');\n    const subjectFeedback = document.getElementById('subjectFeedback');\n    const messageFeedback = document.getElementById('messageFeedback');\n\n    const alertContainer = document.getElementById('supportConfirmationAlert');\n    const ticketIdSpan = document.getElementById('confirmedTicketId');\n    const ticketSummarySpan = document.getElementById('confirmedTicketSummary');\n    const charCounter = document.getElementById('messageCharCount');\n\n    const EMAIL_REGEX = /^[^\\s@]+@[^\\s@]+\\.[^\\s@]{2,}$/;\n\n    function setFieldFeedback(input, feedbackEl, isValid, message) {\n      input.classList.remove('is-valid-ud', 'is-invalid-ud');\n      feedbackEl.classList.remove('is-valid', 'is-invalid');\n\n      if (message === '') {\n        feedbackEl.textContent = '';\n        return;\n      }\n\n      if (isValid) {\n        input.classList.add('is-valid-ud');\n        feedbackEl.classList.add('is-valid');\n      } else {\n        input.classList.add('is-invalid-ud');\n        feedbackEl.classList.add('is-invalid');\n      }\n      feedbackEl.textContent = message;\n    }\n\n    function validateEmailRealtime() {\n      const val = emailInput.value.trim();\n      if (val.length === 0) {\n        setFieldFeedback(emailInput, emailFeedback, false, 'Email address is required.');\n        return false;\n      }\n      if (!EMAIL_REGEX.test(val)) {\n        setFieldFeedback(emailInput, emailFeedback, false, 'Enter a valid email format (e.g., alex@domain.com).');\n        return false;\n      }\n      setFieldFeedback(emailInput, emailFeedback, true, 'Valid email format.');\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    function validateName() {\n      const val = nameInput.value.trim();\n      if (val.length < 2) {\n        setFieldFeedback(nameInput, nameFeedback, false, 'Please enter your full name (at least 2 characters).');\n        return false;\n      }\n      setFieldFeedback(nameInput, nameFeedback, true, '');\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    function validateSubject() {\n      if (!subjectSelect.value) {\n        setFieldFeedback(subjectSelect, subjectFeedback, false, 'Please choose a support subject.');\n        return false;\n      }\n      setFieldFeedback(subjectSelect, subjectFeedback, true, '');\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    function validateMessage() {\n      const val = messageInput.value.trim();\n      if (val.length < 15) {\n        setFieldFeedback(messageInput, messageFeedback, false, 'Message must be at least 15 characters.');\n        return false;\n      }\n      setFieldFeedback(messageInput, messageFeedback, true, '');\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    // Real-time email validation as the user types\n    emailInput.addEventListener('input', validateEmailRealtime);\n    emailInput.addEventListener('blur', validateEmailRealtime);\n\n    nameInput.addEventListener('blur', validateName);\n    subjectSelect.addEventListener('change', validateSubject);\n    messageInput.addEventListener('input', function () {\n      if (charCounter) {\n        charCounter.textContent = messageInput.value.length + ' / 1000';\n      }\n      if (messageInput.classList.contains('is-invalid-ud')) {\n        validateMessage();\n      }\n    });\n\n    form.addEventListener('submit', function (event) {\n      event.preventDefault();\n\n      const isNameOk = validateName();\n      const isEmailOk = validateEmailRealtime();\n      const isSubjectOk = validateSubject();\n      const isMessageOk = validateMessage();\n\n      if (!isNameOk || !isEmailOk || !isSubjectOk || !isMessageOk) {\n        if (alertContainer) alertContainer.classList.add('d-none');\n        return;\n      }\n\n      const ticketCode = 'UD-' + Math.floor(10000 + Math.random() * 90000);\n      if (ticketIdSpan) ticketIdSpan.textContent = '#' + ticketCode;\n      if (ticketSummarySpan) {\n        ticketSummarySpan.textContent =\n          'Ticket logged for \"' + subjectSelect.value + '\". A confirmation has been routed to ' + emailInput.value.trim() + '.';\n      }\n\n      if (alertContainer) {\n        alertContainer.classList.remove('d-none');\n        alertContainer.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'nearest' });\n      }\n\n      form.reset();\n      if (charCounter) charCounter.textContent = '0 / 1000';\n\n      [nameInput, emailInput, subjectSelect, messageInput].forEach(function (el) {\n        el.classList.remove('is-valid-ud', 'is-invalid-ud');\n      });\n      [nameFeedback, emailFeedback, subjectFeedback, messageFeedback].forEach(function (el) {\n        el.textContent = '';\n        el.classList.remove('is-valid', 'is-invalid');\n      });\n    });\n  }\n\n  /* ========================================================================\n     BOOTSTRAP INIT\n     ======================================================================== */\n  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {\n    initCartBadge();\n    initNavbarSearch();\n    initStoreLocator();\n    initFaqAccordion();\n    initContactSupport();\n  });\n})();\n","Overwrite":true,"Path":"c:\\Users\\laptop villa\\SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP\\js\\support.js"}}]}

</ul>\n\n        <div class=\"d-flex flex-column flex-lg-row align-items-stretch align-items-lg-center gap-2\">\n          <form id=\"udNavSearchForm\" class=\"ud-search-form\" role=\"search\">\n     </div>\n        <div class=\"col-lg-4 text-lg-end\">\n          <div class=\"ud-card d-inline-flex align-items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2\">\n            <span class=\"ud-status-pill\">\n              <span class=\"ud-status-dot\" aria-hidden=\"true\"></span>\n              6 DOORS ACTIVE\n            </span>\n            <span class=\"ud-mono small text-secondary\">PK // GMT+5</span>\n       <span id=\"storeCountLabel\" class=\"ud-mono small text-secondary\">Showing 6 Locations</span>\n            <button id=\"resetCityFilter\" type=\"button\" class=\"btn ud-btn-outline btn-sm <div class=\"d-flex flex-wrap justify-content-between align-items-start gap-2 mb-2\">\n                <div>\n                  <span id=\"mapActiveCity\" class=\"ud-mono small text-uppercase\" </div>\n                <span id=\"mapActiveCoords\" class=\"ud-city-badge\">33.7215Â° N, 73.0561Â° E</span>\n              </div>\n              <p id=\"mapActiveAddress\" class=\"text-secondary    <div class=\"container\">\n      <div class=\"row g-4\">\n        <div class=\"col-lg-4\">\n          <a class=\"ud-brand mb-3\" href=\"../index.html\">\n            <img src=\"../images/Logo.png\" DELIVERY</span>\n            </div>\n\n            <div class=\"ud-faq-item is-open\">\n              <button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-faq-trigger\" aria-expanded=\"true\" aria-controls=\"faq-ship-1\">\n                <span>How long does order processing and nationwide delivery take?</span>\n                <span class=\"ud-faq-toggle-icon\" aria-hidden=\"true\">[-]</span>\n              </button>\n              <div id=\"faq-ship-1\" class=\"ud-faq-panel\">\n                <div class=\"ud-faq-panel-inner\">\n                  <div class=\"ud-faq-answer\">\n                    All standard orders placed before 3:00 PM PKT are packed and dispatched the same business day. Delivery takes <strong>1â€“2 business days</strong> within Islamabad, Lahore, and Karachi, and <strong>3â€“5 business days</strong> for all other cities nationwide. During major seasonal drops, please allow an extra 24 hours for quality control and serial numbering.\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n\n            <div class=\"ud-faq-item\">\n              <button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-faq-trigger\" aria-expanded=\"false\" aria-controls=\"faq-ship-2\">\n                <span>What are the shipping charges and free delivery threshold?</span>\n                <span class=\"ud-faq-toggle-icon\" aria-hidden=\"true\">[+]</span>\n              </button>\n              <div id=\"faq-ship-2\" class=\"ud-faq-panel\">\n                <div class=\"ud-faq-panel-inner\">\n                  <div class=\"ud-faq-answer\">\n                    Standard tracked courier shipping is a flat rate of <strong>PKR 250</strong> across Pakistan. Orders above <strong>PKR 10,000</strong> automatically qualify for free express shipping. Cash on Delivery (COD) and prepaid card/wallet orders carry no hidden handling fees.\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n\n            <div class=\"ud-faq-item\">\n              <button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-faq-trigger\" aria-expanded=\"false\" aria-controls=\"faq-ship-3\">\n                <span>Can I pick up an online drop order at a physical URBANDROPS store?</span>\n                <span class=\"ud-faq-toggle-icon\" aria-hidden=\"true\">[+]</span>\n              </button>\n              <div id=\"faq-ship-3\" class=\"ud-faq-panel\">\n                <div class=\"ud-faq-panel-inner\">\n                  <div class=\"ud-faq-answer\">\n                    Yes. Same-day In-Store Pickup is available at our Islamabad (F-7 &amp; Centaurus), Lahore (Gulberg &amp; DHA Phase 5), and Karachi (Clifton &amp; DHA Phase 6) flagships. Bring your digital order confirmation and valid photo ID within 5 days of receiving the ready-for-pickup SMS.\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          </section>\n\n          <!-- SECTION 2: RETURNS -->\n          <section id=\"returns\" class=\"mb-5\" data-faq-section=\"returns\" aria-labelledby=\"faqReturnsHeading\">\n            <div class=\"ud-faq-section-header\">\n              <h2 id=\"faqReturnsHeading\" class=\"h4 fw-bold text-uppercase mb-0\">02 // Returns</h2>\n              <span class=\"ud-mono small\" style=\"color: var(--ud-accent);\">EXCHANGES &amp; REFUNDS</span>\n            </div>\n\n            <div class=\"ud-faq-item\">\n              <button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-faq-trigger\" aria-expanded=\"false\" aria-controls=\"faq-ret-1\">\n                <span>What is your 14-day return and size exchange policy?</span>\n                <span class=\"ud-faq-toggle-icon\" aria-hidden=\"true\">[+]</span>\n              </button>\n              <div id=\"faq-ret-1\" class=\"ud-faq-panel\">\n                <div class=\"ud-faq-panel-inner\">\n                  <div class=\"ud-faq-answer\">\n                    You may request a return or size exchange within <strong>14 days</strong> of delivery. Items must be unworn, unwashed, free of cologne or marks, and returned with all original URBANDROPS hangtags, zip-locks, and dust bags intact. Size exchanges can also be completed immediately at any flagship store.\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n\n            <div class=\"ud-faq-item\">\n              <button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-faq-trigger\" aria-expanded=\"false\" aria-controls=\"faq-ret-2\">\n                <span>Are limited-edition capsule drops eligible for refunds?</span>\n                <span class=\"ud-faq-toggle-icon\" aria-hidden=\"true\">[+]</span>\n              </button>\n              <div id=\"faq-ret-2\" class=\"ud-faq-panel\">\n                <div class=\"ud-faq-panel-inner\">\n                  <div class=\"ud-faq-answer\">\n                    Numbered archive drops and collaboration capsules are eligible for <strong>size exchanges or store credit</strong> only (while replacement stock lasts), unless the garment arrived defective or damaged. Standard core collection pieces qualify for full refunds back to your original payment method or bank transfer for COD orders.\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n\n            <div class=\"ud-faq-item\">\n              <button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-faq-trigger\" aria-expanded=\"false\" aria-controls=\"faq-ret-3\">\n                <span>How quickly are refunds processed after inspection?</span>\n                <span class=\"ud-faq-toggle-icon\" aria-hidden=\"true\">[+]</span>\n              </button>\n              <div id=\"faq-ret-3\" class=\"ud-faq-panel\">\n                <div class=\"ud-faq-panel-inner\">\n                  <div class=\"ud-faq-answer\">\n                    Once your return parcel arrives at our fulfillment hub, quality inspection takes up to <strong>48 hours</strong>. Approved refunds are issued within <strong>3â€“5 business days</strong> to your bank account, JazzCash/Easypaisa wallet, or original card, and you will receive an email confirmation receipt.\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          </section>\n\n          <!-- SECTION 3: SIZING -->\n          <section id=\"sizing\" class=\"mb-4\" data-faq-section=\"sizing\" aria-labelledby=\"faqSizingHeading\">\n            <div class=\"ud-faq-section-header\">\n              <h2 id=\"faqSizingHeading\" class=\"h4 fw-bold text-uppercase mb-0\">03 // Sizing</h2>\n              <span class=\"ud-mono small\" style=\"color: var(--ud-accent);\">FIT &amp; MEASUREMENTS</span>\n            </div>\n\n            <div class=\"ud-faq-item\">\n              <button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-faq-trigger\" aria-expanded=\"false\" aria-controls=\"faq-size-1\">\n                <span>Do URBANDROPS hoodies and tees run true to size or oversized?</span>\n                <span class=\"ud-faq-toggle-icon\" aria-hidden=\"true\">[+]</span>\n              </button>\n              <div id=\"faq-size-1\" class=\"ud-faq-panel\">\n                <div class=\"ud-faq-panel-inner\">\n                  <div class=\"ud-faq-answer\">\n                    Most URBANDROPS heavyweight tees (280 GSM) and French terry hoodies (480 GSM) are cut in a <strong>boxy, dropped-shoulder streetwear silhouette</strong>. Take your usual size for the intended relaxed drape, or size down one step if you prefer a closer standard fit.\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n\n            <div class=\"ud-faq-item\">\n              <button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-faq-trigger\" aria-expanded=\"false\" aria-controls=\"faq-size-2\">\n                <span>What are id=\"faq-size-2\" class=\"ud-faq-panel\">\n                <div class=\"ud-faq-panel-inner\">\n                  <div class=\"ud-faq-answer\">\n                    <p class=\"mb-2\">Reference flat-lay garment measurements (in inches, Â±0.5\" tolerance):</p>\n                    <div class=\"table-responsive\">\n                      <table class=\"table table-dark table-sm table-bordered class=\"ud-faq-item\">\n              <button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-faq-trigger\" aria-expanded=\"false\" aria-controls=\"faq-size-3\">\n                <span>Will heavyweight cotton pieces shrink after washing?</span>\n                <span class=\"ud-faq-toggle-icon\" aria-hidden=\"true\">[+]</span>\n              </button>\n              <div id=\"faq-size-3\" class=\"ud-faq-panel\">\n                <div class=\"ud-faq-panel-inner\">\n                  <div class=\"ud-faq-answer\">\n                    All URBANDROPS 100% combed cotton jerseys and fleeces are <strong>enzyme pre-shrunk</strong> during production. To preserve garment dimensions and puff-print graphics, wash inside out on a cold gentle cycle (30Â°C max) and hang dry flatâ€”never tumble dry on high heat.\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          </section>\n        </div>\n\n        <!-- Sidebar Summary Card -->\n        <aside class=\"col-lg-4\">\n          <div class=\"ud-card p-4 mb-4\">\n            <span class=\"ud-eyebrow\">Quick Policy Summary</span>\n            <h3 class=\"h5 fw-bold mb-3\">Drop Standards</h3>\n            <ul class=\"list-unstyled d-flex flex-column gap-3 mb-4 small text-secondary\">\n              <li class=\"d-flex justify-content-between border-bottom border-secondary border-opacity-25 pb-2\">\n                <span>Same-Day Dispatch</span>\n                <span class=\"ud-mono text-white\">Before 3 PM PKT</span>\n              </li>\n              <li class=\"d-flex justify-content-between border-bottom border-secondary border-opacity-25 pb-2\">\n                <span>Free Shipping</span>\n                <span class=\"ud-mono text-white\">Orders &gt; PKR 10K</span>\n              </li>\n              <li class=\"d-flex justify-content-between border-bottom border-secondary border-opacity-25 pb-2\">\n                <span>Return Window</span>\n                <span class=\"ud-mono text-white\">14 Days Unworn</span>\n              </li>\n              <li class=\"d-flex justify-content-between\">\n                <span>Fit Profile</span>\n                <span class=\"ud-mono\" style=\"color: var(--ud-accent);\">Boxy Streetwear</span>\n              </li>\n            </ul>\n            <a href=\"contact-support.html\" class=\"btn ud-btn-cta w-100 mb-2\">Submit a Support Ticket</a>\n            <a href=\"store-locator.html\" class=\"btn ud-btn-outline w-100\">Find Nearest Flagship</a>\n          </div>\n        </aside>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  </main>\n\n  <!-- Shared URBANDROPS Footer -->\n  <footer class=\"ud-footer\">\n    <div class=\"container\">\n      <div class=\"row g-4\">\n        <div class=\"col-lg-4\">\n          <a class=\"ud-brand mb-3\" href=\"../index.html\">\n            <img src=\"../images/Logo.png\" alt=\"URBANDROPS Logo\" class=\"ud-brand-logo\" onerror=\"this.classList.add('is-missing'); exit 1 }\n}\n$sl = Get-Content 'pages/store-locator.html' -Raw;\n$faq = Get-Content 'pages/faq.html' -Raw;\n$cs = Get-Content 'pages/contact-support.html' -Raw;\n$css = Get-Content 'css/support.css' -Raw;\n$js = Get-Content 'js/support.js' -Raw;\n\n$checks = @{\n  'Palette in CSS (#0D0D0D,#1A1A1A,#FFFFFF,#FF4D00,#CCFF00)' = ($css -match '#0D0D0D' -and $css -match '#1A1A1A' -and $css -match '#FFFFFF' -and $css -match '#FF4D00' -and $css -match '#CCFF00');\n  'Logo.png in all 3 HTMLs' = ($sl -match '../images/Logo.png' -and $faq -match '../images/Logo.png' -and $cs -match '../images/Logo.png');\n  'Bootstrap 5 in all 3 HTMLs' = ($sl -match 'bootstrap@5' -and $faq -match 'bootstrap@5' -and $cs -match 'bootstrap@5');\n  'cartCount in JS & badge in HTMLs' = ($js -match 'cartCount' -and $sl -match 'data-cart-badge' -and $faq -match 'data-cart-badge' -and $cs -match 'data-cart-badge');\n  'Store locator cities & OPEN NOW' = ($sl -match 'Islamabad' -and $sl -match 'Lahore' -and $sl -match 'Karachi' -and $js -match 'OPEN NOW' -and $sl -match 'cityFilter' -and $sl -match 'mapPinsContainer');\n  'FAQ sections & [+] / [-] toggles' = ($faq -match 'data-faq-section=\\\"shipping\\\"' -and $faq -match 'data-faq-section=\\\"returns\\\"' -and $faq -match 'data-faq-section=\\\"sizing\\\"' -and $js -match '\\[\\+\\]' -and $js -match '\\[-\\]');\n  'Contact support form & alert' = ($cs -match 'supportName' -and $cs -match 'supportEmail' -and $cs -match 'supportSubject' -and $cs -match 'supportMessage' -and $cs -match 'supportConfirmationAlert' -and $js -match 'EMAIL_REGEX')\n};\n$checks.GetEnumerator() | ForEach-Object { Write-Output ($_.Key + ' : ' + $_.Value) }\nif ($checks.Values -contains $false) { exit 1 }\n\"","WaitMsBeforeAsync":5000,"toolSummary":"Verify HTML, CSS, and JS requirements"}}]}

Style: Modern dark streetwear palette (#0D0D0D background, #1A1A1A card bg, #FFFFFF text, #FF4D00 CTAs, #CCFF00 accents). Use Bootstrap 5 and vanilla JavaScript only.\n2. Shared Header & Footer: Include shared URBANDROPS top navbar with Logo.png (<img src=\"../images/Logo.png\">), navigation links, search bar, and dynamic cart badge reading from localStorage ('cartCount').\n3. pages/womens-catalog.html (Women's Catalog): Grid of women's streetwear items rendered from a JS data array with search bar filter, category tags, and price sorting options (Low to High / High to Low).\n4. pages/cart-checkout.html (Cart & Checkout): Two-column layout. Left column lists cart items dynamically loaded from localStorage with quantity controls and remove buttons. Right column includes a promo code field ('URBAN10'), order summary breakdown (Subtotal, Shipping, Discount, Total), shipping address form, and 'PLACE ORDER' button.\n5. pages/order-tracking.html (Order Tracking): Order ID lookup input (#UD-1042) that displays a 4-step progress bar (Order Placed -> Processing -> Shipped -> Delivered) with status timestamp cards.\n6. Ensure all pages are 100% responsive using Bootstrap 5 container grid.\n\n</USER_REQUEST>\n<ADDITIONAL_METADATA>\nThe current local time is: 2026-11-27T10:28:39+05:00.\n</ADDITIONAL_METADATA>"}

background-color: var(--ud-cta);\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.68rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.06em;\n  padding: 0.25rem 0.55rem;\n  border-radius: 4px;\n}\n\n.ud-product-silhouette {\n  width: 112px;\n  height: 112px;\n  color: #E5E5E5;\n  opacity: 0.92;\n  transition: transform 0.25s ease, color 0.25s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-product-card:hover .ud-product-silhouette {\n  transform: scale(1.06);\n  color: #FFFFFF;\n}\n\n.ud-size-chip {\n  background-color: var(--ud-bg);\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  color: var(--ud-text-muted);\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.72rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  padding: 0.22rem 0.5rem;\n  border-radius: 4px;\n  cursor: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 1.05rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n}\n\n/* ---------- Cart & Checkout ---------- */\n.ud-cart-item {\n  background-color: var(--ud-surface);\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  border-radius: 10px;\n  padding: 1.15rem;\n  transition: border-color 0.2s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-cart-item:hover {\n  border-color: display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: center;\n  color: var(--ud-accent);\n}\n\n.ud-qty-group {\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  background-color: var(--ud-bg);\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  border-radius: 6px;\n  overflow: hidden;\n}\n\n.ud-qty-btn {\n  background: transparent;\n  border: none;\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-weight: 700;\n  width: 32px;\n  height: 32px;\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: center;\n  cursor: pointer;\n  transition: 0.5rem;\n  padding-top: 0.9rem;\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-display);\n  font-size: 1.25rem;\n  font-weight: 800;\n}\n\n/* ---------- Order Tracking 4-Step Progress Bar ---------- */\n.ud-track-steps {\n  position: relative;\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr);\n  gap: 1rem;\n  margin-top: 1.5rem;\n  margin-bottom: 2rem;\n}\n\n.ud-track-progress-track {\n
