1.5rem;\n}\n\n.ud-track-progress-fill {\n  height: 100%;\n  background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--ud-cta), var(--ud-accent));\n  border-radius: 999px;\n  transition: width 0.4s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-track-step-node {\n  background-color: var(--ud-bg);\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  border-radius: 8px;\n  padding: 1rem;\n  transition: border-color 0.25s ease, background-color 0.25s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-track-step-node.is-completed {\n  border-color: rgba(204, 255, 0, 0.55);\n  background-color: rgba(204, 255, 0, 0.05);\n}\n\n.ud-track-step-node.is-current {\n  border-color: var(--ud-cta);\n  background-color: rgba(255, 77, 0, 0.09);\n  box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px rgba(255, 77, 0, 0.25);\n}\n\n.ud-track-step-index {\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: center;\n  width: 28px;\n  height: 28px;\n  border-radius: 999px;\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.78rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  background-color: var(--ud-surface);\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  color: var(--ud-text-muted);\n  margin-bottom: 0.65rem;\n}\n\n.ud-track-step-node.is-completed .ud-track-step-index {\n  background-color: var(--ud-accent);\n  border-color: var(--ud-accent);\n  color: var(--ud-bg);\n}\n\n.ud-track-step-node.is-current .ud-track-step-index {\n  background-color: var(--ud-cta);\n  border-color: var(--ud-cta);\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n}\n\n.ud-timestamp-card {\n  background-color: var(--ud-bg);\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  border-left: 3px solid var(--ud-border);\n  border-radius: 8px;\n  padding: 1rem 1.15rem;\n  height: 100%;\n}\n\n.ud-timestamp-card.is-completed {\n  border-left-color: var(--ud-accent);\n}\n\n.ud-timestamp-card.is-current {\n  border-left-color: var(--ud-cta);\n}\n\n@media (max-width: 767.98px) {\n  .ud-track-steps {\n    grid-template-columns: 1fr;\n    gap: 0.75rem;\n  }\n}\n","Overwrite":true,"Path":"c:\\Users\\laptop villa\\SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP\\css\\commerce.css"}},{"name":"write_file","args":{"Content":"/**\n * URBANDROPS â€” Commerce & Order Tracking Vanilla JS\n * Handles:\n *  - Women's Catalog (pages/womens-catalog.html): JS data array, category filter, search filter, price sort, Add to Cart\n *  - Cart & Checkout (pages/cart-checkout.html): localStorage cart sync, qty +/-, remove, promo 'URBAN10', summary, shipping form, PLACE ORDER\n *  - Order Tracking (pages/order-tracking.html): Order ID lookup (#UD-1042 + localStorage placed orders), 4-step progress bar & status timestamp cards\n */\n\n(function () {\n  'use strict';\n\n  const CART_STORAGE_KEY = 'urbandropsCart';\n  const CART_COUNT_KEY = 'cartCount';\n  const ORDERS_STORAGE_KEY = 'urbandropsOrders';\n\n  /* ========================================================================\n     SHARED CART & CURRENCY UTILITIES\n     ======================================================================== */\n  function formatPKR(amount) {\n    const safe = Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(amount) || 0));\n    return 'PKR ' + safe.toLocaleString('en-US');\n  }\n\n  function readCart() {\n    try {\n      const raw = localStorage.getItem(CART_STORAGE_KEY);\n      if (!raw) return [];\n      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);\n      return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : [];\n    } catch (err) {\n      return [];\n    }\n  }\n\n  function writeCart(items) {\n    const clean = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];\n    const totalCount = clean.reduce(function (sum, item) {\n      return sum + (parseInt(item.qty, 10) || 0);\n    }, 0);\n\n    try {\n      localStorage.setItem(CART_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(clean));\n      localStorage.setItem(CART_COUNT_KEY, String(totalCount));\n    } catch (err) {\n      // Ignore storage quota errors cleanly\n    }\n\n    // Sync navbar cart badge immediately across the current tab\n    document.querySelectorAll('[data-cart-badge]').forEach(function (badge) {\n      badge.textContent = String(totalCount);\n      badge.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Cart items: ' + totalCount);\n    });\n  }\n\n  function showCommerceToast(message) {\n    const existing = document.getElementById('udCommerceToast');\n    if (existing) existing.remove();\n\n    const toast = document.createElement('div');\n    toast.id = 'udCommerceToast';\n    toast.className = 'ud-toast';\n    toast.setAttribute('role', 'status');\n    toast.textContent = message;\n    document.body.appendChild(toast);\n\n    window.setTimeout(function () {\n      if (toast.parentNode) toast.remove();\n    }, 3400);\n  }\n\n  /* ========================================================================\n     1. WOMEN'S CATALOG DATA & LOGIC (pages/womens-catalog.html)\n     ======================================================================== */\n  const WOMENS_CATALOG = [\n    {\n      id: 'w-hoodie-01',\n      name: 'APEX CROPPED HEAVY HOODIE',\n      category: 'Hoodies & Sweats',\n      price: 7400,\n      colorway: 'Obsidian Black / Acid',\n      tag: '480 GSM FLEECE',\n      badge: 'DROP 04',\n      sizes: ['XS', 'S', 'M', 'L'],\n      icon: 'hoodie',\n      description: 'Boxy cropped cut with double-lined hood and high-density puff back print.'\n    },\n    {\n      id: 'w-jacket-01',\n      name: 'KINETIC NYLON BOMBER SHELL',\n      category: 'Outerwear',\n      price: 12900,\n      colorway: 'Matte Onyx / Signal Orange',\n      tag: 'WATER REPELLENT',\n      badge: 'LIMITED',\n      sizes: ['XS', 'S', 'M', 'L', 'XL'],\n      icon: 'jacket',\n      description: 'Ripstop windproof shell with articulated sleeves and two-way hardware.'\n    },\n    {\n      id: 'w-cargo-01',\n      name: 'SECTOR-07 PARACHUTE CARGOS',\n      category: 'Cargos & Bottoms',\n      price: 8200,\n      colorway: 'Graphite Charcoal',\n      tag: 'RELAXED WIDE LEG',\n      badge: 'BESTSELLER',\n      sizes: ['XS', 'S', 'M', 'L'],\n      icon: 'pants',\n      description: 'Lightweight technical twill with bungee ankle toggles and gusset pockets.'\n    },\n    {\n      id: 'w-tee-01',\n      name: 'RAW SEAM BABY TEE',\n      category: 'Tees & Tops',\n      price: 3600,\n      colorway: 'Washed Charcoal',\n      tag: '260 GSM RIB',\n      badge: 'CORE',\n      sizes: ['XS', 'S', 'M', 'L'],\n      icon: 'tee',\n      description: 'Contoured stretch-cotton jersey with exposed contrast overlock stitching.'\n    },\n    {\n      id: 'w-hoodie-02',\n      name: 'ACID WASH OVERSIZED ZIP-UP',\n      category: 'Hoodies & Sweats',\n      price: 8600,\n      colorway: 'Smoke Slate',\n      tag: 'ENZYME WASHED',\n      badge: 'NEW',\n      sizes: ['S', 'M', 'L', 'XL'],\n      icon: 'hoodie',\n      description: 'Dropped-shoulder full-zip hoodie with antique silver YKK metal zipper.'\n    },\n    {\n      id: 'w-jacket-02',\n      name: 'UTILITY PUFFER VEST',\n      category: 'Outerwear',\n      price: 9800,\n      colorway: 'Carbon Black',\n      tag: 'THERMAL FILL',\n      badge: 'DROP 04',\n      sizes: ['XS', 'S', 'M', 'L'],\n      icon: 'jacket',\n      description: 'Cropped tactical puffer vest with concealed chest stash pocket.'\n    },\n    {\n      id: 'w-cargo-02',\n      name: 'CONCRETE WIDE SWEATPANTS',\n      category: 'Cargos & Bottoms',\n      price: 6200,\n      colorway: 'Asphalt Grey',\n      tag: '420 GSM TERRY',\n      badge: 'CORE',\n      sizes: ['XS', 'S', 'M', 'L', 'XL'],\n      icon: 'pants',\n      description: 'Open-hem wide-leg lounge fleece with raw edge waistband drawcords.'\n    },\n    {\n      id: 'w-tee-02',\n      name: 'CYBER GRAPHIC BOXY TEE',\n      category: 'Tees & Tops',\n      price: 4200,\n      colorway: 'Jet Black / Lime',\n      tag: '280 GSM JERSEY',\n      badge: 'NEW',\n      sizes: ['XS', 'S', 'M', 'L', 'XL'],\n      icon: 'tee',\n      description: 'Oversized unisex-block womenâ€™s cut featuring reflective back typography.'\n    },\n    {\n      id: 'w-acc-01',\n      name: 'TACTICAL CROSSBODY SLING',\n      category: 'Accessories',\n      price: 4500,\n      colorway: 'Stealth Black',\n      tag: 'CORDURA NYLON',\n      badge: 'GEAR',\n      sizes: ['ONE SIZE'],\n      icon: 'bag',\n      description: 'Compact weatherproof crossbody bag with quick-release fidlock buckle.'\n    }\n  ];\n\n  function getSilhouetteSvg(type) {\n    if (type === 'hoodie') {\n      return '<svg class=\"ud-product-silhouette\" viewBox=\"0 0 64 64\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" stroke-width=\"2.2\" stroke-linecap=\"round\" stroke-linejoin=\"round\" aria-hidden=\"true\">' +\n        '<path d=\"M22 14c0-5 4-8 10-8s10 3 10 8l10 6 4 20-8 3-3-13v24H19V30l-3 13-8-3 4-20 10-6z\"/>' +\n        '<path d=\"M24 14c2 4 14 4 16 0\"/>' +\n        '<rect x=\"24\" y=\"38\" width=\"16\" height=\"10\" rx=\"2\"/>' +\n      '</svg>';\n    }\n    if (type === 'jacket') {\n      return '<svg class=\"ud-product-silhouette\" viewBox=\"0 0 64 64\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" stroke-width=\"2.2\" stroke-linecap=\"round\" stroke-linejoin=\"round\" aria-hidden=\"true\">' +\n        '<path d=\"M20 12l12 4 12-4 10 8 4 22-9 2-3-14v24H18V30l-3 14-9-2 4-22 10-8z\"/>' +\n        '<line x1=\"32\" y1=\"16\" x2=\"32\" y2=\"54\"/>' +\n      '</svg>';\n    }\n    if (type === 'pants') {\n      return '<svg class=\"ud-product-silhouette\" viewBox=\"0 0 64 64\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" stroke-width=\"2.2\" stroke-linecap=\"round\" stroke-linejoin=\"round\" aria-hidden=\"true\">' +\n        '<path d=\"M18 10h28l4 44-12 2-6-26-6 26-12-2 4-44z\"/>' +\n        '<line x1=\"18\" y1=\"18\" x2=\"46\" y2=\"18\"/>' +\n        '<rect x=\"14\" y=\"28\" width=\"7\" height=\"10\" rx=\"1\"/>' +\n        '<rect x=\"43\" y=\"28\" width=\"7\" height=\"10\" rx=\"1\"/>' +\n      '</svg>';\n    }\n    if (type === 'bag') {\n      return '<svg class=\"ud-product-silhouette\" viewBox=\"0 0 64 64\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" stroke-width=\"2.2\" stroke-linecap=\"round\" stroke-linejoin=\"round\" aria-hidden=\"true\">' +\n        '<rect x=\"14\" y=\"22\" width=\"36\" height=\"28\" rx=\"4\"/>' +\n        '<path d=\"M22 22c0-7 4-12 10-12s10 5 10 12\"/>' +\n        '<line x1=\"14\" y1=\"34\" x2=\"50\" y2=\"34\"/>' +\n        '<rect x=\"28\" y=\"31\" width=\"8\" height=\"6\" rx=\"1\"/>' +\n      '</svg>';\n    }\n    return '<svg class=\"ud-product-silhouette\" viewBox=\"0 0 64 64\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" stroke-width=\"2.2\" stroke-linecap=\"round\" stroke-linejoin=\"round\" aria-hidden=\"true\">' +\n      '<path d=\"M20 12c3 4 21 4 24 0l12 8-5 11-7-3v24H20V28l-7 3-5-11 12-8z\"/>' +\n    '</svg>';\n  }\n\n  function initWomensCatalog() {\n    const grid = document.getElementById('womensCatalogGrid');\n    if (!grid) return;\n\n    const searchInput = document.getElementById('catalogSearchInput');\n    const navSearchInput = document.getElementById('udNavSearchInput');\n    const navSearchForm = document.getElementById('udNavSearchForm');\n    const sortSelect = document.getElementById('catalogPriceSort');\n    const categoryButtons = document.querySelectorAll('[data-catalog-category]');\n    const countLabel = document.getElementById('catalogCountLabel');\n    const emptyState = document.getElementById('catalogEmptyState');\n    const resetBtn = document.getElementById('resetCatalogFilters');\n\n    let activeCategory = 'All';\n    let searchQuery = '';\n    let sortMode = 'featured';\n    const selectedSizes = {};\n\n    WOMENS_CATALOG.forEach(function (item) {\n      selectedSizes[item.id] = item.sizes.includes('M') ? 'M' : item.sizes[0];\n    });\n\n    function addToCart(productId) {\n      const product = WOMENS_CATALOG.find(function (p) { return p.id === productId; });\n      if (!product) return;\n\n      const chosenSize = selectedSizes[productId] || product.sizes[0];\n      const cart = readCart();\n      const existing = cart.find(function (entry) {\n        return entry.id === product.id && entry.size === chosenSize;\n      });\n\n      if (existing) {\n        existing.qty += 1;\n      } else {\n        cart.push({\n          id: product.id,\n          name: product.name,\n          category: product.category,\n          colorway: product.colorway,\n          size: chosenSize,\n          price: product.price,\n          qty: 1,\n          icon: product.icon\n        });\n      }\n\n      writeCart(cart);\n      showCommerceToast('Added \"' + product.name + '\" (' + chosenSize + ') to cart.');\n    }\n\n    function renderCatalog() {\n      const lowerQuery = searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();\n\n      let filtered = WOMENS_CATALOG.filter(function (item) {\n        const matchesCategory = activeCategory === 'All' || item.category === activeCategory;\n        const matchesSearch = !lowerQuery ||\n          item.name.toLowerCase().includes(lowerQuery) ||\n          item.category.toLowerCase().includes(lowerQuery) ||\n          item.colorway.toLowerCase().includes(lowerQuery) ||\n          item.tag.toLowerCase().includes(lowerQuery) ||\n          item.description.toLowerCase().includes(lowerQuery);\n        return matchesCategory && matchesSearch;\n      });\n\n      if (sortMode === 'price-asc') {\n        filtered = filtered.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return a.price - b.price; });\n      } else if (sortMode === 'price-desc') {\n        filtered = filtered.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return b.price - a.price; });\n      }\n\n      if (countLabel) {\n        countLabel.textContent = 'Showing ' + filtered.length + ' of ' + WOMENS_CATALOG.length + ' Pieces';\n      }\n\n      if (emptyState) {\n        emptyState.classList.toggle('d-none', filtered.length > 0);\n      }\n\n      grid.innerHTML = '';\n\n      filtered.forEach(function (item) {\n        const col = document.createElement('div');\n        col.className = 'col-12 col-sm-6 col-lg-4';\n\n        const sizeChipsHtml = item.sizes.map(function (sz) {\n          const isActive = selectedSizes[item.id] === sz;\n          return '<button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-size-chip ' + (isActive ? 'is-active' : '') + '\" data-product-id=\"' + item.id + '\" data-size-val=\"' + sz + '\">' + sz + '</button>';\n        }).join('');\n\n        col.innerHTML =\n          '<article class=\"ud-product-card\">' +\n            '<div class=\"ud-product-visual\">' +\n              '<span class=\"ud-product-tag\">' + item.tag + '</span>' +\n              '<span class=\"ud-product-badge-drop\">' + item.badge + '</span>' +\n              getSilhouetteSvg(item.icon) +\n            '</div>' +\n            '<div class=\"p-3 p-md-4 d-flex flex-column flex-grow-1\">' +\n              '<div class=\"d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-1\">' +\n                '<span class=\"ud-mono small text-uppercase\" style=\"color: var(--ud-accent);\">' + item.category + '</span>' +\n                '<span class=\"ud-mono small text-secondary\">' + item.colorway + '</span>' +\n              '</div>' +\n              '<h2 class=\"h5 fw-bold mb-2\">' + item.name + '</h2>' +\n              '<p class=\"text-secondary small mb-3 flex-grow-1\">' + item.description + '</p>' +\n              '<div class=\"mb-3\">' +\n                '<div class=\"ud-mono small text-secondary mb-1\">SIZE:</div>' +\n                '<div class=\"d-flex flex-wrap gap-1\">' + sizeChipsHtml + '</div>' +\n              '</div>' +\n              '<div class=\"d-flex align-items-center justify-content-between gap-2 pt-2 border-top border-secondary border-opacity-25\">' +\n                '<span class=\"ud-price-tag\">' + formatPKR(item.price) + '</span>' +\n                '<button type=\"button\" class=\"btn ud-btn-cta btn-sm\" data-add-to-cart=\"' + item.id + '\">Add to Cart</button>' +\n              '</div>' +\n            '</div>' +\n          '</article>';\n\n        grid.appendChild(col);\n      });\n\n      grid.querySelectorAll('[data-size-val]').forEach(function (btn) {\n        btn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n          const pid = btn.getAttribute('data-product-id');\n          const sz = btn.getAttribute('data-size-val');\n          selectedSizes[pid] = sz;\n          grid.querySelectorAll('[data-product-id=\"' + pid + '\"]').forEach(function (sib) {\n            sib.classList.toggle('is-active', sib.getAttribute('data-size-val') === sz);\n          });\n        });\n      });\n\n      grid.querySelectorAll('[data-add-to-cart]').forEach(function (btn) {\n        btn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n          addToCart(btn.getAttribute('data-add-to-cart'));\n        });\n      });\n    }\n\n    categoryButtons.forEach(function (btn) {\n      btn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n        activeCategory = btn.getAttribute('data-catalog-category');\n        categoryButtons.forEach(function (b) {\n          b.classList.toggle('active', b === btn);\n        });\n        renderCatalog();\n      });\n    });\n\n    if (searchInput) {\n      searchInput.addEventListener('input', function () {\n        searchQuery = searchInput.value;\n        if (navSearchInput) navSearchInput.value = searchQuery;\n        renderCatalog();\n      });\n    }\n\n    if (navSearchInput && navSearchForm) {\n      navSearchInput.addEventListener('input', function () {\n        searchQuery = navSearchInput.value;\n        if (searchInput) searchInput.value = searchQuery;\n        renderCatalog();\n      });\n      navSearchForm.addEventListener('submit', function (event) {\n        event.preventDefault();\n        searchQuery = navSearchInput.value;\n        if (searchInput) searchInput.value = searchQuery;\n        renderCatalog();\n      }, true);\n    }\n\n    if (sortSelect) {\n      sortSelect.addEventListener('change', function () {\n        sortMode = sortSelect.value;\n        renderCatalog();\n      });\n    }\n\n    if (resetBtn) {\n      resetBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n        activeCategory = 'All';\n        searchQuery = '';\n        sortMode = 'featured';\n        if (searchInput) searchInput.value = '';\n        if (navSearchInput) navSearchInput.value = '';\n        if (sortSelect) sortSelect.value = 'featured';\n        categoryButtons.forEach(function (b) {\n          b.classList.toggle('active', b.getAttribute('data-catalog-category') === 'All');\n        });\n        renderCatalog();\n      });\n    }\n\n    renderCatalog();\n  }\n\n  /* ========================================================================\n     2. CART & CHECKOUT PAGE (pages/cart-checkout.html)\n     ======================================================================== */\n  const SAMPLE_SEED_CART = [\n    {\n      id: 'w-hoodie-01',\n      name: 'APEX CROPPED HEAVY HOODIE',\n      category: 'Hoodies & Sweats',\n      colorway: 'Obsidian Black / Acid',\n      size: 'M',\n      price: 7400,\n      qty: 1,\n      icon: 'hoodie'\n    },\n    {\n      id: 'w-cargo-01',\n      name: 'SECTOR-07 PARACHUTE CARGOS',\n      category: 'Cargos & Bottoms',\n      colorway: 'Graphite Charcoal',\n      size: 'S',\n      price: 8200,\n      qty: 1,\n      icon: 'pants'\n    }\n  ];\n\n  function initCartCheckout() {\n    const cartItemsContainer = document.getElementById('cartItemsContainer');\n    if (!cartItemsContainer) return;\n\n    const cartEmptyState = document.getElementById('cartEmptyState');\n    const loadSampleCartBtn = document.getElementById('loadSampleCartBtn');\n    const cartItemCountLabel = document.getElementById('cartItemCountLabel');\n\n    const promoInput = document.getElementById('promoCodeInput');\n    const applyPromoBtn = document.getElementById('applyPromoBtn');\n    const promoFeedback = document.getElementById('promoFeedback');\n\n    const summarySubtotalEl = document.getElementById('summarySubtotal');\n    const summaryShippingEl = document.getElementById('summaryShipping');\n    const summaryDiscountEl = document.getElementById('summaryDiscount');\n    const summaryDiscountRow = document.getElementById('summaryDiscountRow');\n    const summaryTotalEl = document.getElementById('summaryTotal');\n\n    const checkoutForm = document.getElementById('checkoutShippingForm');\n    const placeOrderBtn = document.getElementById('placeOrderBtn');\n    const orderAlert = document.getElementById('orderConfirmationAlert');\n    const placedOrderIdEl = document.getElementById('placedOrderId');\n    const placedOrderSummaryEl = document.getElementById('placedOrderSummary');\n    const trackPlacedOrderLink = document.getElementById('trackPlacedOrderLink');\n\n    let appliedPromo = '';\n\n    function computeTotals(cart) {\n      const subtotal = cart.reduce(function (sum, item) {\n        return sum + (Number(item.price) * (parseInt(item.qty, 10) || 0));\n      }, 0);\n      const shipping = subtotal === 0 ? 0 : (subtotal >= 10000 ? 0 : 250);\n      const discount = appliedPromo === 'URBAN10' ? Math.round(subtotal * 0.10) : 0;\n      const total = Math.max(0, subtotal - discount + shipping);\n      return { subtotal: subtotal, shipping: shipping, discount: discount, total: total };\n    }\n\n    function renderSummary(cart) {\n      const totals = computeTotals(cart);\n\n      if (summarySubtotalEl) summarySubtotalEl.textContent = formatPKR(totals.subtotal);\n      if (summaryShippingEl) {\n        summaryShippingEl.textContent = totals.subtotal === 0\n          ? formatPKR(0)\n          : (totals.shipping === 0 ? 'FREE' : formatPKR(totals.shipping));\n      }\n      if (summaryDiscountRow) {\n        summaryDiscountRow.classList.toggle('d-none', totals.discount === 0);\n      }\n      if (summaryDiscountEl) {\n        summaryDiscountEl.textContent = '- ' + formatPKR(totals.discount);\n      }\n      if (summaryTotalEl) summaryTotalEl.textContent = formatPKR(totals.total);\n\n      if (placeOrderBtn) {\n        placeOrderBtn.disabled = cart.length === 0;\n      }\n    }\n\n    function renderCart() {\n      const cart = readCart();\n      const totalQty = cart.reduce(function (sum, item) { return sum + (parseInt(item.qty, 10) || 0); }, 0);\n\n      if (cartItemCountLabel) {\n        cartItemCountLabel.textContent = totalQty + ' ' + (totalQty === 1 ? 'Item' : 'Items');\n      }\n\n      if (cartEmptyState) {\n        cartEmptyState.classList.toggle('d-none', cart.length > 0);\n      }\n\n      cartItemsContainer.innerHTML = '';\n\n      cart.forEach(function (item, index) {\n        const lineTotal = Number(item.price) * (parseInt(item.qty, 10) || 1);\n        const card = document.createElement('article');\n        card.className = 'ud-cart-item';\n\n        card.innerHTML =\n          '<div class=\"d-flex flex-column flex-sm-row align-items-sm-center justify-content-between gap-3\">' +\n            '<div class=\"d-flex align-items-center gap-3\">' +\n              '<div class=\"ud-cart-thumb\">' +\n                getSilhouetteSvg(item.icon || 'tee').replace('class=\"ud-product-silhouette\"', 'width=\"44\" height=\"44\"') +\n              '</div>' +\n              '<div>' +\n                '<span class=\"ud-mono small text-uppercase\" style=\"color: var(--ud-accent);\">' + (item.category || 'URBANDROPS') + '</span>' +\n                '<h2 class=\"h6 fw-bold mb-1\">' + item.name + '</h2>' +\n                '<div class=\"ud-mono small text-secondary\">' +\n                  'SIZE: <span class=\"text-white\">' + item.size + '</span> &bull; ' + (item.colorway || 'Standard') +\n                '</div>' +\n                '<div class=\"ud-mono small text-secondary mt-1\">' + formatPKR(item.price) + ' each</div>' +\n              '</div>' +\n            '</div>' +\n            '<div class=\"d-flex align-items-center justify-content-between justify-content-sm-end gap-3\">' +\n              '<div class=\"ud-qty-group\" aria-label=\"Quantity controls for ' + item.name + '\">' +\n                '<button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-qty-btn\" data-qty-dec=\"' + index + '\" aria-label=\"Decrease quantity\">-</button>' +\n                '<span class=\"ud-qty-value\">' + item.qty + '</span>' +\n                '<button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-qty-btn\" data-qty-inc=\"' + index + '\" aria-label=\"Increase quantity\">+</button>' +\n              '</div>' +\n              '<div class=\"text-end\" style=\"min-width: 92px;\">' +\n                '<div class=\"ud-mono fw-bold text-white\">' + formatPKR(lineTotal) + '</div>' +\n                '<button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-remove-btn mt-1\" data-remove-idx=\"' + index + '\">Remove</button>' +\n              '</div>' +\n            '</div>' +\n          '</div>';\n\n        cartItemsContainer.appendChild(card);\n      });\n\n      cartItemsContainer.querySelectorAll('[data-qty-dec]').forEach(function (btn) {\n        btn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n          const idx = parseInt(btn.getAttribute('data-qty-dec'), 10);\n          const updated = readCart();\n          if (!updated[idx]) return;\n          if (updated[idx].qty > 1) {\n            updated[idx].qty -= 1;\n          } else {\n            updated.splice(idx, 1);\n          }\n          writeCart(updated);\n          renderCart();\n        });\n      });\n\n      cartItemsContainer.querySelectorAll('[data-qty-inc]').forEach(function (btn) {\n        btn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n          const idx = parseInt(btn.getAttribute('data-qty-inc'), 10);\n          const updated = readCart();\n          if (!updated[idx]) return;\n          updated[idx].qty += 1;\n          writeCart(updated);\n          renderCart();\n        });\n      });\n\n      cartItemsContainer.querySelectorAll('[data-remove-idx]').forEach(function (btn) {\n        btn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n          const idx = parseInt(btn.getAttribute('data-remove-idx'), 10);\n          const updated = readCart();\n          const removed = updated.splice(idx, 1);\n          writeCart(updated);\n          renderCart();\n          if (removed[0]) {\n            showCommerceToast('Removed \"' + removed[0].name + '\" from cart.');\n          }\n        });\n      });\n\n      renderSummary(cart);\n    }\n\n    if (loadSampleCartBtn) {\n      loadSampleCartBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n        writeCart(SAMPLE_SEED_CART.map(function (item) { return Object.assign({}, item); }));\n        renderCart();\n        showCommerceToast('Loaded 2 sample URBANDROPS items into your cart.');\n      });\n    }\n\n    if (applyPromoBtn && promoInput) {\n      applyPromoBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n        const code = promoInput.value.trim().toUpperCase();\n        if (promoFeedback) {\n          promoFeedback.classList.remove('is-valid', 'is-invalid');\n        }\n\n        if (code === 'URBAN10') {\n          appliedPromo = 'URBAN10';\n          if (promoFeedback) {\n            promoFeedback.classList.add('is-valid');\n            promoFeedback.textContent = 'Promo URBAN10 applied: 10% off subtotal.';\n          }\n        } else if (code === '') {\n          appliedPromo = '';\n          if (promoFeedback) {\n            promoFeedback.classList.add('is-invalid');\n            promoFeedback.textContent = 'Enter a promo code (try URBAN10).';\n          }\n        } else {\n          appliedPromo = '';\n          if (promoFeedback) {\n            promoFeedback.classList.add('is-invalid');\n            promoFeedback.textContent = 'Invalid promo code. Use URBAN10 for 10% off.';\n          }\n        }\n        renderSummary(readCart());\n      });\n    }\n\n    if (checkoutForm) {\n      checkoutForm.addEventListener('submit', function (event) {\n        event.preventDefault();\n        const cart = readCart();\n        if (cart.length === 0) {\n          showCommerceToast('Your cart is empty. Add items before placing an order.');\n          return;\n        }\n\n        const fullName = document.getElementById('shipName');\n        const email = document.getElementById('shipEmail');\n        const phone = document.getElementById('shipPhone');\n        const address = document.getElementById('shipAddress');\n        const city = document.getElementById('shipCity');\n        const postal = document.getElementById('shipPostal');\n\n        const requiredFields = [fullName, email, phone, address, city, postal];\n        let valid = true;\n\n        requiredFields.forEach(function (field) {\n          if (!field) return;\n          const ok = field.value.trim().length > 0 && (field.id !== 'shipEmail' || /^[^\\s@]+@[^\\s@]+\\.[^\\s@]{2,}$/.test(field.value.trim()));\n          field.classList.toggle('is-invalid-ud', !ok);\n          field.classList.toggle('is-valid-ud', ok);\n          if (!ok) valid = false;\n        });\n\n        if (!valid) {\n          showCommerceToast('Please complete all shipping address fields.');\n          return;\n        }\n\n        const totals = computeTotals(cart);\n        const orderCode = '#UD-' + Math.floor(2000 + Math.random() * 7999);\n        const nowStr = new Date().toLocaleString('en-US', {\n          month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit'\n        });\n\n        // Save placed order so user can immediately track it on order-tracking.html\n        try {\n          const existingOrders = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(ORDERS_STORAGE_KEY) || '{}');\n          existingOrders[orderCode.replace('#', '').toUpperCase()] = {\n            orderId: orderCode,\n            customer: fullName.value.trim(),\n            city: city.value,\n            total: formatPKR(totals.total),\n            carrier: 'URBANDROPS Express Dispatch',\n            eta: 'Est. Delivery: 2â€“3 Business Days',\n            currentStep: 1,\n            steps: [\n              { title: 'Order Placed', status: 'completed', time: nowStr, location: 'Online Checkout', note: 'Order confirmed & payment verified.' },\n              { title: 'Processing', status: 'pending', time: 'Pending', location: 'Lahore Fulfillment Hub', note: 'Queued for garment QC & serial tagging.' },\n              { title: 'Shipped', status: 'pending', time: 'Pending', location: 'In Transit', note: 'Awaiting courier handoff.' },\n              { title: 'Delivered', status: 'pending', time: 'Pending', location: city.value, note: 'Awaiting final doorstep delivery.' }\n            ]\n          };\n          localStorage.setItem(ORDERS_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(existingOrders));\n        } catch (err) {\n          // Ignore storage issues\n        }\n\n        if (placedOrderIdEl) placedOrderIdEl.textContent = orderCode;\n        if (placedOrderSummaryEl) {\n          placedOrderSummaryEl.textContent =\n            'Total charged: ' + formatPKR(totals.total) + ' â€¢ Shipping to ' + address.value.trim() + ', ' + city.value + '.';\n        }\n        if (trackPlacedOrderLink) {\n          trackPlacedOrderLink.href = 'order-tracking.html?order=' + encodeURIComponent(orderCode.replace('#', ''));\n        }\n        if (orderAlert) {\n          orderAlert.classList.remove('d-none');\n          orderAlert.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'nearest' });\n        }\n\n        appliedPromo = '';\n        if (promoInput) promoInput.value = '';\n        if (promoFeedback) promoFeedback.textContent = '';\n        checkoutForm.reset();\n        requiredFields.forEach(function (f) {\n          if (f) f.classList.remove('is-valid-ud', 'is-invalid-ud');\n        });\n        writeCart([]);\n        renderCart();\n      });\n    }\n\n    // Sync if localStorage changes in another tab\n    window.addEventListener('storage', function (event) {\n      if (event.key === CART_STORAGE_KEY || event.key === CART_COUNT_KEY) {\n        renderCart();\n      }\n    });\n\n    renderCart();\n {\n    const normalized = rawCode.trim().replace(/^#/, '').toUpperCase();\n    if (!normalized) return null;\n\n    if (DEMO_ORDERS[normalized]) {\n      return DEMO_ORDERS[normalized];\n    }\n\n    try {\n      const saved = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(ORDERS_STORAGE_KEY) || '{}');\n      if (saved[normalized]) return saved[normalized];\n    } catch (err) {\n      // Ignore parse errors\n    }\n\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  function initOrderTracking() {\n    const lookupForm = document.getElementById('orderTrackingForm');\n    const orderInput = document.getElementById('orderIdInput');\n    const trackingResultCard = document.getElementById('trackingResultCard');\n    const trackingNotFound = document.getElementById('trackingNotFound');\n    const progressFill = document.getElementById('trackingProgressFill');\n    const stepsContainer = document.getElementById('trackingStepsContainer');\n    const timestampsContainer = document.getElementById('trackingTimestampsContainer');\n\n    if (!lookupForm || !orderInput || !stepsContainer || !timestampsContainer) return;\n\n    function renderOrder(order) {\n      if (!order) {\n        if (trackingResultCard) trackingResultCard.classList.add('d-none');\n        if (trackingNotFound) trackingNotFound.classList.remove('d-none');\n        return;\n      }\n\n      if (trackingNotFound) trackingNotFound.classList.add('d-none');\n      if (trackingResultCard) trackingResultCard.classList.remove('d-none');\n\n      const idEl = document.getElementById('trackMetaOrderId');\n      const customerEl = document.getElementById('trackMetaCustomer');\n      const cityEl = document.getElementById('trackMetaCity');\n      const carrierEl = document.getElementById('trackMetaCarrier');\n      const etaEl = document.getElementById('trackMetaEta');\n      const totalEl = document.getElementById('trackMetaTotal');\n\n      if (idEl) idEl.textContent = order.orderId;\n      if (customerEl) customerEl.textContent = order.customer;\n      if (cityEl) cityEl.textContent = order.city;\n      if (carrierEl) carrierEl.textContent = order.carrier;\n      if (etaEl) etaEl.textContent = order.eta;\n      if (totalEl) totalEl.textContent = order.total;\n\n      const percentMap = { 1: '25%', 2: '50%', 3: '75%', 4: '100%' };\n      if (progressFill) {\n        progressFill.style.width = percentMap[order.currentStep] || '25%';\n        progressFill.setAttribute('aria-valuenow', String((order.currentStep || 1) * 25));\n      }\n\n      stepsContainer.innerHTML = '';\n      timestampsContainer.innerHTML = '';\n\n      order.steps.forEach(function (step, idx) {\n        const stateClass = step.status === 'completed'\n          ? 'is-completed'\n          : (step.status === 'current' ? 'is-current' : '');\n\n        const statusBadgeText = step.status === 'completed'\n          ? 'DONE'\n          : (step.status === 'current' ? 'IN PROGRESS' : 'PENDING');\n\n        const stepNode = document.createElement('div');\n        stepNode.className = 'ud-track-step-node ' + stateClass;\n        stepNode.innerHTML =\n          '<div class=\"d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-1\">' +\n            '<span class=\"ud-track-step-index\">0' + (idx + 1) + '</span>' +\n            '<span class=\"ud-mono small\" style=\"color:' + (step.status === 'completed' ? 'var(--ud-accent)' : (step.status === 'current' ? 'var(--ud-cta)' : 'var(--ud-text-muted)')) + ';\">' + statusBadgeText + '</span>' +\n          '</div>' +\n          '<h3 class=\"h6 fw-bold mb-1\">' + step.title + '</h3>' +\n          '<div class=\"ud-mono small text-secondary\">' + step.time + '</div>';\n\n        stepsContainer.appendChild(stepNode);\n\n        const tsCol = document.createElement('div');\n        tsCol.className = 'col-12 col-md-6';\n        tsCol.innerHTML =\n          '<article class=\"ud-timestamp-card ' + stateClass + '\">' +\n            '<div class=\"d-flex flex-wrap justify-content-between align-items-center gap-2 mb-2\">' +\n              '<span class=\"ud-mono small fw-bold text-uppercase\" style=\"color:' + (step.status === 'completed' ? 'var(--ud-accent)' : (step.status === 'current' ? 'var(--ud-cta)' : 'var(--ud-text-muted)')) + ';\">' +\n                'STEP 0' + (idx + 1) + ' // ' + step.title +\n              '</span>' +\n              '<span class=\"ud-city-badge\">' + step.time + '</span>' +\n            '</div>' +\n            '<div class=\"small fw-semibold text-white mb-1\">' + step.location + '</div>' +\n            '<p class=\"small text-secondary mb-0\">' + step.note + '</p>' +\n          '</article>';\n\n        timestampsContainer.appendChild(tsCol);\n      });\n    }\n\n    lookupForm.addEventListener('submit', function (event) {\n      event.preventDefault();\n      const order = getOrderByCode(orderInput.value);\n      renderOrder(order);\n    });\n\n    document.querySelectorAll('[data-sample-order]').forEach(function (btn) {\n      btn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n        const sample = btn.getAttribute('data-sample-order');\n        orderInput.value = sample;\n        renderOrder(getOrderByCode(sample));\n      });\n    });\n\n    // Check URL ?order= parameter or load #UD-1042 by default\n    const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);\n    const urlOrder = params.get('order');\n    const initialCode = urlOrder ? ('#' + urlOrder.replace(/^#/, '').toUpperCase()) : '#UD-1042';\n    orderInput.value = initialCode;\n    renderOrder(getOrderByCode(initialCode));\n  }\n\n  /* ========================================================================\n     INIT ON DOM READY\n     ======================================================================== */\n  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {\n    initWomensCatalog();\n    initCartCheckout();\n    initOrderTracking();\n  });\n})();\n","Overwrite":true,"Path":"c:\\Users\\laptop villa\\SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP\\js\\commerce.js"}}]}

(Processing)</button>\n          <button type=\"button\" class=\"btn ud-btn-outline btn-sm ud-mono\" data-sample-order=\"#UD-1088\">#UD-1088 (Delivered)</button>\n        </div>\n      </section>\n\n      <!-- Not Found Alert -->\n      <div id=\"trackingNotFound\" class=\"ud-card p-4 text-center d-none mb-4\" role=\"alert\">\n        <h2 class=\"h5 fw-bold mb-2\">Order ID Not Found</h2>\n '' : 'none';\n87:           if (matched) matchCount += 1;\n88:         });\n89: \n90:         // Reveal/hide section wrappers based on visible items\n91:         document.querySelectorAll('[data-faq-section]').forEach(function (section) {\n92:           const visibleInSection = Array.from(section.querySelectorAll('.ud-faq-item')).some(function (el) {\n93:             return el.style.display !== 'none';\n94:           });\n95:           section.style.display = visibleInSection ? '' : 'none';\n96:         });\n97: \n98:         const faqEmpty = document.getElementById('faqEmptyState');\n99:         if (faqEmpty) {\n100:           faqEmpty.classList.toggle('d-none', matchCount > 0);\n101:         }\n102:         showToast(matchCount + ' FAQ result(s) for \"' + query + '\".');\n103:         return;\n104:       }\n105: \n106:       // If on Store Locator page, match city or store name\n107:       const cityFilter = document.getElementById('cityFilter');\n108:       if (cityFilter) {\n109:         const lower = query.toLowerCase();\n110:         const cities = ['islamabad', 'lahore', 'karachi'];\n111:         const matchedCity = cities.find(function (c) {\n112:           return c.includes(lower) || lower.includes(c);\n113:         });\n114:         if (matchedCity) {\n115:           const properCity = matchedCity.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + matchedCity.slice(1);\n116:           cityFilter.value = properCity;\n117:           cityFilter.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));\n118:           showToast('Filtered stores for ' + properCity + '.');\n119:           return;\n120:         }"}

{"step_index":77,"source":"MODEL","type":"PLANNER_RESPONSE","status":"DONE","created_at":"2026-11-27T05:31:35Z","model":"gemini-4-hydrogen-high","input_tokens":2412,"cache_read_tokens":92098,"output_tokens":1017,"content":"Built the three new URBANDROPS e-commerce pages using Bootstrap 5, the shared dark streetwear palette (`#0D0D0D`, `#1A1A1A`, `#FFFFFF`, `#FF4D00`, `#CCFF00`), and vanilla JavaScript.\n\n### New & Updated Files\n- [pages/womens-catalog.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/womens-catalog.html) â€” Women's streetwear catalog rendered from the `WOMENS_CATALOG` JS data array, with keyword search filtering (synced with the navbar search input), category filter tags (`All`, `Hoodies & Sweats`, `Outerwear`, `Tees & Tops`, `Cargos & Bottoms`, `Accessories`), price sorting (`Low to High` / `High to Low`), per-card size chips, and `Add to Cart` actions updating `localStorage` (`urbandropsCart` and `cartCount`).\n- [pages/cart-checkout.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/cart-checkout.html) â€” Responsive two-column Cart & Checkout layout. The left column dynamically loads items from `localStorage` with `-`/`+` quantity controls, `Remove` buttons, and a sample-bag loader when empty. The right column features the `URBAN10` promo code field (10% discount), live `Subtotal` / `Shipping` / `Discount` / `Total` breakdown, shipping address form validation, and a `PLACE ORDER` button that records the order in `localStorage` and links straight to tracking.\n- [pages/order-tracking.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/order-tracking.html) â€” Order ID lookup (`#UD-1042` default plus `#UD-1019`, `#UD-1088`, and newly placed checkout orders) displaying a 4-step progress bar (`Order Placed` â†’ `Processing` â†’ `Shipped` â†’ `Delivered`) and status timestamp cards with location and courier notes.\n- [css/commerce.css](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/css/commerce.css) â€” Styling for product cards, size chips, cart rows, quantity controls, and the 4-step order tracking progress bar/timestamp cards (paired with [css/support.css](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/css/support.css)).\n- [js/commerce.js](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/js/commerce.js) â€” Vanilla JS powering the catalog rendering/filtering/sorting, cart persistence (`cartCount` + `urbandropsCart`), `URBAN10` promo math, checkout submission, and order tracking lookup.\n- Updated shared navbar & cart links across [pages/store-locator.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/store-locator.html), [pages/faq.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/faq.html), [pages/contact-support.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/contact-support.html), and [js/support.js](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/js/support.js) so every page includes `<img src=\"../images/Logo.png\">`, unified navigation (`Women's`, `Stores`, `Track Order`, `FAQ`, `Support`), search bar, `cart-checkout.html` cart button with `[data-cart-badge]`, and shared footer.\n\n### Verification\n- Verified with `Select-String` across all new and updated HTML, CSS, and JS files that Bootstrap 5 CDN includes, `../images/Logo.png`, `data-cart-badge` / `cartCount`, catalog filter/sort IDs, `URBAN10` promo & `PLACE ORDER` checkout IDs, `#UD-1042` 4-step tracking IDs, and cross-page navigation links are all present and aligned. (No headless browser or Node CLI runtime was executed in this environment.)"}

villa\\\\SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP\\\\css\\\\support.css\""}},{"name":"write_file","args":{"Content":"\"/**\\n * URBANDROPS â€” Shared Support Pages Vanilla JS\\n * Handles:\\n *  - Dynamic Cart Badge synced with localStorage ('cartCount')\\n *  - Shared Navbar Search bar behavior\\n *  - Store Locator filtering & dark map pin selection (pages/store-locator.html)\\n *  - FAQ Accordion [+] / [-] toggles & category filtering (pages/faq.html)\\n *  - Contact Support real-time email validation & ticket confirmation (pages/contact-support.html)\\n */\\n\\n(function () {\\n  'use strict';\\n\\n  /* ========================================================================\\n     1. SHARED CART BADGE (localStorage 'cartCount')\\n     ======================================================================== */\\n  function getCartCount() {\\n    try {\\n      const raw = localStorage.getItem('cartCount');\\n      const parsed = parseInt(raw, 10);\\n      return Number.isFinite(parsed) && parsed >= 0 ? parsed : 0;\\n    } catch (err) {\\n      return 0;\\n    }\\n  }\\n\\n  function renderCartBadge() {\\n    const badges = document.querySelectorAll('[data-cart-badge]');\\n    const count = getCartCount();\\n    badges.forEach(function (badge) {\\n      badge.textContent = String(count);\\n      badge.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Cart items: ' + count);\\n    });\\n  }\\n\\n  function initCartBadge() {\\n    renderCartBadge();\\n    window.addEventListener('storage', function (event) {\\n      if (event.key === 'cartCount') {\\n        renderCartBadge();\\n      }\\n    });\\n  }\\n\\n  /* ========================================================================\\n     2. SHARED NAVBAR SEARCH BAR\\n     ======================================================================== */\\n  function showToast(message) {\\n    const existing = document.getElementById('udSharedToast');\\n    if (existing) existing.remove();\\n\\n    const toast = document.createElement('div');\\n    toast.id = 'udSharedToast';\\n    toast.className = 'ud-toast';\\n    toast.setAttribute('role', 'status');\\n    toast.textContent = message;\\n    document.body.appendChild(toast);\\n\\n    window.setTimeout(functio\n<truncated 19912 bytes>","Overwrite":"true","Path":"\"c:\\\\Users\\\\laptop villa\\\\SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP\\\\js\\\\support.js\""}}],"truncated_fields":["tool_calls"]}

{"step_index":58,"source":"MODEL","type":"GENERIC","status":"DONE","created_at":"2026-11-27T05:30:49Z","content":"Created At: 2026-11-27T10:30:49+05:00\nCompleted At: 2026-11-27T10:30:50+05:00\nCreated At: 2026-11-27T10:30:49+05:00\nCompleted At: 2026-11-27T10:30:50+05:00\nShowing lines 55-120 of 574\n55:     toast.setAttribute('role', 'status');\n56:     toast.textContent = message;\n57:     document.body.appendChild(toast);\n58: \n59:     window.setTimeout(function () {\n60:       if (toast.parentNode) toast.remove();\n61:     }, 3500);\n62:   }\n63: \n64:   function initNavbarSearch() {\n65:     const searchForm = document.getElementById('udNavSearchForm');\n66:     const searchInput = document.getElementById('udNavSearchInput');\n67:     if (!searchForm || !searchInput) return;\n68: \n69:     searchForm.addEventListener('submit', function (event) {\n70:       event.preventDefault();\n71:       const query = searchInput.value.trim();\n72:       if (!query) {\n73:         searchInput.focus();\n74:         showToast('Enter a keyword to search URBANDROPS.');\n75:         return;\n76:       }\n77: \n78:       // If on FAQ page, filter FAQ items directly\n79:       const faqItems = document.querySelectorAll('.ud-faq-item');\n80:       if (faqItems.length > 0) {\n81:         const lower = query.toLowerCase();\n82:         let matchCount = 0;\n83:         faqItems.forEach(function (item) {\n84:           const text = item.textContent.toLowerCase();\n85:           const matched = text.includes(lower);\n86:           item.style.display = matched ? '' : 'none';\n87:           if (matched) matchCount += 1;\n88:         });\n89: \n90:         // Reveal/hide section wrappers based on visible items\n91:         document.querySelectorAll('[data-faq-section]').forEach(function (section) {\n92:           const visibleInSection = Array.from(section.querySelectorAll('.ud-faq-item')).some(function (el) {\n93:             return el.style.display !== 'none';\n94:           });\n95:           section.style.display = visibleInSection ? '' : 'none';\n96:         });\n97: \n98:         const faqEmpty = document.getElementById('faqEmptyState');\n99:         if (faqEmpty) {\n100:           faqEmpty.classList.toggle('d-none', matchCount > 0);\n101:         }\n102:         showToast(matchCount + ' FAQ result(s) for \"' + query + '\".');\n103:         return;\n104:       }\n105: \n106:       // If on Store Locator page, match city or store name\n107:       const cityFilter = document.getElementById('cityFilter');\n108:       if (cityFilter) {\n109:         const lower = query.toLowerCase();\n110:         const cities = ['islamabad', 'lahore', 'karachi'];\n111:         const matchedCity = cities.find(function (c) {\n112:           return c.includes(lower) || lower.includes(c);\n113:         });\n114:         if (matchedCity) {\n115:           const properCity = matchedCity.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + matchedCity.slice(1);\n116:           cityFilter.value = properCity;\n117:           cityFilter.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));\n118:           showToast('Filtered stores for ' + properCity + '.');\n119:           return;\n120:         }"}

{"step_index":77,"source":"MODEL","type":"PLANNER_RESPONSE","status":"DONE","created_at":"2026-11-27T05:31:35Z","model":"gemini-4-hydrogen-high","input_tokens":2412,"cache_read_tokens":92098,"output_tokens":1017,"content":"Built the three new URBANDROPS e-commerce pages using Bootstrap 5, the shared dark streetwear palette (`#0D0D0D`, `#1A1A1A`, `#FFFFFF`, `#FF4D00`, `#CCFF00`), and vanilla JavaScript.\n\n### New & Updated Files\n- [pages/womens-catalog.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/womens-catalog.html) â€” Women's streetwear catalog rendered from the `WOMENS_CATALOG` JS data array, with keyword search filtering (synced with the navbar search input), category filter tags (`All`, `Hoodies & Sweats`, `Outerwear`, `Tees & Tops`, `Cargos & Bottoms`, `Accessories`), price sorting (`Low to High` / `High to Low`), per-card size chips, and `Add to Cart` actions updating `localStorage` (`urbandropsCart` and `cartCount`).\n- [pages/cart-checkout.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/cart-checkout.html) â€” Responsive two-column Cart & Checkout layout. The left column dynamically loads items from `localStorage` with `-`/`+` quantity controls, `Remove` buttons, and a sample-bag loader when empty. The right column features the `URBAN10` promo code field (10% discount), live `Subtotal` / `Shipping` / `Discount` / `Total` breakdown, shipping address form validation, and a `PLACE ORDER` button that records the order in `localStorage` and links straight to tracking.\n- [pages/order-tracking.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_

## 00000001.jsonl

Group05_URBANDROP/pages/order-tracking.html) â€” Order ID lookup (`#UD-1042` default plus `#UD-1019`, `#UD-1088`, and newly placed checkout orders) displaying a 4-step progress bar (`Order Placed` â†’ `Processing` â†’ `Shipped` â†’ `Delivered`) and status timestamp cards with location and courier notes.\n- [css/commerce.css](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/css/commerce.css) â€” Styling for product cards, size chips, cart rows, quantity controls, and the 4-step order tracking progress bar/timestamp cards (paired with [css/support.css](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/css/support.css)).\n- [js/commerce.js](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/js/commerce.js) â€” Vanilla JS powering the catalog rendering/filtering/sorting, cart persistence (`cartCount` + `urbandropsCart`), `URBAN10` promo math, checkout submission, and order tracking lookup.\n- Updated shared navbar & cart links across [pages/store-locator.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/store-locator.html), [pages/faq.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/faq.html), [pages/contact-support.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/contact-support.html), and [js/support.js](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/js/support.js) so every page includes `<img src=\"../images/Logo.png\">`, unified navigation (`Women's`, `Stores`, `Track Order`, `FAQ`, `Support`), search bar, `cart-checkout.html` cart button with `[data-cart-badge]`, and shared footer.\n\n### Verification\n- Verified with `Select-String` across all new and updated HTML, CSS, and JS files that Bootstrap 5 CDN includes, `../images/Logo.png`, `data-cart-badge` / `cartCount`, catalog filter/sort IDs, `URBAN10` promo & `PLACE ORDER` checkout IDs, `#UD-1042` 4-step tracking IDs, and cross-page navigation links are all present and aligned. (No headless browser or Node CLI runtime was executed in this environment.)"}

Add real-time JS email format validation and a submit button displaying a confirmation alert.\n6. High-contrast color tokens establish the required streetwear aesthetic, balancing dark backgrounds with sharp accent highlights.\n\nNavbar markup structure integrates fallback badges for missing brand logos alongside active route assignments and responsive search inputs. Filtering logic for regional locations requires dynamic city selection parameters bound to interactive store cards.\n\nInteractive store cards require precise grid distribution paired with custom vector map overlays to guarantee offline-first performance. --ud-font-body: 'Inter', system-ui, -apple-system, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, sans-serif;\n  --ud-font-mono: 'JetBrains Mono', ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, Monaco, Consolas, monospace;\n}\n\n*,\n*::before,\n*::after {\n  box-sizing: border-box;\n}\n\nhtml {\n  scroll-behavior: smooth;\n}\n\nbody {\n  background-color: var(--ud-bg);\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-body);\n  min-height: 100vh;\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  overflow-x: hidden;\n  -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;\n}\n\nmain {\n  flex: 1 0 auto;\n}\n\nh1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6,\n.ud-display {\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-display);\n  letter-spacing: -0.02em;\n}\n\n.ud-mono {\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n}\n\n/* ---------- Selection & Scrollbar ---------- */\n::selection {\n  background-color: var(--ud-accent);\n  color: var(--ud-bg);\n}\n\n/* ---------- Top Announcement Strip ---------- */\n.ud-ticker {\n  background-color: var(--ud-accent);\n  color: var(--ud-bg);\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.75rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  letter-spacing: 0.08em;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  padding: 0.4rem 0;\n}\n\n/* ---------- Shared Navbar ---------- */\n.ud-navbar {\n  background-color: rgba(13, 13, 13, 0.94);\n  backdrop-filter: blur(12px);\n  -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(12px);\n  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  padding-top: 0.85rem;\n  padding-bottom: 0.85rem;\n}\n\n.ud-brand {\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 0.65rem;\n  text-decoration: none;\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n}\n\n.ud-brand-logo {\n  height: 36px;\n  width: auto;\n  max-width: 140px;\n  object-fit: contain;\n  display: block;\n}\n\n.ud-brand-fallback {\n  display: none;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: center;\n  width: 34px;\n  height: 34px;\n  background-color: var(--ud-cta);\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-display);\n  font-weight: 800;\n  font-size: 0.95rem;\n  letter-spacing: 0.04em;\n  border-radius: 4px;\n}\n\n.ud-brand-logo.is-missing + .ud-brand-fallback {\n  display: inline-flex;\n}\n\n.ud-brand-text {\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-display);\n  font-weight: 800;\n  font-size: 1.2rem;\n  letter-spacing: 0.08em;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n}\n\n.ud-navbar .nav-link {\n  color: var(--ud-text-muted);\n  font-size: 0.875rem;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.06em;\n  padding: 0.5rem 0.85rem;\n  transition: color 0.2s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-navbar .nav-link:hover,\n.ud-navbar .nav-link:focus-visible {\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n}\n\n.ud-navbar .nav-link.active {\n  color: var(--ud-accent);\n}\n\n.ud-navbar .navbar-toggler {\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n  padding: 0.45rem 0.65rem;\n}\n\n.ud-navbar .navbar-toggler:focus {\n  box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px rgba(204, 255, 0, 0.35);\n}\n\n.ud-navbar-toggler-icon {\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: center;\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n}\n\n/* Search Bar */\n.ud-search-form {\n  position: relative;\n  min-width: 210px;\n}\n\n.ud-search-input {\n  background-color: var(--ud-surface);\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n  font-size: 0.875rem;\n  padding: 0.5rem 2.6rem 0.5rem 0.9rem;\n  border-radius: 6px;\n  transition: border-color 0.2s ease, box-shadow 0.2s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-search-input::placeholder {\n  color: var(--ud-text-muted);\n}\n\n.ud-search-input:focus {\n  background-color: var(--ud-surface);\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n  border-color: var(--ud-accent);\n  box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px rgba(204, 255, 0, 0.18);\n  outline: none;\n}\n\n.ud-search-btn {\n  position: absolute;\n  right: 4px;\n  top: 50%;\n  transform: translateY(-50%);\n  background: transparent;\n  border: none;\n  color: var(--ud-text-muted);\n  padding: 0.35rem 0.5rem;\n  border-radius: 4px;\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: center;\n  transition: color 0.2s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-search-btn:hover {\n  color: var(--ud-accent);\n}\n\n/* Cart Button & Badge */\n.ud-cart-btn {\n  position: relative;\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 0.5rem;\n  background-color: var(--ud-surface);\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.8rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  padding: 0.5rem 0.85rem;\n  border-radius: 6px;\n  text-decoration: none;\n  transition: border-color 0.2s ease, transform 0.15s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-cart-btn:hover {\n  border-color: var(--ud-cta);\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n}\n\n.ud-cart-badge {\n  background-color: var(--ud-accent);\n  color: var(--ud-bg);\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.72rem;\n  font-weight: 800;\n  min-width: 22px;\n  height: 22px;\n  padding: 0 6px;\n  border-radius: 999px;\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: center;\n}\n\n/* ---------- Page Hero Header ---------- */\n.ud-page-hero {\n  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  background:\n    radial-gradient(circle at 88% 18%, rgba(255, 77, 0, 0.14), transparent 45%),\n    radial-gradient(circle at 12% 85%, rgba(204, 255, 0, 0.08), transparent 40%),\n    var(--ud-bg);\n  padding: 3.25rem 0 2.75rem;\n}\n\n.ud-eyebrow {\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 0.5rem;\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.75rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.12em;\n  color: var(--ud-accent);\n  margin-bottom: 0.75rem;\n}\n\n.ud-eyebrow::before {\n  content: \"\";\n  display: inline-block;\n  width: 8px;\n  height: 8px;\n  background-color: var(--ud-accent);\n  border-radius: 2px;\n}\n\n.ud-hero-title {\n  font-size: clamp(2rem, 4vw, 3.25rem);\n  font-weight: 800;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  line-height: 1.05;\n  margin-bottom: 0.85rem;\n}\n\n.ud-hero-subtitle {\n  color: var(--ud-text-muted);\n  font-size: 1.02rem;\n  max-width: 640px;\n  margin-bottom: 0;\n}\n\n/* ---------- Cards & Controls ---------- */\n.ud-card {\n  background-color: var(--ud-surface);\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  border-radius: 10px;\n  transition: border-color 0.2s ease, transform 0.2s ease, box-shadow 0.2s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-card-interactive:hover {\n  border-color: var(--ud-border-hover);\n  transform: translateY(-2px);\n}\n\n.ud-card.is-selected {\n  border-color: var(--ud-accent);\n  box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px rgba(204, 255, 0, 0.35);\n}\n\n.ud-btn-cta {\n  background-color: var(--ud-cta);\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-cta);\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-display);\n  font-weight: 700;\n  font-size: 0.875rem;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.06em;\n  padding: 0.65rem 1.25rem;\n  border-radius: 6px;\n  transition: background-color 0.2s ease, border-color 0.2s ease, transform 0.15s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-btn-cta:hover,\n.ud-btn-cta:focus-visible {\n  background-color: var(--ud-cta-hover);\n  border-color: var(--ud-cta-hover);\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n}\n\n.ud-btn-cta:active {\n  transform: translateY(1px);\n}\n\n.ud-btn-outline {\n  background-color: transparent;\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-display);\n  font-weight: 600;\n  font-size: 0.85rem;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.05em;\n  padding: 0.55rem 1rem;\n  border-radius: 6px;\n  transition: border-color 0.2s ease, color 0.2s ease, background-color 0.2s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-btn-outline:hover,\n.ud-btn-outline.active {\n  border-color: var(--ud-accent);\n  color: var(--ud-accent);\n  background-color: rgba(204, 255, 0, 0.06);\n}\n\n.ud-form-label {\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.78rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.08em;\n  color: var(--ud-text-muted);\n  margin-bottom: 0.45rem;\n}\n\n.ud-form-control,\n.ud-form-select {\n  background-color: var(--ud-bg);\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n  border-radius: 6px;\n  padding: 0.72rem 0.95rem;\n  font-size: 0.95rem;\n  transition: border-color 0.2s ease, box-shadow 0.2s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-form-control::placeholder {\n  color: #6B6B6B;\n}\n\n.ud-form-control:focus,\n.ud-form-select:focus {\n  background-color: var(--ud-bg);\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n  border-color: var(--ud-accent);\n  box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(204, 255, 0, 0.14);\n}\n\n.ud-form-select option {\n  background-color: var(--ud-surface);\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n}\n\n.ud-form-control.is-valid-ud {\n  border-color: var(--ud-accent);\n}\n\n.ud-form-control.is-invalid-ud,\n.ud-form-select.is-invalid-ud {\n  border-color: var(--ud-cta);\n}\n\n.ud-validation-msg {\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.76rem;\n  margin-top: 0.4rem;\n  min-height: 1.1rem;\n}\n\n.ud-validation-msg.is-valid {\n  color: var(--ud-accent);\n}\n\n.ud-validation-msg.is-invalid {\n  color: var(--ud-cta);\n}\n\n/* ---------- Store Locator Specific ---------- */\n.ud-status-pill {\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 0.4rem;\n  background-color: rgba(204, 255, 0, 0.12);\n  border: 1px solid rgba(204, 255, 0, 0.4);\n  color: var(--ud-accent);\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.72rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  letter-spacing: 0.08em;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  padding: 0.25rem 0.6rem;\n  border-radius: 999px;\n}\n\n.ud-status-dot {\n  width: 7px;\n  height: 7px;\n  border-radius: 50%;\n  background-color: var(--ud-accent);\n  box-shadow: 0 0 8px var(--ud-accent);\n}\n\n.ud-city-badge {\n  display: inline-block;\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.72rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.08em;\n  color: var(--ud-text-muted);\n  background-color: var(--ud-bg);\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  padding: 0.22rem 0.55rem;\n  border-radius: 4px;\n}\n\n.ud-map-card {\n  position: sticky;\n  top: 5.5rem;\n  overflow: hidden;\n}\n\n.ud-map-canvas {\n  position: relative;\n  height: 360px;\n  background-color: #101010;\n  background-image:\n    linear-gradient(to right, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04) 1px, transparent 1px),\n    linear-gradient(to bottom, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04) 1px, transparent 1px);\n  background-size: 28px 28px;\n  border-top: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  overflow: hidden;\n}\n\n.ud-map-radar {\n  position: absolute;\n  inset: 0;\n  background: radial-gradient(circle at 50% 50%, rgba(204, 255, 0, 0.08), transparent 65%);\n  pointer-events: none;\n}\n\n.ud-map-grid-label {\n  position: absolute;\n  top: 0.75rem;\n  left: 0.9rem;\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.7rem;\n  color: var(--ud-text-muted);\n  letter-spacing: 0.08em;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  background: rgba(13, 13, 13, 0.85);\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  padding: 0.25rem 0.55rem;\n  border-radius: 4px;\n  z-index: 2;\n}\n\n.ud-map-pin {\n  position: absolute;\n  transform: translate(-50%, -50%);\n  background-color: var(--ud-surface);\n  border: 2px solid var(--ud-cta);\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.72rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  padding: 0.28rem 0.6rem;\n  border-radius: 999px;\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 0.35rem;\n  cursor: pointer;\n  z-index: 3;\n  transition: transform 0.2s ease, background-color 0.2s ease, border-color 0.2s ease, color 0.2s ease;\n  box-shadow: 0 6px 16px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.55);\n}\n\n.ud-map-pin:hover,\n.ud-map-pin.is-active {\n  background-color: var(--ud-accent);\n  border-color: var(--ud-accent);\n  color: var(--ud-bg);\n  transform: translate(-50%, -50%) scale(1.08);\n  z-index: 4;\n}\n\n.ud-map-pin-dot {\n  width: 7px;\n  height: 7px;\n  border-radius: 50%;\n  background-color: currentColor;\n}\n\n/* ---------- FAQ Accordion Specific ---------- */\n.ud-faq-section-header {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  padding-bottom: 0.75rem;\n  margin-bottom: 1rem;\n}\n\n.ud-faq-item {\n  background-color: var(--ud-surface);\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  border-radius: 8px;\n  margin-bottom: 0.75rem;\n  overflow: hidden;\n  transition: border-color 0.2s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-faq-item.is-open {\n  border-color: var(--ud-accent);\n}\n\n.ud-faq-trigger {\n  width: 100%;\n  background: transparent;\n  border: none;\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n  padding: 1.15rem 1.25rem;\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  gap: 1rem;\n  text-align: left;\n  cursor: pointer;\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-display);\n  font-size: 1.02rem;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  transition: color 0.2s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-faq-trigger:hover {\n  color: var(--ud-accent);\n}\n\n.ud-faq-toggle-icon {\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.95rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  color: var(--ud-accent);\n  flex-shrink: 0;\n  transition: transform 0.2s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-faq-panel {\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-rows: 0fr;\n  transition: grid-template-rows 0.28s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-faq-item.is-open .ud-faq-panel {\n  grid-template-rows: 1fr;\n}\n\n.ud-faq-panel-inner {\n  overflow: hidden;\n}\n\n.ud-faq-answer {\n  padding: 0 1.25rem 1.25rem;\n  color: var(--ud-text-muted);\n  font-size: 0.95rem;\n  line-height: 1.65;\n  border-top: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);\n  padding-top: 0.95rem;\n}\n\n/* ---------- Confirmation Alert ---------- */\n.ud-alert-success {\n  background-color: rgba(204, 255, 0, 0.1);\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-accent);\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n  border-radius: 8px;\n  padding: 1.15rem 1.25rem;\n}\n\n.ud-alert-success .ud-alert-title {\n  color: var(--ud-accent);\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-display);\n  font-weight: 700;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.04em;\n}\n\n/* ---------- Shared Footer ---------- */\n.ud-footer {\n  background-color: #080808;\n  border-top: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  padding: 3.25rem 0 2rem;\n  margin-top: 4rem;\n}\n\n.ud-footer-heading {\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.78rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.1em;\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n  margin-bottom: 1rem;\n}\n\n.ud-footer-link {\n  display: inline-block;\n  color: var(--ud-text-muted);\n  text-decoration: none;\n  font-size: 0.9rem;\n  margin-bottom: 0.5rem;\n  transition: color 0.2s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-footer-link:hover {\n  color: var(--ud-accent);\n}\n\n.ud-footer-bottom {\n  border-top: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  margin-top: 2.25rem;\n  padding-top: 1.25rem;\n  font-size: 0.82rem;\n  color: var(--ud-text-muted);\n}\n\n/* ---------- Toast Notification ---------- */\n.ud-toast {\n  position: fixed;\n  bottom: 1.25rem;\n  right: 1.25rem;\n  z-index: 1080;\n  background-color: var(--ud-surface);\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-accent);\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n  padding: 0.8rem 1.1rem;\n  border-radius: 8px;\n  font-size: 0.875rem;\n  box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.65);\n  max-width: 340px;\n}\n\n@media (max-width: 991.98px) {\n  .ud-map-card {\n    position: static;\n  }\n\n  .ud-search-form {\n    width: 100%;\n    margin-top: 0.75rem;\n    margin-bottom: 0.75rem;\n  }\n}\n","Overwrite":true,"Path":"c:\\Users\\laptop villa\\SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP\\css\\support.css"}},{"name":"write_file","args":{"Content":"/**\n * URBANDROPS â€” Shared Support Pages Vanilla JS\n * Handles:\n *  - Dynamic Cart Badge synced with localStorage ('cartCount')\n *  - Shared Navbar Search bar behavior\n *  - Store Locator filtering & dark map pin selection (pages/store-locator.html)\n *  - FAQ Accordion [+] / [-] toggles & category filtering (pages/faq.html)\n *  - Contact Support real-time email validation & ticket confirmation (pages/contact-support.html)\n */\n\n(function () {\n  'use strict';\n\n  /* ========================================================================\n     1. SHARED CART BADGE (localStorage 'cartCount')\n     ======================================================================== */\n  function getCartCount() {\n    try {\n      const raw = localStorage.getItem('cartCount');\n      const parsed = parseInt(raw, 10);\n      return Number.isFinite(parsed) && parsed >= 0 ? parsed : 0;\n    } catch (err) {\n      return 0;\n    }\n  }\n\n  function renderCartBadge() {\n    const badges = document.querySelectorAll('[data-cart-badge]');\n    const count = getCartCount();\n    badges.forEach(function (badge) {\n      badge.textContent = String(count);\n      badge.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Cart items: ' + count);\n    });\n  }\n\n  function initCartBadge() {\n    renderCartBadge();\n    window.addEventListener('storage', function (event) {\n      if (event.key === 'cartCount') {\n        renderCartBadge();\n      }\n    });\n  }\n\n  /* ========================================================================\n     2. SHARED NAVBAR SEARCH BAR\n     ======================================================================== */\n  function showToast(message) {\n    const existing = document.getElementById('udSharedToast');\n    if (existing) existing.remove();\n\n    const toast = document.createElement('div');\n    toast.id = 'udSharedToast';\n    toast.className = 'ud-toast';\n    toast.setAttribute('role', 'status');\n    toast.textContent = message;\n    document.body.appendChild(toast);\n\n    window.setTimeout(function () {\n      if (toast.parentNode) toast.remove();\n    }, 3500);\n  }\n\n  function initNavbarSearch() {\n    const searchForm = document.getElementById('udNavSearchForm');\n    const searchInput = document.getElementById('udNavSearchInput');\n    if (!searchForm || !searchInput) return;\n\n    searchForm.addEventListener('submit', function (event) {\n      event.preventDefault();\n      const query = searchInput.value.trim();\n      if (!query) {\n        searchInput.focus();\n        showToast('Enter a keyword to search URBANDROPS.');\n        return;\n      }\n\n      // If on FAQ page, filter FAQ items directly\n      const faqItems = document.querySelectorAll('.ud-faq-item');\n      if (faqItems.length > 0) {\n        const lower = query.toLowerCase();\n        let matchCount = 0;\n        faqItems.forEach(function (item) {\n          const text = item.textContent.toLowerCase();\n          const matched = text.includes(lower);\n          item.style.display = matched ? '' : 'none';\n          if (matched) matchCount += 1;\n        });\n\n        // Reveal/hide section wrappers based on visible items\n        document.querySelectorAll('[data-faq-section]').forEach(function (section) {\n          const visibleInSection = Array.from(section.querySelectorAll('.ud-faq-item')).some(function (el) {\n            return el.style.display !== 'none';\n          });\n          section.style.display = visibleInSection ? '' : 'none';\n        });\n\n        const faqEmpty = document.getElementById('faqEmptyState');\n        if (faqEmpty) {\n          faqEmpty.classList.toggle('d-none', matchCount > 0);\n        }\n        showToast(matchCount + ' FAQ result(s) for \"' + query + '\".');\n        return;\n      }\n\n      // If on Store Locator page, match city or store name\n      const cityFilter = document.getElementById('cityFilter');\n      if (cityFilter) {\n        const lower = query.toLowerCase();\n        const cities = ['islamabad', 'lahore', 'karachi'];\n        const matchedCity = cities.find(function (c) {\n          return c.includes(lower) || lower.includes(c);\n        });\n        if (matchedCity) {\n          const properCity = matchedCity.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + matchedCity.slice(1);\n          cityFilter.value = properCity;\n          cityFilter.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));\n          showToast('Filtered stores for ' + properCity + '.');\n          return;\n        }\n      }\n\n      showToast('Searching catalog for \"' + query + '\"...');\n    });\n  }\n\n  /* ========================================================================\n     3. STORE LOCATOR PAGE (pages/store-locator.html)\n     ======================================================================== */\n  const STORES = [\n    {\n      id: 'isb-f7',\n      city: 'Islamabad',\n      name: 'URBANDROPS // F-7 JINNAH SUPER',\n      badge: 'Flagship Store',\n      address: 'Shop 14, Block 13-L, Jinnah Super Market, F-7 Markaz, Islamabad',\n      hours: 'Mon â€“ Sun: 11:00 AM â€“ 10:30 PM',\n      phone: '+92 51 2654109',\n      status: 'OPEN NOW',\n      coords: '33.7215Â° N, 73.0561Â° E',\n      pinX: 58,\n      pinY: 22\n    },\n    {\n      id: 'isb-centaurus',\n      city: 'Islamabad',\n      name: 'URBANDROPS // CENTAURUS DROP LAB',\n      badge: 'Concept Studio',\n      address: 'Level 2, Unit 208, The Centaurus Mall, Jinnah Ave, F-8/4, Islamabad',\n      hours: 'Mon â€“ Sun: 11:00 AM â€“ 11:00 PM',\n      phone: '+92 51 2701822',\n      status: 'OPEN NOW',\n      coords: '33.7077Â° N, 73.0501Â° E',\n      pinX: 66,\n      pinY: 30\n    },\n    {\n      id: 'lhr-gulberg',\n      city: 'Lahore',\n      name: 'URBANDROPS // MM ALAM GULBERG',\n      badge: 'Flagship Store',\n      address: '42-B, MM Alam Road, Block C-2, Gulberg III, Lahore',\n      hours: 'Mon â€“ Sun: 12:00 PM â€“ 11:00 PM',\n      phone: '+92 42 35784910',\n      status: 'OPEN NOW',\n      coords: '31.5122Â° N, 74.3514Â° E',\n      pinX: 72,\n      pinY: 48\n    },\n    {\n      id: 'lhr-dha',\n      city: 'Lahore',\n      name: 'URBANDROPS // DHA PHASE 5',\n      badge: 'Archive & Drops',\n      address: 'Plaza 18, Sector CCA, DHA Phase 5, Lahore',\n
