  id: 'khi-clifton',\n      city: 'Karachi',\n      name: 'URBANDROPS // CLIFTON BLOCK 4',\n      badge: 'Flagship Store',\n      address: 'Plot BC-9, Marine Promenade, Block 4 Clifton, Karachi',\n      hours: 'Mon â€“ Sun: 12:00 PM â€“ 11:30 PM',\n      phone: '+92 21 35871140',\n      status: 'OPEN NOW',\n      coords: '24.8138Â° N, 67.0299Â° E',\n      pinX: 25,\n      pinY: 78\n    },\n    {\n      id: 'khi-dha',\n      city: 'Karachi',\n      name: 'URBANDROPS // DHA BUKHARI COMMERCIAL',\n      badge: 'Streetwear Vault',\n      address: '17-C, Lane 5, Bukhari Commercial, DHA Phase 6, Karachi',\n      hours: 'Tue â€“ Sun: 1:00 PM â€“ 11:00 PM',\n      phone: '+92 21 35249081',\n      status: 'OPEN NOW',\n      coords: '24.7937Â° N, 67.0645Â° E',\n      pinX: 34,\n      pinY: 84\n    }\n  ];\n\n  function initStoreLocator() {\n    const cityFilter = document.getElementById('cityFilter');\n    const storeCardsContainer = document.getElementById('storeCardsContainer');\n    const mapPinsContainer = document.getElementById('mapPinsContainer');\n    const storeCountLabel = document.getElementById('storeCountLabel');\n    const resetCityBtn = document.getElementById('resetCityFilter');\n\n    if (!cityFilter || !storeCardsContainer || !mapPinsContainer) return;\n\n    let selectedStoreId = STORES[0].id;\n\n    function updateMapOverlay(store) {\n      const titleEl = document.getElementById('mapActiveName');\n      const cityEl = document.getElementById('mapActiveCity');\n      const addressEl = document.getElementById('mapActiveAddress');\n      const hoursEl = document.getElementById('mapActiveHours');\n      const coordsEl = document.getElementById('mapActiveCoords');\n      const directionsEl = document.getElementById('mapDirectionsBtn');\n\n      if (!store) return;\n      if (titleEl) titleEl.textContent = store.name;\n      if (cityEl) cityEl.textContent = store.city + ' â€¢ ' + store.badge;\n      if (addressEl) addressEl.textContent = store.address;\n      if (hoursEl) hoursEl.textContent = store.hours;\n      if (coordsEl) coordsEl.textContent = store.coords;\n      if (directionsEl) {\n        const query = encodeURIComponent(store.address);\n        directionsEl.href = 'https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=' + query;\n      }\n    }\n\n    function selectStore(storeId) {\n      selectedStoreId = storeId;\n      const store = STORES.find(function (s) {\n        return s.id === storeId;\n      });\n      updateMapOverlay(store);\n\n      storeCardsContainer.querySelectorAll('[data-store-id]').forEach(function (card) {\n        card.classList.toggle('is-selected', card.getAttribute('data-store-id') === storeId);\n      });\n\n      mapPinsContainer.querySelectorAll('[data-pin-id]').forEach(function (pin) {\n        pin.classList.toggle('is-active', pin.getAttribute('data-pin-id') === storeId);\n      });\n    }\n\n    function renderStores() {\n      const selectedCity = cityFilter.value;\n      const filtered = selectedCity === 'all'\n        ? STORES\n        : STORES.filter(function (s) {\n            return s.city === selectedCity;\n          });\n\n      if (storeCountLabel) {\n        storeCountLabel.textContent = 'Showing ' + filtered.length + ' ' + (filtered.length === 1 ? 'Location' : 'Locations');\n      }\n\n      if (resetCityBtn) {\n        resetCityBtn.classList.toggle('d-none', selectedCity === 'all');\n      }\n\n      if (!filtered.some(function (s) { return s.id === selectedStoreId; })) {\n        selectedStoreId = filtered.length > 0 ? filtered[0].id : null;\n      }\n\n      // Render Store Cards\n      storeCardsContainer.innerHTML = '';\n      filtered.forEach(function (store) {\n        const col = document.createElement('div');\n        col.className = 'col-12 col-md-6 col-lg-12';\n\n        const isSelected = store.id === selectedStoreId;\n        col.innerHTML =\n          '<article class=\"ud-card ud-card-interactive p-4 h-100 ' + (isSelected ? 'is-selected' : '') + '\" data-store-id=\"' + store.id + '\">' +\n            '<div class=\"d-flex flex-wrap justify-content-between align-items-start gap-2 mb-3\">' +\n              '<div>' +\n                '<span class=\"ud-city-badge me-2\">' + store.city + '</span>' +\n                '<span class=\"ud-mono small text-secondary\">' + store.badge + '</span>' +\n              '</div>' +\n              '<span class=\"ud-status-pill\">' +\n                '<span class=\"ud-status-dot\" aria-hidden=\"true\"></span>' +\n                store.status +\n              '</span>' +\n            '</div>' +\n            '<h2 class=\"h5 fw-bold mb-2\">' + store.name + '</h2>' +\n            '<p class=\"text-secondary small mb-3\">' + store.address + '</p>' +\n            '<div class=\"d-flex flex-column gap-1 mb-3 small\">' +\n              '<div><span class=\"ud-mono text-secondary\">HOURS:</span> <span class=\"text-white\">' + store.hours + '</span></div>' +\n              '<div><span class=\"ud-mono text-secondary\">PHONE:</span> <span class=\"text-white\">' + store.phone + '</span></div>' +\n            '</div>' +\n            '<div class=\"d-flex flex-wrap gap-2\">' +\n              '<button type=\"button\" class=\"btn ud-btn-cta btn-sm\" data-focus-store=\"' + store.id + '\">Focus on Map</button>' +\n              '<a href=\"contact-support.html\" class=\"btn ud-btn-outline btn-sm\">Store Inquiry</a>' +\n            '</div>' +\n          '</article>';\n\n        storeCardsContainer.appendChild(col);\n      });\n\n      // Render Map Pins\n      mapPinsContainer.innerHTML = '';\n      filtered.forEach(function (store) {\n        const pin = document.createElement('button');\n        pin.type = 'button';\n        pin.className = 'ud-map-pin' + (store.id === selectedStoreId ? ' is-active' : '');\n        pin.style.left = store.pinX + '%';\n        pin.style.top = store.pinY + '%';\n        pin.setAttribute('data-pin-id', store.id);\n        pin.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Select ' + store.name + ' in ' + store.city);\n        pin.innerHTML = '<span class=\"ud-map-pin-dot\" aria-hidden=\"true\"></span><span>' + store.city.slice(0, 3).toUpperCase() + '</span>';\n\n        pin.addEventListener('click', function () {\n          selectStore(store.id);\n        });\n        mapPinsContainer.appendChild(pin);\n      });\n\n      // Bind focus buttons on cards\n      storeCardsContainer.querySelectorAll('[data-focus-store]').forEach(function (btn) {\n        btn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n          selectStore(btn.getAttribute('data-focus-store'));\n        });\n      });\n\n      const activeStore = filtered.find(function (s) { return s.id === selectedStoreId; });\n      updateMapOverlay(activeStore);\n    }\n\n    cityFilter.addEventListener('change', renderStores);\n\n    if (resetCityBtn) {\n      resetCityBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n        cityFilter.value = 'all';\n        renderStores();\n      });\n    }\n\n    renderStores();\n  }\n\n  /* ========================================================================\n     4. FAQ ACCORDION PAGE (pages/faq.html)\n     ======================================================================== */\n  function initFaqAccordion() {\n    const triggers = document.querySelectorAll('.ud-faq-trigger');\n    if (triggers.length === 0) return;\n\n    function setFaqState(item, open) {\n      const trigger = item.querySelector('.ud-faq-trigger');\n      const icon = item.querySelector('.ud-faq-toggle-icon');\n      item.classList.toggle('is-open', open);\n      if (trigger) {\n        trigger.setAttribute('aria-expanded', open ? 'true' : 'false');\n      }\n      if (icon) {\n        icon.textContent = open ? '[-]' : '[+]';\n      }\n    }\n\n    triggers.forEach(function (trigger) {\n      trigger.addEventListener('click', function () {\n        const item = trigger.closest('.ud-faq-item');\n        if (!item) return;\n        const currentlyOpen = item.classList.contains('is-open');\n        setFaqState(item, !currentlyOpen);\n      });\n    });\n\n    // Category filter buttons (All / Shipping / Returns / Sizing)\n    const categoryButtons = document.querySelectorAll('[data-faq-filter]');\n    const sections = document.querySelectorAll('[data-faq-section]');\n    const resetFaqSearchBtn = document.getElementById('resetFaqSearchBtn');\n\n    categoryButtons.forEach(function (btn) {\n      btn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n        const target = btn.getAttribute('data-faq-filter');\n\n        categoryButtons.forEach(function (b) {\n          b.classList.toggle('active', b === btn);\n        });\n\n        // Clear any search-filtered hidden items first\n        document.querySelectorAll('.ud-faq-item').forEach(function (item) {\n          item.style.display = '';\n        });\n\n        sections.forEach(function (section) {\n          const sectionKey = section.getAttribute('data-faq-section');\n          section.style.display = (target === 'all' || target === sectionKey) ? '' : 'none';\n        });\n\n        const faqEmpty = document.getElementById('faqEmptyState');\n        if (faqEmpty) faqEmpty.classList.add('d-none');\n      });\n    });\n\n    if (resetFaqSearchBtn) {\n      resetFaqSearchBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n        const searchInput = document.getElementById('udNavSearchInput');\n        if (searchInput) searchInput.value = '';\n        document.querySelectorAll('.ud-faq-item').forEach(function (item) {\n          item.style.display = '';\n        });\n        sections.forEach(function (section) {\n          section.style.display = '';\n        });\n        const faqEmpty = document.getElementById('faqEmptyState');\n        if (faqEmpty) faqEmpty.classList.add('d-none');\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n  /* ========================================================================\n     5. CONTACT SUPPORT PAGE (pages/contact-support.html)\n     ======================================================================== */\n  function initContactSupport() {\n    const form = document.getElementById('supportTicketForm');\n    if (!form) return;\n\n    const nameInput = document.getElementById('supportName');\n    const emailInput = document.getElementById('supportEmail');\n    const subjectSelect = document.getElementById('supportSubject');\n    const messageInput = document.getElementById('supportMessage');\n\n    const nameFeedback = document.getElementById('nameFeedback');\n    const emailFeedback = document.getElementById('emailFeedback');\n    const subjectFeedback = document.getElementById('subjectFeedback');\n    const messageFeedback = document.getElementById('messageFeedback');\n\n    const alertContainer = document.getElementById('supportConfirmationAlert');\n    const ticketIdSpan = document.getElementById('confirmedTicketId');\n    const ticketSummarySpan = document.getElementById('confirmedTicketSummary');\n    const charCounter = document.getElementById('messageCharCount');\n\n    const EMAIL_REGEX = /^[^\\s@]+@[^\\s@]+\\.[^\\s@]{2,}$/;\n\n    function setFieldFeedback(input, feedbackEl, isValid, message) {\n      input.classList.remove('is-valid-ud', 'is-invalid-ud');\n      feedbackEl.classList.remove('is-valid', 'is-invalid');\n\n      if (message === '') {\n        feedbackEl.textContent = '';\n        return;\n      }\n\n      if (isValid) {\n        input.classList.add('is-valid-ud');\n        feedbackEl.classList.add('is-valid');\n      } else {\n        input.classList.add('is-invalid-ud');\n        feedbackEl.classList.add('is-invalid');\n      }\n      feedbackEl.textContent = message;\n    }\n\n    function validateEmailRealtime() {\n      const val = emailInput.value.trim();\n      if (val.length === 0) {\n        setFieldFeedback(emailInput, emailFeedback, false, 'Email address is required.');\n        return false;\n      }\n      if (!EMAIL_REGEX.test(val)) {\n        setFieldFeedback(emailInput, emailFeedback, false, 'Enter a valid email format (e.g., alex@domain.com).');\n        return false;\n      }\n      setFieldFeedback(emailInput, emailFeedback, true, 'Valid email format.');\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    function validateName() {\n      const val = nameInput.value.trim();\n      if (val.length < 2) {\n        setFieldFeedback(nameInput, nameFeedback, false, 'Please enter your full name (at least 2 characters).');\n        return false;\n      }\n      setFieldFeedback(nameInput, nameFeedback, true, '');\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    function validateSubject() {\n      if (!subjectSelect.value) {\n        setFieldFeedback(subjectSelect, subjectFeedback, false, 'Please choose a support subject.');\n        return false;\n      }\n      setFieldFeedback(subjectSelect, subjectFeedback, true, '');\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    function validateMessage() {\n      const val = messageInput.value.trim();\n      if (val.length < 15) {\n        setFieldFeedback(messageInput, messageFeedback, false, 'Message must be at least 15 characters.');\n        return false;\n      }\n      setFieldFeedback(messageInput, messageFeedback, true, '');\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    // Real-time email validation as the user types\n    emailInput.addEventListener('input', validateEmailRealtime);\n    emailInput.addEventListener('blur', validateEmailRealtime);\n\n    nameInput.addEventListener('blur', validateName);\n    subjectSelect.addEventListener('change', validateSubject);\n    messageInput.addEventListener('input', function () {\n      if (charCounter) {\n        charCounter.textContent = messageInput.value.length + ' / 1000';\n      }\n      if (messageInput.classList.contains('is-invalid-ud')) {\n        validateMessage();\n      }\n    });\n\n    form.addEventListener('submit', function (event) {\n      event.preventDefault();\n\n      const isNameOk = validateName();\n      const isEmailOk = validateEmailRealtime();\n      const isSubjectOk = validateSubject();\n      const isMessageOk = validateMessage();\n\n      if (!isNameOk || !isEmailOk || !isSubjectOk || !isMessageOk) {\n        if (alertContainer) alertContainer.classList.add('d-none');\n        return;\n      }\n\n      const ticketCode = 'UD-' + Math.floor(10000 + Math.random() * 90000);\n      if (ticketIdSpan) ticketIdSpan.textContent = '#' + ticketCode;\n      if (ticketSummarySpan) {\n        ticketSummarySpan.textContent =\n          'Ticket logged for \"' + subjectSelect.value + '\". A confirmation has been routed to ' + emailInput.value.trim() + '.';\n      }\n\n      if (alertContainer) {\n        alertContainer.classList.remove('d-none');\n        alertContainer.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'nearest' });\n      }\n\n      form.reset();\n      if (charCounter) charCounter.textContent = '0 / 1000';\n\n      [nameInput, emailInput, subjectSelect, messageInput].forEach(function (el) {\n        el.classList.remove('is-valid-ud', 'is-invalid-ud');\n      });\n      [nameFeedback, emailFeedback, subjectFeedback, messageFeedback].forEach(function (el) {\n        el.textContent = '';\n        el.classList.remove('is-valid', 'is-invalid');\n      });\n    });\n  }\n\n  /* ========================================================================\n     BOOTSTRAP INIT\n     ======================================================================== */\n  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {\n    initCartBadge();\n    initNavbarSearch();\n    initStoreLocator();\n    initFaqAccordion();\n    initContactSupport();\n  });\n})();\n","Overwrite":true,"Path":"c:\\Users\\laptop villa\\SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP\\js\\support.js"}}]}

-->\n  <div class=\"ud-ticker text-center\">\n    <div class=\"container\">\n      DROP 04 // FLAGSHIP DOORS OPEN IN ISLAMABAD, LAHORE &amp; KARACHI â€” FREE IN-STORE PICKUP\n    </div>\n  </div>\n\n  <!-- Shared URBANDROPS Top Navbar -->\n  <nav class=\"navbar navbar-expand-lg sticky-top ud-navbar\" aria-label=\"Primary Navigation\">\n    <div class=\"container\">\n      <a class=\"ud-brand\" href=\"../index.html\">\n        <img src=\"../images/Logo.png\" alt=\"URBANDROPS Logo\" class=\"ud-brand-logo\" onerror=\"this.classList.add('is-missing'); this.style.display='none';\">\n data-bs-toggle=\"collapse\" data-bs-target=\"#udMainNavbar\" aria-controls=\"udMainNavbar\" aria-expanded=\"false\" aria-label=\"Toggle navigation\">\n        <span class=\"ud-navbar-toggler-icon\">\n          <svg width=\"22\" height=\"22\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" stroke-width=\"2\" aria-hidden=\"true\">\n            <line x1=\"3\" y1=\"6\" x2=\"21\" y2=\"6\"></line>\n            <line x1=\"3\" y1=\"12\" x2=\"21\" y2=\"12\"></line>\n            <line x1=\"3\" y1=\"18\" x2=\"21\" y2=\"18\"></line>\n          </svg>\n        </span>\n      </button>\n\n      <div class=\"collapse navbar-collapse\" id=\"udMainNavbar\">\n        <ul class=\"navbar-nav mx-auto mb-2 mb-lg-0\">\n          <li class=\"nav-item\">\n            <a class=\"nav-link\" href=\"../index.html\">Home</a>\n          </li>\n          <li class=\"nav-item\">\n            <a class=\"nav-link\" href=\"shop.html\">Drops</a>\n          </li>\n          <li class=\"nav-item\">\n            <a class=\"nav-link active\" aria-current=\"page\" href=\"store-locator.html\">Store Locator</a>\n          </li>\n          <li class=\"nav-item\">\n            <a class=\"nav-link\" href=\"faq.html\">FAQ</a>\n          </li>\n          <li class=\"nav-item\">\n            <a class=\"nav-link\" href=\"contact-support.html\">Support</a>\n          </li>\n        </ul>\n\n        <div class=\"d-flex flex-column flex-lg-row align-items-stretch align-items-lg-center gap-2\">\n          <form id=\"udNavSearchForm\" class=\"ud-search-form\" role=\"search\">\n            <input id=\"udNavSearchInput\" class=\"form-control ud-search-input\" type=\"search\" placeholder=\"Search city or drops...\" aria-label=\"Search URBANDROPS\">\n            <button class=\"ud-search-btn\" type=\"submit\" aria-label=\"Submit search\">\n              <svg width=\"16\" height=\"16\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" stroke-width=\"2.2\" aria-hidden=\"true\">\n                <circle cx=\"11\" cy=\"11\" r=\"8\"></circle>\n                <line x1=\"21\" y1=\"21\" x2=\"16.65\" y2=\"16.65\"></line>\n              </svg>\n            </button>\n          </form>\n\n          <a href=\"cart.html\" class=\"ud-cart-btn justify-content-center\" aria-label=\"Shopping Cart\">\n            <svg width=\"16\" height=\"16\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" stroke-width=\"2\" aria-hidden=\"true\">\n              <path d=\"M6 2L3 6v14a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h14a2 2 0 0 0 2-2V6l-3-4z\"></path>\n              <line x1=\"3\" y1=\"6\" x2=\"21\" y2=\"6\"></line>\n              <path d=\"M16 10a4 4 0 0 1-8 0\"></path>\n            </svg>\n            <span>Cart</span>\n            <span class=\"ud-cart-badge\" data-cart-badge>0</span>\n          </a>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  </nav>\n\n  <!-- Hero Header -->\n  <header class=\"ud-page-hero\">\n    <div class=\"container\">\n      <div class=\"row align-items-end g-4\">\n        <div class=\"col-lg-8\">\n          <span class=\"ud-eyebrow\">Retail Network // Flagship Spaces</span>\n          <h1 class=\"ud-hero-title\">Store Locator</h1>\n          <p class=\"ud-hero-subtitle\">\n            Find URBANDROPS flagship stores, concept studios, and drop pickup points across Islamabad, Lahore, and Karachi.\n          </p>\n        </div>\n        <div class=\"col-lg-4 text-lg-end\">\n          <div class=\"ud-card d-inline-flex align-items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2\">\n            <span class=\"ud-status-pill\">\n              <span class=\"ud-status-dot\" aria-hidden=\"true\"></span>\n              6 DOORS ACTIVE\n            </span>\n            <span class=\"ud-mono small text-secondary\">PK // GMT+5</span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  </header>\n\n  <!-- Main Content -->\n  <main class=\"py-5\">\n    <div class=\"container\">\n      <!-- City Filter Toolbar -->\n      <div class=\"ud-card p-3 p-md-4 mb-4\">\n        <div class=\"row align-items-center g-3\">\n          <div class=\"col-md-5 col-lg-4\">\n            <label for=\"cityFilter\" class=\"ud-form-label d-block\">Filter by City</label>\n            <select id=\"cityFilter\" class=\"form-select ud-form-select\" aria-label=\"Filter stores by city\">\n              <option value=\"all\" selected>All class=\"row align-items-end g-4\">\n        <div class=\"col-lg-8\">\n          <span class=\"ud-eyebrow\">Support Center // Knowledge Base</span>\n          <h1 class=\"ud-hero-title\">Frequently Asked Questions</h1>\n          <p class=\"ud-hero-subtitle\">\n            Everything you need to know about URBANDROPS dispatch timelines, return eligibility, and oversized garment sizing.\n          </p>\n        </div>\n        <div class=\"col-lg-4 text-lg-end\">\n          <a href=\"contact-support.html\" class=\"btn ud-btn-cta\">Still Need Help? Contact Us</a>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  </header>\n\n  <!-- Main Content -->\n  <main class=\"py-5\">\n    <div class=\"container\">\n      <!-- Category Filter Pills -->\n      <div class=\"d-flex flex-wrap gap-2 mb-4\" role=\"group\" aria-label=\"Filter FAQ sections\">\n        <button type=\"button\" class=\"btn ud-btn-outline active\" data-faq-filter=\"all\">All Topics</button>\n        <button type=\"button\" class=\"btn ud-btn-outline\" data-faq-filter=\"shipping\">Shipping</button>\n        <button type=\"button\" class=\"btn ud-btn-outline\" data-faq-filter=\"returns\">Returns</button>\n        <button type=\"button\" class=\"btn ud-btn-outline\" data-faq-filter=\"sizing\">Sizing</button>\n      </div>\n\n      <!-- Empty Search State -->\n      <div id=\"faqEmptyState\" class=\"ud-card p-4 text-center mb-4 d-none\">\n        <p class=\"mb-3 text-secondary\">No FAQ entries matched your search query.</p>\n        <button id=\"resetFaqSearchBtn\" type=\"button\" class=\"btn ud-btn-outline btn-sm\">Reset Search</button>\n      </div>\n\n      <div class=\"row g-4\">\n        <div class=\"col-lg-8\">\n          <!-- SECTION 1: SHIPPING -->\n          <section id=\"shipping\" class=\"mb-5\" data-faq-section=\"shipping\" aria-labelledby=\"faqShippingHeading\">\n            <div class=\"ud-faq-section-header\">\n              <h2 id=\"faqShippingHeading\" class=\"h4 fw-bold text-uppercase mb-0\">01 // Shipping</h2>\n              <span class=\"ud-mono small\" style=\"color: var(--ud-accent);\">DISPATCH &amp; DELIVERY</span>\n            </div>\n\n            <div class=\"ud-faq-item is-open\">\n              <button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-faq-trigger\" aria-expanded=\"true\" aria-controls=\"faq-ship-1\">\n                <span>How long does order processing and nationwide delivery take?</span>\n                <span class=\"ud-faq-toggle-icon\" aria-hidden=\"true\">[-]</span>\n              </button>\n              <div id=\"faq-ship-1\" class=\"ud-faq-panel\">\n                <div class=\"ud-faq-panel-inner\">\n                  <div class=\"ud-faq-answer\">\n                    All standard orders placed before 3:00 PM PKT are packed and dispatched the same business day. Delivery takes <strong>1â€“2 business days</strong> within Islamabad, Lahore, and Karachi, and <strong>3â€“5 business days</strong> for all other cities nationwide. During major seasonal drops, please allow an extra 24 hours for quality control and serial numbering.\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n\n            <div class=\"ud-faq-item\">\n              <button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-faq-trigger\" aria-expanded=\"false\" aria-controls=\"faq-ship-2\">\n                <span>What are the shipping charges and free delivery threshold?</span>\n                <span class=\"ud-faq-toggle-icon\" aria-hidden=\"true\">[+]</span>\n              </button>\n              <div id=\"faq-ship-2\" class=\"ud-faq-panel\">\n                <div class=\"ud-faq-panel-inner\">\n                  <div class=\"ud-faq-answer\">\n                    Standard tracked courier shipping is a flat rate of <strong>PKR 250</strong> across Pakistan. Orders above <strong>PKR 10,000</strong> automatically qualify for free express shipping. Cash on Delivery (COD) and prepaid card/wallet orders carry no hidden handling fees.\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n\n            <div class=\"ud-faq-item\">\n              <button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-faq-trigger\" aria-expanded=\"false\" aria-controls=\"faq-ship-3\">\n                <span>Can I pick up an online drop order at a physical URBANDROPS store?</span>\n                <span class=\"ud-faq-toggle-icon\" aria-hidden=\"true\">[+]</span>\n              </button>\n              <div id=\"faq-ship-3\" class=\"ud-faq-panel\">\n                <div class=\"ud-faq-panel-inner\">\n                  <div class=\"ud-faq-answer\">\n                    Yes. Same-day In-Store Pickup is available at our Islamabad (F-7 &amp; Centaurus), Lahore (Gulberg &amp; DHA Phase 5), and Karachi (Clifton &amp; DHA Phase 6) flagships. Bring your digital order confirmation and valid photo ID within 5 days of receiving the ready-for-pickup SMS.\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          </section>\n\n          <!-- SECTION 2: RETURNS -->\n          <section id=\"returns\" class=\"mb-5\" data-faq-section=\"returns\" aria-labelledby=\"faqReturnsHeading\">\n            <div class=\"ud-faq-section-header\">\n              <h2 id=\"faqReturnsHeading\" class=\"h4 fw-bold text-uppercase mb-0\">02 // Returns</h2>\n              <span class=\"ud-mono small\" style=\"color: var(--ud-accent);\">EXCHANGES &amp; REFUNDS</span>\n            </div>\n\n            <div class=\"ud-faq-item\">\n              <button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-faq-trigger\" aria-expanded=\"false\" aria-controls=\"faq-ret-1\">\n                <span>What is your 14-day return and size exchange policy?</span>\n                <span class=\"ud-faq-toggle-icon\" aria-hidden=\"true\">[+]</span>\n              </button>\n              <div id=\"faq-ret-1\" class=\"ud-faq-panel\">\n                <div class=\"ud-faq-panel-inner\">\n                  <div class=\"ud-faq-answer\">\n                    You may request a return or size exchange within <strong>14 days</strong> of delivery. Items must be unworn, unwashed, free of cologne or marks, and returned with all original URBANDROPS hangtags, zip-locks, and dust bags intact. Size exchanges can also be completed immediately at any flagship store.\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n\n            <div class=\"ud-faq-item\">\n              <button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-faq-trigger\" aria-expanded=\"false\" aria-controls=\"faq-ret-2\">\n                <span>Are limited-edition capsule drops eligible for refunds?</span>\n                <span class=\"ud-faq-toggle-icon\" aria-hidden=\"true\">[+]</span>\n              </button>\n              <div id=\"faq-ret-2\" class=\"ud-faq-panel\">\n                <div class=\"ud-faq-panel-inner\">\n                  <div class=\"ud-faq-answer\">\n                    Numbered archive drops and collaboration capsules are eligible for <strong>size exchanges or store credit</strong> only (while replacement stock lasts), unless the garment arrived defective or damaged. Standard core collection pieces qualify for full refunds back to your original payment method or bank transfer for COD orders.\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n\n            <div class=\"ud-faq-item\">\n              <button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-faq-trigger\" aria-expanded=\"false\" aria-controls=\"faq-ret-3\">\n                <span>How quickly are refunds processed after inspection?</span>\n                <span class=\"ud-faq-toggle-icon\" aria-hidden=\"true\">[+]</span>\n              </button>\n              <div id=\"faq-ret-3\" class=\"ud-faq-panel\">\n                <div class=\"ud-faq-panel-inner\">\n                  <div class=\"ud-faq-answer\">\n                    Once your return parcel arrives at our fulfillment hub, quality inspection takes up to <strong>48 hours</strong>. Approved refunds are issued within <strong>3â€“5 business days</strong> to your bank account, JazzCash/Easypaisa wallet, or original card, and you will receive an email confirmation receipt.\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          </section>\n\n          <!-- SECTION 3: SIZING -->\n          <section id=\"sizing\" class=\"mb-4\" data-faq-section=\"sizing\" aria-labelledby=\"faqSizingHeading\">\n            <div class=\"ud-faq-section-header\">\n              <h2 id=\"faqSizingHeading\" class=\"h4 fw-bold text-uppercase mb-0\">03 // Sizing</h2>\n              <span class=\"ud-mono small\" style=\"color: var(--ud-accent);\">FIT &amp; MEASUREMENTS</span>\n            </div>\n\n            <div class=\"ud-faq-item\">\n              <button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-faq-trigger\" aria-expanded=\"false\" aria-controls=\"faq-size-1\">\n                <span>Do URBANDROPS hoodies and tees run true to size or oversized?</span>\n                <span class=\"ud-faq-toggle-icon\" aria-hidden=\"true\">[+]</span>\n              </button>\n              <div id=\"faq-size-1\" class=\"ud-faq-panel\">\n                <div class=\"ud-faq-panel-inner\">\n                  <div class=\"ud-faq-answer\">\n                    Most URBANDROPS heavyweight tees (280 GSM) and French terry hoodies (480 GSM) are cut in a <strong>boxy, dropped-shoulder streetwear silhouette</strong>. Take your usual size for the intended relaxed drape, or size down one step if you prefer a closer standard fit.\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n\n            <div class=\"ud-faq-item\">\n              <button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-faq-trigger\" aria-expanded=\"false\" aria-controls=\"faq-size-2\">\n                <span>What are the exact chest and length measurements for tops?</span>\n                <span class=\"ud-faq-toggle-icon\" aria-hidden=\"true\">[+]</span>\n              </button>\n              <div id=\"faq-size-2\" class=\"ud-faq-panel\">\n                <div class=\"ud-faq-panel-inner\">\n                  <div class=\"ud-faq-answer\">\n                    <p class=\"mb-2\">Reference flat-lay garment measurements (in inches, Â±0.5\" tolerance):</p>\n                    <div class=\"table-responsive\">\n                      <table class=\"table table-dark table-sm table-bordered border-secondary mb-0 ud-mono small\">\n                        <thead>\n                          <tr>\n                            <th>Size</th>\n                            <th>Chest Width</th>\n                            <th>Body Length</th>\n                            <th>Shoulder Drop</th>\n                          </tr>\n                        </thead>\n                        <tbody>\n                          <tr>\n                            <td>S</td>\n                            <td>22.5\"</td>\n                            <td>27.5\"</td>\n                            <td>21.5\"</td>\n                          </tr>\n                          <tr>\n                            <td>M</td>\n                            <td>24.0\"</td>\n                            <td>28.5\"</td>\n                            <td>22.5\"</td>\n                          </tr>\n                          <tr>\n                            <td>L</td>\n                            <td>25.5\"</td>\n                            <td>29.5\"</td>\n                            <td>23.5\"</td>\n                          </tr>\n                          <tr>\n                            <td>XL</td>\n                            <td>27.0\"</td>\n                            <td>30.5\"</td>\n                            <td>24.5\"</td>\n                          </tr>\n                        </tbody>\n                      </table>\n                    </div>\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n\n            <div class=\"ud-faq-item\">\n              <button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-faq-trigger\" aria-expanded=\"false\" aria-controls=\"faq-size-3\">\n                <span>Will heavyweight cotton pieces shrink after washing?</span>\n                <span class=\"ud-faq-toggle-icon\" aria-hidden=\"true\">[+]</span>\n              </button>\n              <div id=\"faq-size-3\" class=\"ud-faq-panel\">\n                <div class=\"ud-faq-panel-inner\">\n                  <div class=\"ud-faq-answer\">\n                    All URBANDROPS 100% combed cotton jerseys and fleeces are <strong>enzyme pre-shrunk</strong> during production. Shared Header & Footer: Include shared URBANDROPS top navbar with Logo.png (<img src=\"../images/Logo.png\">), navigation links, search bar, and dynamic cart badge reading from localStorage ('cartCount').\n3. pages/womens-catalog.html (Women's Catalog): Grid of women's streetwear items rendered from a JS data array with search bar filter, category tags, and price sorting options (Low to High / High to Low).\n4. pages/cart-checkout.html (Cart & Checkout): Two-column layout. Left column lists cart items dynamically loaded from localStorage with quantity controls and remove buttons. Right column includes a promo code field ('URBAN10'), order summary breakdown (Subtotal, Shipping, Discount, Total), shipping address form, and 'PLACE ORDER' button.\n5. pages/order-tracking.html (Order Tracking): Order ID lookup input (#UD-1042) that displays a 4-step progress bar (Order Placed -> Processing -> Shipped -> Delivered) with status timestamp cards.\n6. */\n\n/* ---------- Women's Catalog ---------- */\n.ud-product-card {\n  background-color: var(--ud-surface);\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  border-radius: 10px;\n  overflow: hidden;\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  height: 100%;\n  transition: border-color 0.2s ease, transform 0.2s ease, box-shadow 0.2s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-product-card:hover {\n  border-color: var(--ud-accent);\n  transform: translateY(-3px);\n  box-shadow: 0 12px 28px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.55);\n}\n\n.ud-product-visual {\n  position: relative;\n  height: 220px;\n  background:\n    radial-gradient(circle at 75% 25%, rgba(255, 77, 0, 0.16), transparent 55%),\n    radial-gradient(circle at 20% 80%, rgba(204, 255, 0, 0.1), transparent 50%),\n    #121212;\n  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: center;\n  padding: 1.5rem;\n}\n\n.ud-product-tag {\n  position: absolute;\n  top: 0.85rem;\n  left: 0.85rem;\n  background-color: rgba(13, 13, 13, 0.9);\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  color: var(--ud-accent);\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.68rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.08em;\n  padding: 0.25rem 0.55rem;\n  border-radius: 4px;\n}\n\n.ud-product-badge-drop {\n  position: absolute;\n  top: 0.85rem;\n  right: 0.85rem;\n  background-color: var(--ud-cta);\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.68rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.06em;\n  padding: 0.25rem 0.55rem;\n  border-radius: 4px;\n}\n\n.ud-product-silhouette {\n  width: 112px;\n  height: 112px;\n  color: #E5E5E5;\n  opacity: 0.92;\n  transition: transform 0.25s ease, color 0.25s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-product-card:hover .ud-product-silhouette {\n  transform: scale(1.06);\n  color: #FFFFFF;\n}\n\n.ud-size-chip {\n  background-color: var(--ud-bg);\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  color: var(--ud-text-muted);\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.72rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  padding: 0.22rem 0.5rem;\n  border-radius: 4px;\n  cursor: pointer;\n  transition: border-color 0.15s ease, color 0.15s ease, background-color 0.15s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-size-chip:hover {\n  border-color: var(--ud-text-muted);\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n}\n\n.ud-size-chip.is-active {\n  background-color: var(--ud-accent);\n  border-color: var(--ud-accent);\n  color: var(--ud-bg);\n}\n\n.ud-price-tag {\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 1.05rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n}\n\n/* ---------- Cart & Checkout ---------- */\n.ud-cart-item {\n  background-color: var(--ud-surface);\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  border-radius: 10px;\n  padding: 1.15rem;\n  transition: border-color 0.2s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-cart-item:hover {\n  border-color: var(--ud-border-hover);\n}\n\n.ud-cart-thumb {\n  width: 76px;\n  height: 76px;\n  flex-shrink: 0;\n  border-radius: 8px;\n  background-color: #121212;\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: center;\n  color: var(--ud-accent);\n}\n\n.ud-qty-group {\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  background-color: var(--ud-bg);\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  border-radius: 6px;\n  overflow: hidden;\n}\n\n.ud-qty-btn {\n  background: transparent;\n  border: none;\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-weight: 700;\n  width: 32px;\n  height: 32px;\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: center;\n  cursor: pointer;\n  transition: background-color 0.15s ease, color 0.15s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-qty-btn:hover {\n  background-color: var(--ud-surface-elevated);\n  color: var(--ud-accent);\n}\n\n.ud-qty-value {\n  min-width: 32px;\n  text-align: center;\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.85rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n}\n\n.ud-remove-btn {\n  background: transparent;\n  border: 1px solid transparent;\n  color: var(--ud-text-muted);\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.74rem;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  padding: 0.3rem 0.55rem;\n  border-radius: 4px;\n  cursor: pointer;\n  transition: color 0.15s ease, border-color 0.15s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-remove-btn:hover {\n  color: var(--ud-cta);\n  border-color: rgba(255, 77, 0, 0.45);\n}\n\n.ud-summary-row {\n  display: flex;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  align-items: center;\n  font-size: 0.94rem;\n  padding: 0.45rem 0;\n}\n\n.ud-summary-total {\n  border-top: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  margin-top: 0.5rem;\n  padding-top: 0.9rem;\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-display);\n  font-size: 1.25rem;\n  font-weight: 800;\n}\n\n/* ---------- Order Tracking 4-Step Progress Bar ---------- */\n.ud-track-steps {\n  position: relative;\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr);\n  gap: 1rem;\n  margin-top: 1.5rem;\n  margin-bottom: 2rem;\n}\n\n.ud-track-progress-track {\n  position: relative;\n  height: 6px;\n  background-color: var(--ud-bg);\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  border-radius: 999px;\n  overflow: hidden;\n  margin-bottom: 1.5rem;\n}\n\n.ud-track-progress-fill {\n  height: 100%;\n  background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--ud-cta), var(--ud-accent));\n  border-radius: 999px;\n  transition: width 0.4s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-track-step-node {\n  background-color: var(--ud-bg);\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  border-radius: 8px;\n  padding: 1rem;\n  transition: border-color 0.25s ease, background-color 0.25s ease;\n}\n\n.ud-track-step-node.is-completed {\n  border-color: rgba(204, 255, 0, 0.55);\n  background-color: rgba(204, 255, 0, 0.05);\n}\n\n.ud-track-step-node.is-current {\n  border-color: var(--ud-cta);\n  background-color: rgba(255, 77, 0, 0.09);\n  box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px rgba(255, 77, 0, 0.25);\n}\n\n.ud-track-step-index {\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: center;\n  width: 28px;\n  height: 28px;\n  border-radius: 999px;\n  font-family: var(--ud-font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.78rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  background-color: var(--ud-surface);\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  color: var(--ud-text-muted);\n  margin-bottom: 0.65rem;\n}\n\n.ud-track-step-node.is-completed .ud-track-step-index {\n  background-color: var(--ud-accent);\n  border-color: var(--ud-accent);\n  color: var(--ud-bg);\n}\n\n.ud-track-step-node.is-current .ud-track-step-index {\n  background-color: var(--ud-cta);\n  border-color: var(--ud-cta);\n  color: var(--ud-text);\n}\n\n.ud-timestamp-card {\n  background-color: var(--ud-bg);\n  border: 1px solid var(--ud-border);\n  border-left: 3px solid var(--ud-border);\n  border-radius: 8px;\n  padding: 1rem 1.15rem;\n  height: 100%;\n}\n\n.ud-timestamp-card.is-completed {\n  border-left-color: var(--ud-accent);\n}\n\n.ud-timestamp-card.is-current {\n  border-left-color: var(--ud-cta);\n}\n\n@media (max-width: 767.98px) {\n  .ud-track-steps {\n    grid-template-columns: 1fr;\n    gap: 0.75rem;\n  }\n}\n","Overwrite":true,"Path":"c:\\Users\\laptop villa\\SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP\\css\\commerce.css"}},{"name":"write_file","args":{"Content":"/**\n * URBANDROPS â€” Commerce & Order Tracking Vanilla JS\n * Handles:\n *  - Women's Catalog (pages/womens-catalog.html): JS data array, category filter, search filter, price sort, Add to Cart\n *  - Cart & Checkout (pages/cart-checkout.html): localStorage cart sync, qty +/-, remove, promo 'URBAN10', summary, shipping form, PLACE ORDER\n *  - Order Tracking (pages/order-tracking.html): Order ID lookup (#UD-1042 + localStorage placed orders), 4-step progress bar & status timestamp cards\n */\n\n(function () {\n  'use strict';\n\n  const CART_STORAGE_KEY = 'urbandropsCart';\n  const CART_COUNT_KEY = 'cartCount';\n  const ORDERS_STORAGE_KEY = 'urbandropsOrders';\n\n  /* ========================================================================\n     SHARED CART & CURRENCY UTILITIES\n     ======================================================================== */\n  function formatPKR(amount) {\n    const safe = Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(amount) || 0));\n    return 'PKR ' + safe.toLocaleString('en-US');\n  }\n\n  function readCart() {\n    try {\n      const raw = localStorage.getItem(CART_STORAGE_KEY);\n      if (!raw) return [];\n      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);\n      return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : [];\n    } catch (err) {\n      return [];\n    }\n  }\n\n  function writeCart(items) {\n    const clean = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];\n    const totalCount = clean.reduce(function (sum, item) {\n      return sum + (parseInt(item.qty, 10) || 0);\n    }, 0);\n\n    try {\n      localStorage.setItem(CART_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(clean));\n      localStorage.setItem(CART_COUNT_KEY, String(totalCount));\n    } catch (err) {\n      // Ignore storage quota errors cleanly\n    }\n\n    // Sync navbar cart badge immediately across the current tab\n    document.querySelectorAll('[data-cart-badge]').forEach(function (badge) {\n      badge.textContent = String(totalCount);\n      badge.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Cart items: ' + totalCount);\n    });\n  }\n\n  function showCommerceToast(message) {\n    const existing = document.getElementById('udCommerceToast');\n    if (existing) existing.remove();\n\n    const toast = document.createElement('div');\n    toast.id = 'udCommerceToast';\n    toast.className = 'ud-toast';\n    toast.setAttribute('role', 'status');\n    toast.textContent = message;\n    document.body.appendChild(toast);\n\n    window.setTimeout(function () {\n      if (toast.parentNode) toast.remove();\n    }, 3400);\n  }\n\n  /* ========================================================================\n     1. WOMEN'S CATALOG DATA & LOGIC (pages/womens-catalog.html)\n     ======================================================================== */\n  const WOMENS_CATALOG = [\n    {\n      id: 'w-hoodie-01',\n      name: 'APEX CROPPED HEAVY HOODIE',\n      category: 'Hoodies & Sweats',\n      price: 7400,\n      colorway: 'Obsidian Black / Acid',\n      tag: '480 GSM FLEECE',\n      badge: 'DROP 04',\n      sizes: ['XS', 'S', 'M', 'L'],\n      icon: 'hoodie',\n      description: 'Boxy cropped cut with double-lined hood and high-density puff back print.'\n    },\n    {\n      id: 'w-jacket-01',\n      name: 'KINETIC NYLON BOMBER SHELL',\n      category: 'Outerwear',\n      price: 12900,\n      colorway: 'Matte Onyx / Signal Orange',\n      tag: 'WATER REPELLENT',\n      badge: 'LIMITED',\n      sizes: ['XS', 'S', 'M', 'L', 'XL'],\n      icon: 'jacket',\n      description: 'Ripstop windproof shell with articulated sleeves and two-way hardware.'\n    },\n    {\n      id: 'w-cargo-01',\n      name: 'SECTOR-07 PARACHUTE CARGOS',\n      category: 'Cargos & Bottoms',\n      price: 8200,\n      colorway: 'Graphite Charcoal',\n      tag: 'RELAXED WIDE LEG',\n      badge: 'BESTSELLER',\n      sizes: ['XS', 'S', 'M', 'L'],\n      icon: 'pants',\n      description: 'Lightweight technical twill with bungee ankle toggles and gusset pockets.'\n    },\n    {\n      id: 'w-tee-01',\n      name: 'RAW SEAM BABY TEE',\n      category: 'Tees & Tops',\n      price: 3600,\n      colorway: 'Washed Charcoal',\n      tag: '260 GSM RIB',\n      badge: 'CORE',\n      sizes: ['XS', 'S', 'M', 'L'],\n      icon: 'tee',\n      description: 'Contoured stretch-cotton jersey with exposed contrast overlock stitching.'\n    },\n    {\n      id: 'w-hoodie-02',\n      name: 'ACID WASH OVERSIZED ZIP-UP',\n      category: 'Hoodies & Sweats',\n      price: 8600,\n      colorway: 'Smoke Slate',\n      tag: 'ENZYME WASHED',\n      badge: 'NEW',\n      sizes: ['S', 'M', 'L', 'XL'],\n      icon: 'hoodie',\n      description: 'Dropped-shoulder full-zip hoodie with antique silver YKK metal zipper.'\n    },\n    {\n      id: 'w-jacket-02',\n      name: 'UTILITY PUFFER VEST',\n      category: 'Outerwear',\n      price: 9800,\n      colorway: 'Carbon Black',\n      tag: 'THERMAL FILL',\n      badge: 'DROP 04',\n      sizes: ['XS', 'S', 'M', 'L'],\n      icon: 'jacket',\n      description: 'Cropped tactical puffer vest with concealed chest stash pocket.'\n    },\n    {\n      id: 'w-cargo-02',\n      name: 'CONCRETE WIDE SWEATPANTS',\n      category: 'Cargos & Bottoms',\n      price: 6200,\n      colorway: 'Asphalt Grey',\n      tag: '420 GSM TERRY',\n      badge: 'CORE',\n      sizes: ['XS', 'S', 'M', 'L', 'XL'],\n      icon: 'pants',\n      description: 'Open-hem wide-leg lounge fleece with raw edge waistband drawcords.'\n    },\n    {\n      id: 'w-tee-02',\n      name: 'CYBER GRAPHIC BOXY TEE',\n      category: 'Tees & Tops',\n      price: 4200,\n      colorway: 'Jet Black / Lime',\n      tag: '280 GSM JERSEY',\n      badge: 'NEW',\n      sizes: ['XS', 'S', 'M', 'L', 'XL'],\n      icon: 'tee',\n      description: 'Oversized unisex-block womenâ€™s cut featuring reflective back typography.'\n    },\n    {\n      id: 'w-acc-01',\n      name: 'TACTICAL CROSSBODY SLING',\n      category: 'Accessories',\n      price: 4500,\n      colorway: 'Stealth Black',\n      tag: 'CORDURA NYLON',\n      badge: 'GEAR',\n      sizes: ['ONE SIZE'],\n      icon: 'bag',\n      description: 'Compact weatherproof crossbody bag with quick-release fidlock buckle.'\n    }\n  ];\n\n  function getSilhouetteSvg(type) {\n    if (type === 'hoodie') {\n      return '<svg class=\"ud-product-silhouette\" viewBox=\"0 0 64 64\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" stroke-width=\"2.2\" stroke-linecap=\"round\" stroke-linejoin=\"round\" aria-hidden=\"true\">' +\n        '<path d=\"M22 14c0-5 4-8 10-8s10 3 10 8l10 6 4 20-8 3-3-13v24H19V30l-3 13-8-3 4-20 10-6z\"/>' +\n        '<path d=\"M24 14c2 4 14 4 16 0\"/>' +\n        '<rect x=\"24\" y=\"38\" width=\"16\" height=\"10\" rx=\"2\"/>' +\n      '</svg>';\n    }\n    if (type === 'jacket') {\n      return '<svg class=\"ud-product-silhouette\" viewBox=\"0 0 64 64\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" stroke-width=\"2.2\" stroke-linecap=\"round\" stroke-linejoin=\"round\" aria-hidden=\"true\">' +\n        '<path d=\"M20 12l12 4 12-4 10 8 4 22-9 2-3-14v24H18V30l-3 14-9-2 4-22 10-8z\"/>' +\n        '<line x1=\"32\" y1=\"16\" x2=\"32\" y2=\"54\"/>' +\n      '</svg>';\n    }\n    if (type === 'pants') {\n      return '<svg class=\"ud-product-silhouette\" viewBox=\"0 0 64 64\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" stroke-width=\"2.2\" stroke-linecap=\"round\" stroke-linejoin=\"round\" aria-hidden=\"true\">' +\n        '<path d=\"M18 10h28l4 44-12 2-6-26-6 26-12-2 4-44z\"/>' +\n        '<line x1=\"18\" y1=\"18\" x2=\"46\" y2=\"18\"/>' +\n        '<rect x=\"14\" y=\"28\" width=\"7\" height=\"10\" rx=\"1\"/>' +\n        '<rect x=\"43\" y=\"28\" width=\"7\" height=\"10\" rx=\"1\"/>' +\n      '</svg>';\n    }\n    if (type === 'bag') {\n      return '<svg class=\"ud-product-silhouette\" viewBox=\"0 0 64 64\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" stroke-width=\"2.2\" stroke-linecap=\"round\" stroke-linejoin=\"round\" aria-hidden=\"true\">' +\n        '<rect x=\"14\" y=\"22\" width=\"36\" height=\"28\" rx=\"4\"/>' +\n        '<path d=\"M22 22c0-7 4-12 10-12s10 5 10 12\"/>' +\n        '<line x1=\"14\" y1=\"34\" x2=\"50\" y2=\"34\"/>' +\n        '<rect x=\"28\" y=\"31\" width=\"8\" height=\"6\" rx=\"1\"/>' +\n      '</svg>';\n    }\n    return '<svg class=\"ud-product-silhouette\" viewBox=\"0 0 64 64\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" stroke-width=\"2.2\" stroke-linecap=\"round\" stroke-linejoin=\"round\" aria-hidden=\"true\">' +\n      '<path d=\"M20 12c3 4 21 4 24 0l12 8-5 11-7-3v24H20V28l-7 3-5-11 12-8z\"/>' +\n    '</svg>';\n  }\n\n  function initWomensCatalog() {\n    const grid = document.getElementById('womensCatalogGrid');\n    if (!grid) return;\n\n    const searchInput = document.getElementById('catalogSearchInput');\n    const navSearchInput = document.getElementById('udNavSearchInput');\n    const navSearchForm = document.getElementById('udNavSearchForm');\n    const sortSelect = document.getElementById('catalogPriceSort');\n    const categoryButtons = document.querySelectorAll('[data-catalog-category]');\n    const countLabel = document.getElementById('catalogCountLabel');\n    const emptyState = document.getElementById('catalogEmptyState');\n    const resetBtn = document.getElementById('resetCatalogFilters');\n\n    let activeCategory = 'All';\n    let searchQuery = '';\n    let sortMode = 'featured';\n    const selectedSizes = {};\n\n    WOMENS_CATALOG.forEach(function (item) {\n      selectedSizes[item.id] = item.sizes.includes('M') ? 'M' : item.sizes[0];\n    });\n\n    function addToCart(productId) {\n      const product = WOMENS_CATALOG.find(function (p) { return p.id === productId; });\n      if (!product) return;\n\n      const chosenSize = selectedSizes[productId] || product.sizes[0];\n      const cart = readCart();\n      const existing = cart.find(function (entry) {\n        return entry.id === product.id && entry.size === chosenSize;\n      });\n\n      if (existing) {\n        existing.qty += 1;\n      } else {\n        cart.push({\n          id: product.id,\n          name: product.name,\n          category: product.category,\n          colorway: product.colorway,\n          size: chosenSize,\n          price: product.price,\n          qty: 1,\n          icon: product.icon\n        });\n      }\n\n      writeCart(cart);\n      showCommerceToast('Added \"' + product.name + '\" (' + chosenSize + ') to cart.');\n    }\n\n    function renderCatalog() {\n      const lowerQuery = searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();\n\n      let filtered = WOMENS_CATALOG.filter(function (item) {\n        const matchesCategory = activeCategory === 'All' || item.category === activeCategory;\n        const matchesSearch = !lowerQuery ||\n          item.name.toLowerCase().includes(lowerQuery) ||\n          item.category.toLowerCase().includes(lowerQuery) ||\n          item.colorway.toLowerCase().includes(lowerQuery) ||\n          item.tag.toLowerCase().includes(lowerQuery) ||\n          item.description.toLowerCase().includes(lowerQuery);\n        return matchesCategory && matchesSearch;\n      });\n\n      if (sortMode === 'price-asc') {\n        filtered = filtered.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return a.price - b.price; });\n      } else if (sortMode === 'price-desc') {\n        filtered = filtered.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return b.price - a.price; });\n      }\n\n      if (countLabel) {\n        countLabel.textContent = 'Showing ' + filtered.length + ' of ' + WOMENS_CATALOG.length + ' Pieces';\n      }\n\n      if (emptyState) {\n        emptyState.classList.toggle('d-none', filtered.length > 0);\n      }\n\n      grid.innerHTML = '';\n\n      filtered.forEach(function (item) {\n        const col = document.createElement('div');\n        col.className = 'col-12 col-sm-6 col-lg-4';\n\n        const sizeChipsHtml = item.sizes.map(function (sz) {\n          const isActive = selectedSizes[item.id] === sz;\n          return '<button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-size-chip ' + (isActive ? 'is-active' : '') + '\" data-product-id=\"' + item.id + '\" data-size-val=\"' + sz + '\">' + sz + '</button>';\n        }).join('');\n\n        col.innerHTML =\n          '<article class=\"ud-product-card\">' +\n            '<div class=\"ud-product-visual\">' +\n              '<span class=\"ud-product-tag\">' + item.tag + '</span>' +\n              '<span class=\"ud-product-badge-drop\">' + item.badge + '</span>' +\n              getSilhouetteSvg(item.icon) +\n            '</div>' +\n            '<div class=\"p-3 p-md-4 d-flex flex-column flex-grow-1\">' +\n              '<div class=\"d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-1\">' +\n                '<span class=\"ud-mono small text-uppercase\" style=\"color: var(--ud-accent);\">' + item.category + '</span>' +\n                '<span class=\"ud-mono small text-secondary\">' + item.colorway + '</span>' +\n              '</div>' +\n              '<h2 class=\"h5 fw-bold mb-2\">' + item.name + '</h2>' +\n              '<p class=\"text-secondary small mb-3 flex-grow-1\">' + item.description + '</p>' +\n              '<div class=\"mb-3\">' +\n                '<div class=\"ud-mono small text-secondary mb-1\">SIZE:</div>' +\n                '<div class=\"d-flex flex-wrap gap-1\">' + sizeChipsHtml + '</div>' +\n              '</div>' +\n              '<div class=\"d-flex align-items-center justify-content-between gap-2 pt-2 border-top border-secondary border-opacity-25\">' +\n                '<span class=\"ud-price-tag\">' + formatPKR(item.price) + '</span>' +\n                '<button type=\"button\" class=\"btn ud-btn-cta btn-sm\" data-add-to-cart=\"' + item.id + '\">Add to Cart</button>' +\n              '</div>' +\n            '</div>' +\n          '</article>';\n\n        grid.appendChild(col);\n      });\n\n      grid.querySelectorAll('[data-size-val]').forEach(function (btn) {\n        btn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n          const pid = btn.getAttribute('data-product-id');\n          const sz = btn.getAttribute('data-size-val');\n          selectedSizes[pid] = sz;\n          grid.querySelectorAll('[data-product-id=\"' + pid + '\"]').forEach(function (sib) {\n            sib.classList.toggle('is-active', sib.getAttribute('data-size-val') === sz);\n          });\n        });\n      });\n\n      grid.querySelectorAll('[data-add-to-cart]').forEach(function (btn) {\n        btn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n          addToCart(btn.getAttribute('data-add-to-cart'));\n        });\n      });\n    }\n\n    categoryButtons.forEach(function (btn) {\n      btn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n        activeCategory = btn.getAttribute('data-catalog-category');\n        categoryButtons.forEach(function (b) {\n          b.classList.toggle('active', b === btn);\n        });\n        renderCatalog();\n      });\n    });\n\n    if (searchInput) {\n      searchInput.addEventListener('input', function () {\n        searchQuery = searchInput.value;\n        if (navSearchInput) navSearchInput.value = searchQuery;\n        renderCatalog();\n      });\n    }\n\n    if (navSearchInput && navSearchForm) {\n      navSearchInput.addEventListener('input', function () {\n        searchQuery = navSearchInput.value;\n        if (searchInput) searchInput.value = searchQuery;\n        renderCatalog();\n      });\n      navSearchForm.addEventListener('submit', function (event) {\n        event.preventDefault();\n        searchQuery = navSearchInput.value;\n        if (searchInput) searchInput.value = searchQuery;\n        renderCatalog();\n      }, true);\n    }\n\n    if (sortSelect) {\n      sortSelect.addEventListener('change', function () {\n        sortMode = sortSelect.value;\n        renderCatalog();\n      });\n    }\n\n    if (resetBtn) {\n      resetBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n        activeCategory = 'All';\n        searchQuery = '';\n        sortMode = 'featured';\n        if (searchInput) searchInput.value = '';\n        if (navSearchInput) navSearchInput.value = '';\n        if (sortSelect) sortSelect.value = 'featured';\n        categoryButtons.forEach(function (b) {\n          b.classList.toggle('active', b.getAttribute('data-catalog-category') === 'All');\n        });\n        renderCatalog();\n      });\n    }\n\n    renderCatalog();\n  }\n\n  /* ========================================================================\n     2. CART & CHECKOUT PAGE (pages/cart-checkout.html)\n     ======================================================================== */\n  const SAMPLE_SEED_CART = [\n    {\n      id: 'w-hoodie-01',\n      name: 'APEX CROPPED HEAVY HOODIE',\n      category: 'Hoodies & Sweats',\n      colorway: 'Obsidian Black / Acid',\n      size: 'M',\n      price: 7400,\n      qty: 1,\n      icon: 'hoodie'\n    },\n    {\n      id: 'w-cargo-01',\n      name: 'SECTOR-07 PARACHUTE CARGOS',\n      category: 'Cargos & Bottoms',\n      colorway: 'Graphite Charcoal',\n      size: 'S',\n      price: 8200,\n      qty: 1,\n      icon: 'pants'\n    }\n  ];\n\n  function initCartCheckout() {\n    const cartItemsContainer = document.getElementById('cartItemsContainer');\n    if (!cartItemsContainer) return;\n\n    const cartEmptyState = document.getElementById('cartEmptyState');\n    const loadSampleCartBtn = document.getElementById('loadSampleCartBtn');\n    const cartItemCountLabel = document.getElementById('cartItemCountLabel');\n\n    const promoInput = document.getElementById('promoCodeInput');\n    const applyPromoBtn = document.getElementById('applyPromoBtn');\n    const promoFeedback = document.getElementById('promoFeedback');\n\n    const summarySubtotalEl = document.getElementById('summarySubtotal');\n    const summaryShippingEl = document.getElementById('summaryShipping');\n    const summaryDiscountEl = document.getElementById('summaryDiscount');\n    const summaryDiscountRow = document.getElementById('summaryDiscountRow');\n    const summaryTotalEl = document.getElementById('summaryTotal');\n\n    const checkoutForm = document.getElementById('checkoutShippingForm');\n    const placeOrderBtn = document.getElementById('placeOrderBtn');\n    const orderAlert = document.getElementById('orderConfirmationAlert');\n    const placedOrderIdEl = document.getElementById('placedOrderId');\n    const placedOrderSummaryEl = document.getElementById('placedOrderSummary');\n    const trackPlacedOrderLink = document.getElementById('trackPlacedOrderLink');\n\n    let appliedPromo = '';\n\n    function computeTotals(cart) {\n      const subtotal = cart.reduce(function (sum, item) {\n        return sum + (Number(item.price) * (parseInt(item.qty, 10) || 0));\n      }, 0);\n      const shipping = subtotal === 0 ? 0 : (subtotal >= 10000 ? 0 : 250);\n      const discount = appliedPromo === 'URBAN10' ? Math.round(subtotal * 0.10) : 0;\n      const total = Math.max(0, subtotal - discount + shipping);\n      return { subtotal: subtotal, shipping: shipping, discount: discount, total: total };\n    }\n\n    function renderSummary(cart) {\n      const totals = computeTotals(cart);\n\n      if (summarySubtotalEl) summarySubtotalEl.textContent = formatPKR(totals.subtotal);\n      if (summaryShippingEl) {\n        summaryShippingEl.textContent = totals.subtotal === 0\n          ? formatPKR(0)\n          : (totals.shipping === 0 ? 'FREE' : formatPKR(totals.shipping));\n      }\n      if (summaryDiscountRow) {\n        summaryDiscountRow.classList.toggle('d-none', totals.discount === 0);\n      }\n      if (summaryDiscountEl) {\n        summaryDiscountEl.textContent = '- ' + formatPKR(totals.discount);\n      }\n      if (summaryTotalEl) summaryTotalEl.textContent = formatPKR(totals.total);\n\n      if (placeOrderBtn) {\n        placeOrderBtn.disabled = cart.length === 0;\n      }\n    }\n\n    function renderCart() {\n      const cart = readCart();\n      const totalQty = cart.reduce(function (sum, item) { return sum + (parseInt(item.qty, 10) || 0); }, 0);\n\n      if (cartItemCountLabel) {\n        cartItemCountLabel.textContent = totalQty + ' ' + (totalQty === 1 ? 'Item' : 'Items');\n      }\n\n      if (cartEmptyState) {\n        cartEmptyState.classList.toggle('d-none', cart.length > 0);\n      }\n\n      cartItemsContainer.innerHTML = '';\n\n      cart.forEach(function (item, index) {\n        const lineTotal = Number(item.price) * (parseInt(item.qty, 10) || 1);\n        const card = document.createElement('article');\n        card.className = 'ud-cart-item';\n\n        card.innerHTML =\n          '<div class=\"d-flex flex-column flex-sm-row align-items-sm-center justify-content-between gap-3\">' +\n            '<div class=\"d-flex align-items-center gap-3\">' +\n              '<div class=\"ud-cart-thumb\">' +\n                getSilhouetteSvg(item.icon || 'tee').replace('class=\"ud-product-silhouette\"', 'width=\"44\" height=\"44\"') +\n              '</div>' +\n              '<div>' +\n                '<span class=\"ud-mono small text-uppercase\" style=\"color: var(--ud-accent);\">' + (item.category || 'URBANDROPS') + '</span>' +\n                '<h2 class=\"h6 fw-bold mb-1\">' + item.name + '</h2>' +\n                '<div class=\"ud-mono small text-secondary\">' +\n                  'SIZE: <span class=\"text-white\">' + item.size + '</span> &bull; ' + (item.colorway || 'Standard') +\n                '</div>' +\n                '<div class=\"ud-mono small text-secondary mt-1\">' + formatPKR(item.price) + ' each</div>' +\n              '</div>' +\n            '</div>' +\n            '<div class=\"d-flex align-items-center justify-content-between justify-content-sm-end gap-3\">' +\n              '<div class=\"ud-qty-group\" aria-label=\"Quantity controls for ' + item.name + '\">' +\n                '<button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-qty-btn\" data-qty-dec=\"' + index + '\" aria-label=\"Decrease quantity\">-</button>' +\n                '<span class=\"ud-qty-value\">' + item.qty + '</span>' +\n                '<button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-qty-btn\" data-qty-inc=\"' + index + '\" aria-label=\"Increase quantity\">+</button>' +\n              '</div>' +\n              '<div class=\"text-end\" style=\"min-width: 92px;\">' +\n                '<div class=\"ud-mono fw-bold text-white\">' + formatPKR(lineTotal) + '</div>' +\n                '<button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-remove-btn mt-1\" data-remove-idx=\"' + index + '\">Remove</button>' +\n              '</div>' +\n            '</div>' +\n          '</div>';\n\n        cartItemsContainer.appendChild(card);\n      });\n\n      cartItemsContainer.querySelectorAll('[data-qty-dec]').forEach(function (btn) {\n        btn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n          const idx = parseInt(btn.getAttribute('data-qty-dec'), 10);\n          const updated = readCart();\n          if (!updated[idx]) return;\n          if (updated[idx].qty > 1) {\n            updated[idx].qty -= 1;\n          } else {\n            updated.splice(idx, 1);\n          }\n          writeCart(updated);\n          renderCart();\n        });\n      });\n\n      cartItemsContainer.querySelectorAll('[data-qty-inc]').forEach(function (btn) {\n        btn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n          const idx = parseInt(btn.getAttribute('data-qty-inc'), 10);\n          const updated = readCart();\n          if (!updated[idx]) return;\n          updated[idx].qty += 1;\n          writeCart(updated);\n          renderCart();\n        });\n      });\n\n      cartItemsContainer.querySelectorAll('[data-remove-idx]').forEach(function (btn) {\n        btn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n          const idx = parseInt(btn.getAttribute('data-remove-idx'), 10);\n          const updated = readCart();\n          const removed = updated.splice(idx, 1);\n          writeCart(updated);\n          renderCart();\n          if (removed[0]) {\n            showCommerceToast('Removed \"' + removed[0].name + '\" from cart.');\n          }\n        });\n      });\n\n      renderSummary(cart);\n    }\n\n    if (loadSampleCartBtn) {\n      loadSampleCartBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n        writeCart(SAMPLE_SEED_CART.map(function (item) { return Object.assign({}, item); }));\n        renderCart();\n        showCommerceToast('Loaded 2 sample URBANDROPS items into your cart.');\n      });\n    }\n\n    if (applyPromoBtn && promoInput) {\n      applyPromoBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n        const code = promoInput.value.trim().toUpperCase();\n        if (promoFeedback) {\n          promoFeedback.classList.remove('is-valid', 'is-invalid');\n        }\n\n        if (code === 'URBAN10') {\n          appliedPromo = 'URBAN10';\n          if (promoFeedback) {\n            promoFeedback.classList.add('is-valid');\n            promoFeedback.textContent = 'Promo URBAN10 applied: 10% off subtotal.';\n          }\n        } else if (code === '') {\n          appliedPromo = '';\n          if (promoFeedback) {\n            promoFeedback.classList.add('is-invalid');\n            promoFeedback.textContent = 'Enter a promo code (try URBAN10).';\n          }\n        } else {\n          appliedPromo = '';\n          if (promoFeedback) {\n            promoFeedback.classList.add('is-invalid');\n            promoFeedback.textContent = 'Invalid promo code. Use URBAN10 for 10% off.';\n          }\n        }\n        renderSummary(readCart());\n      });\n    }\n\n    if (checkoutForm) {\n      checkoutForm.addEventListener('submit', function (event) {\n        event.preventDefault();\n        const cart = readCart();\n        if (cart.length === 0) {\n          showCommerceToast('Your cart is empty. Add items before placing an order.');\n          return;\n        }\n\n        const fullName = document.getElementById('shipName');\n        const email = document.getElementById('shipEmail');\n        const phone = document.getElementById('shipPhone');\n        const address = document.getElementById('shipAddress');\n        const city = document.getElementById('shipCity');\n        const postal = document.getElementById('shipPostal');\n\n        const requiredFields = [fullName, email, phone, address, city, postal];\n        let valid = true;\n\n        requiredFields.forEach(function (field) {\n          if (!field) return;\n          const ok = field.value.trim().length > 0 && (field.id !== 'shipEmail' || /^[^\\s@]+@[^\\s@]+\\.[^\\s@]{2,}$/.test(field.value.trim()));\n          field.classList.toggle('is-invalid-ud', !ok);\n          field.classList.toggle('is-valid-ud', ok);\n          if (!ok) valid = false;\n        });\n\n        if (!valid) {\n          showCommerceToast('Please complete all shipping address fields.');\n          return;\n        }\n\n        const totals = computeTotals(cart);\n        const orderCode = '#UD-' + Math.floor(2000 + Math.random() * 7999);\n        const nowStr = new Date().toLocaleString('en-US', {\n          month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit'\n        });\n\n        // Save placed order so user can immediately track it on order-tracking.html\n        try {\n          const existingOrders = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(ORDERS_STORAGE_KEY) || '{}');\n          existingOrders[orderCode.replace('#', '').toUpperCase()] = {\n            orderId: orderCode,\n            customer: fullName.value.trim(),\n            city: city.value,\n            total: formatPKR(totals.total),\n            carrier: 'URBANDROPS Express Dispatch',\n            eta: 'Est. Delivery: 2â€“3 Business Days',\n            currentStep: 1,\n            steps: [\n              { title: 'Order Placed', status: 'completed', time: nowStr, location: 'Online Checkout', note: 'Order confirmed & payment verified.' },\n              { title: 'Processing', status: 'pending', time: 'Pending', location: 'Lahore Fulfillment Hub', note: 'Queued for garment QC & serial tagging.' },\n              { title: 'Shipped', status: 'pending', time: 'Pending', location: 'In Transit', note: 'Awaiting courier handoff.' },\n              { title: 'Delivered', status: 'pending', time: 'Pending', location: city.value, note: 'Awaiting final doorstep delivery.' }\n            ]\n          };\n          localStorage.setItem(ORDERS_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(existingOrders));\n        } catch (err) {\n          // Ignore storage issues\n        }\n\n        if (placedOrderIdEl) placedOrderIdEl.textContent = orderCode;\n        if (placedOrderSummaryEl) {\n          placedOrderSummaryEl.textContent =\n            'Total charged: ' + formatPKR(totals.total) + ' â€¢ Shipping to ' + address.value.trim() + ', ' + city.value + '.';\n        }\n        if (trackPlacedOrderLink) {\n          trackPlacedOrderLink.href = 'order-tracking.html?order=' + encodeURIComponent(orderCode.replace('#', ''));\n        }\n        if (orderAlert) {\n          orderAlert.classList.remove('d-none');\n          orderAlert.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'nearest' });\n        }\n\n        appliedPromo = '';\n        if (promoInput) promoInput.value = '';\n        if (promoFeedback) promoFeedback.textContent = '';\n        checkoutForm.reset();\n        requiredFields.forEach(function (f) {\n          if (f) f.classList.remove('is-valid-ud', 'is-invalid-ud');\n        });\n        writeCart([]);\n        renderCart();\n      });\n    }\n\n    // Sync if localStorage changes in another tab\n    window.addEventListener('storage', function (event) {\n      if (event.key === CART_STORAGE_KEY || event.key === CART_COUNT_KEY) {\n        renderCart();\n      }\n    });\n\n    renderCart();\n Nov 27, 2026 Evening',\n          location: 'DHA Phase 5, Lahore',\n          note: 'Rider will call 15 mins prior to arrival.'\n        }\n      ]\n    }\n  };\n\n  function getOrderByCode(rawCode) {\n    const normalized = rawCode.trim().replace(/^#/, '').toUpperCase();\n    if (!normalized) return null;\n\n    if (DEMO_ORDERS[normalized]) {\n      return DEMO_ORDERS[normalized];\n    }\n\n    try {\n      const saved = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(ORDERS_STORAGE_KEY) || '{}');\n      if (saved[normalized]) return saved[normalized];\n    } catch (err) {\n      // Ignore parse errors\n    }\n\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  function initOrderTracking() {\n    const lookupForm = document.getElementById('orderTrackingForm');\n    const orderInput = document.getElementById('orderIdInput');\n    const trackingResultCard = document.getElementById('trackingResultCard');\n    const trackingNotFound = document.getElementById('trackingNotFound');\n    const progressFill = document.getElementById('trackingProgressFill');\n    const stepsContainer = document.getElementById('trackingStepsContainer');\n    const timestampsContainer = document.getElementById('trackingTimestampsContainer');\n\n    if (!lookupForm || !orderInput || !stepsContainer || !timestampsContainer) return;\n\n    function renderOrder(order) {\n      if (!order) {\n        if (trackingResultCard) trackingResultCard.classList.add('d-none');\n        if (trackingNotFound) trackingNotFound.classList.remove('d-none');\n        return;\n      }\n\n      if (trackingNotFound) trackingNotFound.classList.add('d-none');\n      if (trackingResultCard) trackingResultCard.classList.remove('d-none');\n\n      const idEl = = order.carrier;\n      if (etaEl) etaEl.textContent = order.eta;\n      if (totalEl) totalEl.textContent = order.total;\n\n      const percentMap = { 1: '25%', 2: '50%', 3: '75%', 4: '100%' };\n      if (progressFill) {\n        progressFill.style.width = percentMap[order.currentStep] || '25%';\n        progressFill.setAttribute('aria-valuenow', String((order.currentStep || 1) * 25));\n      }\n\n      stepsContainer.innerHTML = '';\n      timestampsContainer.innerHTML = '';\n\n      order.steps.forEach(function (step, idx) {\n        const stateClass = step.status === 'completed'\n          ? 'is-completed'\n          : (step.status === 'current' ? 'is-current' : '');\n\n        const statusBadgeText = step.status === 'completed'\n          ? 'DONE'\n          : (step.status === 'current' ? 'IN PROGRESS' : 'PENDING');\n\n        const stepNode = document.createElement('div');\n        stepNode.className = 'ud-track-step-node ' + stateClass;\n        stepNode.innerHTML =\n          '<div class=\"d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-1\">' +\n            '<span class=\"ud-track-step-index\">0' + (idx + 1) + '</span>' +\n            '<span class=\"ud-mono small\" style=\"color:' + (step.status === 'completed' ? 'var(--ud-accent)' : (step.status === 'current' ? 'var(--ud-cta)' : 'var(--ud-text-muted)')) + ';\">' + statusBadgeText + '</span>' +\n          '</div>' +\n          '<h3 class=\"h6 fw-bold mb-1\">' + step.title + '</h3>' +\n          '<div class=\"ud-mono small text-secondary\">' + step.time + '</div>';\n\n        stepsContainer.appendChild(stepNode);\n\n        const tsCol = document.createElement('div');\n        tsCol.className = 'col-12 col-md-6';\n        tsCol.innerHTML =\n          '<article class=\"ud-timestamp-card ' + stateClass + '\">' +\n            '<div class=\"d-flex flex-wrap justify-content-between align-items-center gap-2 mb-2\">' +\n              '<span class=\"ud-mono small fw-bold text-uppercase\" style=\"color:' + (step.status === 'completed' ? 'var(--ud-accent)' : (step.status === 'current' ? 'var(--ud-cta)' : 'var(--ud-text-muted)')) + ';\">' +\n                'STEP 0' + (idx + 1) + ' // ' + step.title +\n              '</span>' +\n              '<span class=\"ud-city-badge\">' + step.time + '</span>' +\n            '</div>' +\n            '<div class=\"small fw-semibold text-white mb-1\">' + step.location + '</div>' +\n            '<p class=\"small text-secondary mb-0\">' + step.note + '</p>' +\n          '</article>';\n\n        timestampsContainer.appendChild(tsCol);\n      });\n    }\n\n    lookupForm.addEventListener('submit', function (event) {\n      event.preventDefault();\n      const order = getOrderByCode(orderInput.value);\n      renderOrder(order);\n    });\n\n    document.querySelectorAll('[data-sample-order]').forEach(function (btn) {\n      btn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n        const sample = btn.getAttribute('data-sample-order');\n        orderInput.value = sample;\n        renderOrder(getOrderByCode(sample));\n      });\n    });\n\n    // Check URL ?order= parameter or load #UD-1042 by default\n    const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);\n    const urlOrder = params.get('order');\n    const initialCode = urlOrder ? ('#' + urlOrder.replace(/^#/, '').toUpperCase()) : '#UD-1042';\n    orderInput.value = initialCode;\n    renderOrder(getOrderByCode(initialCode));\n  }\n\n  /* ========================================================================\n     INIT ON DOM READY\n     ======================================================================== */\n  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {\n    initWomensCatalog();\n    initCartCheckout();\n    initOrderTracking();\n  });\n})();\n","Overwrite":true,"Path":"c:\\Users\\laptop villa\\SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP\\js\\commerce.js"}}]}

class=\"ud-page-hero\">\n    <div class=\"container\">\n      <div class=\"row align-items-end g-4\">\n        <div class=\"col-lg-8\">\n          <span class=\"ud-eyebrow\">Bag &amp; Dispatch // flex-column flex-md-row align-items-md-center justify-content-between gap-3\">\n          <div>\n            <div class=\"ud-alert-title mb-1\">\n              Order Confirmed <span id=\"placedOrderId\" class=\"ud-mono ms-1\">#UD-0000</span>\n            </div>\n            <p id=\"placedOrderSummary\" class=\"mb-0 small text-white\">\n              Your URBANDROPS order has been placed and queued for dispatch.\n            </p>\n          </div>\n          <a id=\"trackPlacedOrderLink\" href=\"order-tracking.html\" class=\"btn ud-btn-cta btn-sm flex-shrink-0\">\n            Track This Order\n          </a>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <div class=\"row g-4\">\n        <!-- LEFT COLUMN: Dynamic Cart Items from localStorage -->\n        <section class=\"col-lg-7\" text-uppercase mb-3\">Order Summary</h2>\n\n            <!-- Promo Code Field -->\n            <div class=\"mb-3 pb-3 border-bottom border-secondary border-opacity-25\">\n              <label for=\"promoCodeInput\" class=\"ud-form-label d-block\">Promo Code</label>\n              <div class=\"input-group\">\n                <input\n                  id=\"promoCodeInput\"\n                  type=\"text\"\n                  class=\"form-control ud-form-control ud-mono\"\n                  placeholder=\"Enter URBAN10\"\n                  aria-label=\"Promo code\"\n                >\n aria-live=\"polite\"></div>\n            </div>\n\n            <!-- Breakdown: Subtotal, Shipping, Discount, Total -->\n            <div>\n              <div class=\"ud-summary-row\">\n                <span class=\"text-secondary\">Subtotal</span>\n                <span id=\"summarySubtotal\" class=\"ud-mono text-white\">PKR 0</span>\n              </div>\n              <div class=\"ud-summary-row\">\n                <span class=\"text-secondary\">Shipping</span>\n                <span id=\"summaryShipping\" class=\"ud-mono text-white\">PKR 0</span>\n id=\"summaryDiscount\" class=\"ud-mono\" style=\"color: var(--ud-accent);\">- PKR 0</span>\n              </div>\n              <div class=\"ud-summary-row ud-summary-total\">\n                <span>Total</span>\n                <span id=\"summaryTotal\" class=\"ud-mono\" style=\"color: var(--ud-accent);\">PKR 0</span>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n\n          <!-- Shipping Address Form -->\n          <section class=\"ud-card p-4\" aria-labelledby=\"shippingHeading\">\n            <h2 id=\"shippingHeading\" class=\"h5 fw-bold text-uppercase mb-3\">Shipping class=\"ud-form-label\">Full Name</label>\n                  <input id=\"shipName\" type=\"text\" class=\"form-control ud-form-control\" placeholder=\"e.g., Ayesha Khan\" required>\n class=\"ud-form-label\">Phone</label>\n                  <input id=\"shipPhone\" type=\"tel\" class=\"form-control ud-form-control\" placeholder=\"+92 300 1234567\" required>\n                </div>\n for=\"shipCity\" class=\"ud-form-label\">City</label>\n                  <select id=\"shipCity\" class=\"form-select ud-form-select\" required>\n                    <option value=\"\" selected disabled>Select city...</option>\n                    <option value=\"Islamabad\">Islamabad</option>\n                    <option value=\"Lahore\">Lahore</option>\n                    <option
